Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood(rem)
Vol.1

Chapter 3540
Putting aside the fact that Luo Zheng had changed his body shape, it was not difficult for existences at their level to change their physical appearance.
However, these two spirit mermaids and mermen were notorious in the lower reaches of the divine river, which was why no one was willing to go into the water to hunt Donghuang and Old Ying.
Anyone who went into the water and encountered these two spirit mermaids and mermen would die without a doubt, without exception!
Back then, He Meng people attempted to kill these two devil-like spirit mermaids and mermen, but after sacrificing three He Meng people, the two spirit mermaids and mermen remained unharmed. At last, He Meng people had to give up.
Fortunately, the two spirit mermaids and mermen could only stay in the divine river, as He Meng people forbade their people from going into the lower reaches of the divine river. Therefore, nothing happened to them.
However, the other non-humankind beings and Immortality stage beings in the Supreme Clear Heaven had fallen in the mouths of these two spirit mermaids and mermen over and over again.
Speaking of these two small fish, the hearts of those Immortality stage non-humankind beings would occasionally speed up.
But why were these two small fish so easily manipulated by Luo Zheng?
Donghuang's state of mind was greatly shaken!
No matter how highly he thought of Luo Zheng, he couldn't have imagined that Luo Zheng could become so powerful within such a short period of time.
Clap! Clap!
Luo Zheng threw the two spirit mermaids and mermen to the ground and said, "Behave!"
How could these two spirit mermaids and mermen possibly behave?
They were intelligent and could understand human language, but after all, they were beasts.
Once they landed on the ground, the two spirit mermaids and mermen slapped their bodies against the ground and leaped towards the divine river, releasing an amazing power from their small bodies.
Clap! Clap!
Luo Zheng wielded his hands at an extremely high speed, slapping the two small fish back to the ground like a giant paddle.
After landing on the ground again, the two spirit mermaids and mermen changed their strategy and drilled into the ground again.
The soft soil wasn't an obstacle to these spirit mermaids and mermen, but before they drilled into the ground, Luo Zheng's giant fist had already landed on one side of them.
"Boom!"
A dent was created on the ground, and the soil under the two spirit mermaids and mermen squeezed them into the ground.
"Don't even think about running. Otherwise, I'll smash you into pieces." Luo Zheng warned them in a deep voice, then pulled the two small fish out of the soil.
Looking at the two spirit mermaids and mermen, Donghuang asked, "Luo Zheng, why didn't you kill them?"
"They seem to have a special way of moving. if I can merge them, they should be a nice token of the Nirvana realm," said Luo Zheng said.
Nuwa, Fuxi, and Rat King Sheng Tian's eyes lit up. Donghuang shook his head and said, "You're right. The power that this small body can unleash is quite impressive, and its speed in the water is incredible.
Luo Zheng said confidently, "Of course, I have a way to make them submit, but how do I keep these two small fish now?"
Ying! Leave this to me, "said Winning Sky Rat King. It had been in the Blazing Maple Forest for many years, so it naturally had its ways.
While Winning Sky Rat King was dealing with the spirit mermaids and mermen, Donghuang finally asked, "How did Luo Zheng climb up to the 33 Heavens at such a speed? What about the Greater One Heavenly Palace? "
Elder Ying also showed a concerned look. When the two were trapped in the underground river, they were most worried about the Greater One Heavenly Palace and Qishan.
"The Heavenly Palace …" Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment, then said, "Donghuang and Elder Ying being trapped in the 33 Heavens was originally Ji Xuanyuan's scheme. After Elder Ying was also trapped, the Jinwu invaded our Greater One Heavenly Palace."
So it was true!
Donghuang and Elder Ying looked at each other.
From their actions before they were trapped on the Nirvana Realm, the two had basically speculated that the Jinwu would invade.
However, the two's bodies had always been in peace, which made them suspect that either the Jinwu did not invade, or the Greater One Heavenly Palace had already won.
Now that Luo Zheng mentioned this matter, the Heavenly Palace had most likely won.
Luo Zheng then reported to Donghuang about the Jinwu's invasion, the death of Di Jun and so on.
Although Luo Zheng spoke very briefly, the dangers and twists of the story still shocked Donghuang and Elder Ying.
"Later, after I entered the Immortal Painting, Ji Xuanyuan and Shennong led people to attack the Greater One Heavenly Palace. Elder Gan and Elder Ning had to give up the Center God Province and transferred the elites of Qishan to Li Mountain, but they were still intercepted halfway …
"…"
In the first half of the Battle of Li Mountain, Luo Zheng was in the same state as Donghuang and Elder Ying. They were also trapped on the Nirvana Realm. Therefore, Fuxi took over Luo Zheng's words and told Donghuang and Elder Ying about the Battle of Li Mountain.
From the black ship to the death of Tongtian, Donghuang and Elder Ying stood in the same place for a long time without regaining their senses.
If Donghuang was shocked when Luo Zheng easily took down the two spiritual mermaids, now that he heard that Luo Zheng defeated Tongtian, he was speechless.
After Elder Ying recovered from the shock, he suddenly realized a problem. "Luo Zheng, where is Shuang 'er?"
She heard Luo Zheng mention Feng Ge several times, but Lingshuang did not mention him even once …
The elites of the Greater One Heavenly Palace had all moved to Li Mountain. Lingshuang was probably facing a lot of danger!
Luo Zheng's hesitant expression deepened Elder Ying's worries. She asked, "What happened to Lingshuang? Boy, tell me quickly! "
"Ever since Elder Ying was trapped on the Nirvana Realm, Lingshuang disappeared from Taiyi Mountain. Uncle Lin and Aunt Ping searched the entire Taiyi Mountain but could not find her …" Luo Zheng answered.
"That means Shuang 'er is not in Li Mountain?" Elder Ying asked seriously.
"Yes …" Luo Zheng nodded. "She may be trying to find a way to save you."
Elder Ying glared at Luo Zheng with cold eyes. She had entrusted Ling Shuang to Luo Zheng, so Ling Shuang's safety was definitely related to Luo Zheng's. However, looking at Luo Zheng's self-reproaching expression, her bright eyes dimmed again. "Shuang 'er …
"What can she do …"
"There's a door in Lingshuang's mind, right?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
Elder Ying had already linked the mystery of Lingshuang's disappearance to that door. Suddenly hearing Luo Zheng mention that door, he was shocked. "You … know that door?"
"I've seen it once when I entered the First Heaven with Lingshuang," Luo Zheng said. "After entering the Sky Thunder Plain, I saw it for the second time."
Fuxi and Nuwa also saw the door that suddenly appeared at that time.
"What appeared in the door?" Elder Ying asked.
Luo Zheng answered first. "After the door opened, two long-haired monsters came out. These two long-haired monsters were not afraid of the thunderbolts of the Sky Thunder Plain. They wanted to drag His Majesty into the door, but they did not succeed."
Hearing Luo Zheng mention the long-haired monsters, Elder Ying's expression did not change much. When Lingshuang was still young, Elder Ying had entered her mind several times to touch that door. She had seen the long-haired monsters through that door and even communicated with them once.

Chapter 3541
The long-haired monsters were also wary of Elder Gall. They only asked Elder Gall to treat Ling Shuang well and raise her.
Other than that, they only revealed that they were a cursed race. Although they were powerful, they needed Ling Shuang's help.
As Ling Shuang grew up, Elder Gall became more disgusted with the door.
Elder Gall did not want Ling Shuang to be involved in this unknown dispute, so he had been strongly against Ling Shuang asking about the secret of the door. He even warned Ling Shuang not to open the door.
"As long as Ling Shuang doesn't open the door, they won't be able to come out. Ling Shuang must have gone in." Elder Gall was very sure of his deduction.
"But why did this door appear in the Sky Thunder Plain?" Donghuang asked.
Nuwa's beautiful eyes flickered. She said, "If Ling Shuang can really enter the door and command those long-haired monsters, what will she do?"
After Nuwa said that, Luo Zheng, Donghuang, Rat King Sheng Tian, and Fuxi all looked at Elder Gall.
Since Elder Gall was the most important person to Ling Shuang, Ling Shuang naturally had to save him.
In this way, it was easy to explain why the door appeared in the Sky Thunder Plain. After all, the Sky Thunder Plain was close to the Blazing Maple Forest. The goal of the long-haired monsters in the door was probably to save Elder Gall.
Although it was just a guess, Elder Gall already believed most of it. He just kept shaking his head and calling Ling Shuang a silly child …
Deep in the Blazing Maple Forest, two mutated people were quietly watching every move of Luo Zheng's group.
Although these mutated people were afraid of Luo Zheng's strength, they could not really escape directly. If so, they would not be able to explain it to the Elemental Spirit civilization.
Moreover, even if Luo Zheng was very strong, it did not mean that they did not have the power to fight back.
After Luo Zheng left the forest, the three people from the He Meng tribe led by Luo Meng also left quickly.
The other Nirvana Realm creatures and Immortal Realm creatures in the open space were also discussing.
"That guy is really bold. He didn't give Luo Meng any face."
"I have a bad feeling. Let's just watch the show."
"What's wrong? At most, Hei Meng will take that guy's life …"
Although Luo Zheng's strength was exaggerated, no one was optimistic about Luo Zheng because Hei Meng was almost invincible!
The land of the He Meng Clan was located to the south of the Divine River. It was the most beautiful area in Blazing Maple Forest.
After Luo Meng returned to the clan, he went straight to the village in the center of the clan.
After pushing open the gate of the stronghold, they saw a man from He Meng Clan lying in a large water tank. He was the current patriarch of He Meng Clan, Wu Meng, who was also Luo Meng's father.
There were all kinds of bones soaking in the large water tank. These bones were all fished up from the divine river, and their quality was much better than the gold and silver bones.
Melting bones with water and burning bones with fire were the two major body-refining methods of the He Meng Clan.
However, the Blaze Maple Forest could not contain flames. Flames were also a type of energy release, which would also attract attacks from the Blaze Maple Forest.
The previous Patriarch wanted to break through, so he went outside the Blazing Maple Forest to burn his bones with fire. In the end, he was burned to ashes the moment he left the Blazing Maple Forest.
"Father, there's a guy who doesn't know his place," Luo Meng said with a gloomy face.
Wu Meng leaned against the water tank and glanced at his son with his head tilted. "If it's really a guy who doesn't know his place, he would have died at your hands long ago."
Those in the Immortal Realm who wanted to survive in the Blaze Maple Forest had to be acutely aware of their own strength and avoid going against the He Meng Clan. Otherwise, it was not difficult to imagine the consequences.
Luo Zheng did not take the He Meng Clan seriously, so he must have powerful backing. Luo Meng did not make a move because he was afraid of Luo Zheng.
"Father, you already know?" Luo Meng said indignantly.
"There's no need for us to stand up for the Yuan Ling Civilization. They gave us benefits in exchange for allowing them to move freely downstream. Everything else has nothing to do with us," Wu Meng said.
Luo Meng frowned and said, "But that human brat is so arrogant. How can we tolerate him?"
"If I don't have the confidence to defeat him, will I be able to tolerate him?" Wu Meng retorted.
Luo Meng choked at his father's words.
He had never thought that his father would admit that he was weaker than Luo Zheng …
"Father, you can't defeat that brat? How is that possible! "Luo Meng shook his head vigorously.
Wu Meng said seriously, "Ku Hou's strength is extraordinary. Even if I wanted to deal with him, I would not be able to do it so cleanly. That brat … Our He Meng Clan has no need to provoke such a powerful opponent."
Luo Meng felt somewhat helpless after hearing his father's words. He was afraid that he would not be able to regain the face he had lost today.
Just as Luo Meng was about to leave, the door of the village was pushed open again. A foreign being stood outside the door. This foreign being's body was covered with scars that looked like bitter tree bark. He looked extremely ugly.
Luo Meng recognized this foreign being. His name was Xian Kan, and he had always been following Ku Hou. He should be the second-in-command of Ku Hou's group.
After entering the village, Xian Kan bowed to Wu Meng and Luo Meng. Before he could speak, Wu Meng said, "If Mu Ling wants me to deal with that human brat, you can scram now."
Unexpectedly, Xian Kan said unhurriedly, "Lord Mu Ling wants you to make a move, Lord Wu Meng."
Luo Meng glared at Xian Kan coldly. "Then why are you here?"
"Lord Mu Ling wants Lord Hei Meng to make a move," Xian Kan continued.
As soon as he said this, Wu Meng's eyes flashed. Luo Meng's figure flashed, and he grabbed Xian Kan's collar and said coldly, "Bastard, do you know what you're saying?"
The strongest person in the He Meng Tribe was not the patriarch, but someone else, the "Hei Meng" that Xian Kan spoke of.
Hei Meng had never been the patriarch of the He Meng Tribe, but he was the true legend of the He Meng Tribe. It was because of Hei Meng's existence that the He Meng Tribe's status in the Blaze Maple Forest was decided.
It was said that Hei Meng had unintentionally found a charred arm in the Divine River. At that time, Hei Meng wasn't considered strong, and was trying his luck in the Divine River like all other living beings. He didn't know the origin of this charred arm either.
He did not care about it, and just ate it.
After eating the arm, Hei Meng's strength instantly skyrocketed to an extremely terrifying level. It was also because of this that he led the He Meng Tribe to become the overlords of the Blaze Maple Forest.
"You want Lord Hei Meng to make a move, you talk big." Wu Meng waved his hand, telling Luo Meng not to act rashly. At the same time, he asked, "What are the conditions?"
Xian Kan smiled slightly and said, "Mu Ling wants the Blaze Maple Forest to expand fifty thousand kilometers to the southwest."
"Pa!"
The large water tank that Wu Meng was soaking in suddenly shattered, and water gushed out from the cracks. Luo Meng looked at Xian Kan in shock, as if he had heard an inconceivable answer.
"Is this true?" Wu Meng asked.
"Lord Mu Ling has always been a man of his word." Xian Kan nodded.
The He Meng Tribe had very few people, and their strength was average in the Supreme Pure Heaven. They were able to achieve what they had today mainly because of the Blaze Maple Forest.
The expansion of the Blaze Maple Forest meant the expansion of the He Meng Tribe's sphere of influence.
What Wu Meng cared about the most was the expansion of fifty thousand kilometers to the southwest. That was the Second Heaven of the Three Pure Heaven, which was the border of the Supreme Pure Heaven.
This was an offer that the He Meng Tribe could not refuse.

Chapter 3542
The area around Blaze Maple Forest was the territory of the He Meng Clan. Many people in Sanqing Heaven thought so.
The young people in the He Meng Clan like Luo Meng also thought so.
However, as the clan leader, Wu Meng knew very well that this was not the case.
After the previous clan leader of the He Meng Clan died, Hei Meng appointed Wu Meng as the new clan leader. He told Wu Meng that the real masters of Blaze Maple Forest were the motionless Blaze Maple Forest, not the He Meng Clan.
The He Meng Clan was just a companion species of Blaze Maple Forest. Everything the He Meng Clan had was given to them by Blaze Maple Forest.
If there was any chance to expand Blaze Maple Forest, as the clan leader, Wu Meng must seize it!
In fact, it was extremely difficult to expand Blaze Maple Forest. To the south was the Sky Thunder Plain. If Blaze Maple Forest grew there, it would only be struck to ashes by the rolling thunder. To the east was a cold land. It was not suitable for Blaze Maple Forest to grow.
The most suitable place was the southwest, which was to expand upstream of the Divine River.
Blaze Maple Forest was originally expanded this way. However, when it expanded to this extent, it was discovered by the Yuan Ling Civilization.
The Elemental Spirit civilization had poisoned the soil in the southwest, creating a poisonous land with a width of twenty miles. This poisonous land was an insurmountable natural moat for the Blaze Maple Forest. Any seed or root of the Blaze Maple Forest that drilled into the poisonous land would immediately rot.
The He Meng Clan once tried to negotiate with the Yuan Ling Civilization, but they were rejected. Since the previous clan leader was killed, the He Meng Clan also understood that the Yuan Ling Civilization would not allow Blaze Maple Forest to expand.
Now that the Yuan Ling Civilization took the initiative to mention it, Wu Meng could not refuse.
Xian Kan should have seen through Wu Meng's thoughts. He did not say anything more and left silently.
Wu Meng stood up and wiped his body. He said to Luo Meng, "I'm going to see Hei Meng."
… …
Luo Zheng, Elder Ying, and Donghuang's Soul-Avoiding Water completely dried up. After the restriction effect disappeared, they all left the other side.
Concubine Yan, Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan, and the experts of the Heavenly Palace also understood that this was a matter of life and death. They all waited at the side. Feng Ge, who was originally cultivating, also rushed over.
After waiting for so long, they did not receive any news from the other side. They were all anxious.
At this moment, Luo Zheng was the first to exit from the Nirvana Realm and immediately woke up.
"Luo Zheng, how is my father …"
Feng Ge was the first to step forward. However, before he could finish his sentence, Elder Ying coughed twice and woke up. Then, Donghuang, Fuxi, Nuwa, Rat King Sheng Tian, and the others followed suit.
"Elder Ying!"
"Your Majesty …"
"Imperial Father …"
The experts of Greater One Mountain and Great Yi Mountain all gathered around.
Since Donghuang and Elder Ying were trapped, the backbone of Great Yi Heavenly Palace was gone. Many people thought that the two of them would never have the chance to wake up.
Now that the two of them had returned safe and sound, how could the cultivators of the Heavenly Palace not feel excited?
Donghuang and Elder Ying had been soaking in the divine river for a long time and had suffered a lot. They were also very excited to see their relatives and subordinates alive.
The people from the Heavenly Palace were looking forward to Donghuang bringing them back to the Center God Province after he left the Paramita Realm.
Immediately, several old ministers of Greater One Mountain proposed this idea.
The Center Divine Province was the root of the Great Yi Heavenly Palace. Even the nearby Bright Lake, as well as the various cities at the foot of the Seven Mountains, were inextricably linked to the people of the Heavenly Palace.
Although Li Shan treated the people of the Heavenly Palace very well, there were still many people who hoped to return to the Center God Province.
After a brief consideration, Donghuang rejected this request for the time being.
Although the Path of the End had won the battle of Li Shan, this era was different from the previous era!
Whether it was the Wukong Clan or the Yuanling Civilization, they all had a way to descend into the world.
The era where a single Sempiternal Realm expert was enough to protect an entire clan was long gone.
If Donghuang brought his subordinates back to the Center God Province, not only would he not be able to protect the Great Yi Heavenly Palace, but he might also destroy the Center God Province.
Moreover, Li Shan was gathering the people of the Path of the End. Those super forces who agreed with the Path of the End were also gathering at Li Shan. Now was not the time to return.
After Donghuang and Elder Ying met with the people of the Heavenly Palace, they returned to the Paramita Realm.
Blazing Maple Forest was not a safe place. After all, there had been incidents of people being forcibly pulled to the Paramita Realm before! Luo Zheng still needed to take them out of Blazing Maple Forest.
After they returned to the Paramita Realm, they followed Luo Zheng upstream. Just as they passed by the empty space in the forest beside the Divine River, they felt a series of intense tremors coming from the ground. Clearly, there were many huge creatures approaching, and there were many of them.
"Squeak, looks like we're in trouble," said the Heaven Winning Rat King.
The Heaven Winning Rat King knew the character of the He Meng people well. Those guys would definitely not give up.
Luo Zheng's group was not the only ones who felt uneasy. The other Immortal Realm experts and the non-humans also looked uneasy. The tremors on the ground meant that the He Meng people were making a big move.
Far away, Xian Kan led a few non-humans and appeared first. These non-humans stared at Luo Zheng as if they were looking at a dead man. Their leader, Xian Kan, had a strange smile on his face.
"They found a strong ally," Luo Zheng sneered in his heart.
Dong dong dong dong …
At this time, the tremors on the ground were getting closer and closer. Then, many He Meng people covered in copper powder appeared.
The number of He Meng people was already very small. Most of them were probably here.
Clang, clang!
Wu Meng, who was covered in dark green, stood in front of Luo Zheng. He did not show any contempt for Luo Zheng. Instead, he said in a negotiating tone, "You are not a match for the Yuan Ling civilization. Obediently surrender and leave Blazing Maple Forest. You can save yourself a lot of pain and trouble.
However, I can also guarantee the lives of these people. "
As he spoke, Wu Meng pointed at the Heaven Winning Rat King, Fuxi, and the others.
Wu Meng felt that his conditions were good. If Luo Zheng knew what was good for him, he should agree.
Luo Zheng glanced at Wu Meng and smiled. "Did the Yuan Ling civilization ask you to make a move? They have said the same thing eight or ten times. "
Luo Zheng thought that the strength he had used to kill Ku Hou was enough to intimidate the He Meng people.
The He Meng people would not attack him just because he provoked them.
Then, there was only one possibility. The Yuan Ling civilization had promised the He Meng people great benefits.
"What do you think?" Wu Meng asked again.
"I have never compromised, and I don't intend to compromise." Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders.
Wu Meng looked regretful. "Then you will never have the chance to compromise. You will sleep in Blazing Maple Forest forever, and your flesh and blood will become the nourishment of Blazing Maple Forest."
"You?" Luo Zheng asked in a serious voice.
"The chief is more than enough to kill you!"
"No one will dare to collect your corpse if you die here!"
"When the chief tears you to pieces, you won't be so stubborn!"
Seeing Luo Zheng so arrogant, the He Meng people immediately clamored.
However, Wu Meng shook his head and said, "I can't beat you."
These words made the He Meng people speechless. Then Wu Meng took a step back and said, "But he should be able to."
Boom, boom, boom …
A big man about 30 meters tall stepped out of the forest. This He Meng people was completely black, but his eyes were staring at Luo Zheng like the most dangerous viper.

Chapter 3543
The clearing in the forest suddenly became very quiet. Everyone's eyes were focused on the body of this huge black creature.
"If he isn't the patriarch of the He Meng Tribe, who is he?" someone asked softly.
"I've never seen this He Meng Tribe member before …"
"His aura is much stronger than Wu Meng's!"
Hei Meng had not appeared for many years, so many newcomers in the clearing did not know him.
However, some old people knew that things were getting troublesome …
"Wu Meng really came out?"
"What are the conditions of the Yuan Ling Civilization? Invite Hei Meng out!"
"There's a show to watch!"
Hei Meng was the greatest pillar of support for the He Meng Tribe. If he personally came out, it would naturally be extraordinary. In fact, the Outsiders and Immortal Realms who had been in the Blaze Maple Forest all year round were very experienced. Given the thunderous means that Luo Zheng had used to kill Ku Hou, he was indeed qualified to contend with Wu Meng. However, some of them thought that Hei Meng was undefeatable.
This guy still did not think highly of Luo Zheng.
They did not expect Wu Meng to be so straightforward. He did not fight with Luo Zheng before directly inviting Hei Meng out.
Luo Zheng felt a little uncomfortable under Hei Meng's venomous gaze. He returned it with the same gaze and said with a faint smile, "What? You're not going to persuade me anymore? "
"I won't do such a boring thing," Hei Meng said.
His voice was very dry, like the sound of scorched mud rubbing against each other. His dry voice was also hoarse.
"I like fighting." Before Luo Zheng could respond, Hei Meng continued, "Unfortunately, I've become too strong. In the past, no one in the Blaze Maple Forest was worthy of my attention. Now, one has finally appeared. How can I give up?"
Hei Meng's entire body was black. Only his eyes were bright and full of expression, like stars embedded in the night sky.
As he spoke, his fighting spirit spread out.
Anyone who made eye contact with him would feel their blood circulate faster and heat up. The battle intent that was engraved in their bones was extremely contagious.
Even Luo Zheng felt his heart thump. An inexplicable desire arose from the bottom of his heart.
Luo Zheng turned around and said to Nuwa and the others, "Do you want to leave the Nirvana Realm or stay here?"
The safest way was naturally to leave the other side. However, Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others also understood that Luo Zheng was probably facing a life and death trial. They should not have fled because of fear.
"We will stay here with you." Nuwa made her choice without hesitation.
Rat King Sheng Tian, Fuxi, Donghuang, and Elder Ying nodded at the same time.
Their current strength and status were all obtained through a narrow escape from death. Naturally, they could face it calmly. Moreover, they were confident in Luo Zheng's strength.
Boom!
Hei Meng lightly stomped his foot, and the entire clearing in the forest shook three times.
As he abruptly exerted force, his pitch-black muscles rippled like water.
His body instantly grew fatter, and the black muscles on his shoulders bulged, almost completely covering his head. At the same time, Hei Meng also said, "Let me see your strongest physical form …"
"Strongest physical form?" Luo Zheng shook his head. "This is already the strongest."
Luo Zheng's physical body was bestowed by the Origin Divine Blood. He could not interfere with it. Luo Zheng could only answer in this way.
"Is that so?" Hei Meng laughed. "If that's the case, then you have already lost …"
As Hei Meng's voice fell, his pitch-black figure strangely moved and suddenly appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
Supernatural powers could not be used in the Blazing Maple Forest, so Hei Meng could not use greater teleportation or instant teleportation. However, he still achieved a considerable effect!
"So fast!"
Luo Zheng's pupils shrank slightly, and he quickly retreated.
Boom!
Hei Meng's huge fist smashed the ground.
The huge fist print was imprinted on the ground like a seal, and the lines between the bones of the fist were clearly displayed.
Luo Zheng had almost no time to react. He almost instinctively dodged!
The moment Luo Zheng dodged, his heart throbbed, and he frantically dodged with his instincts.
Boom, boom, boom, boom, boom …
A series of fist prints were imprinted on the road in the forest. Luo Zheng and Hei Meng turned into two shadows and fled. It was almost impossible to track them with their eyes!
Hei Meng's power was enough to overturn the soil within a radius of a hundred li.
However, the Blazing Maple Trees' developed roots covered the entire forest. These Blazing Maple Trees relieved and absorbed Hei Meng's power, maintaining the stability of the Blazing Maple Forest.
Boom!
In a breath's time, Hei Meng punched thirty to forty times, and appeared thousands of zhang away with Luo Zheng.
The two still maintained a distance of a few feet, and even their standing posture did not change much.
"Your reaction and speed are very strong." Hei Meng's eyes revealed a trace of admiration. "But if you keep dodging, the result is not difficult to guess!"
Boom, boom, boom, boom …
Hei Meng launched another series of attacks.
Luo Zheng still relied on his body's instincts to make judgments and keep retreating. However, Hei Meng chased and attacked all the way, deliberately forcing Luo Zheng into a dead end. Behind Luo Zheng, three Blazing Maple Trees appeared in a row!
"Oh no! Jump! "
Luo Zheng's legs stomped on the ground, and his body shot up like a shuttle. Although the Blazing Maple Trees were very tall, he could easily jump over them with this body's terrifying jumping ability.
However, Hei Meng intended to force Luo Zheng into a dead end. He had already expected Luo Zheng's actions.
The moment Luo Zheng's feet left the ground, Hei Meng threw a straight punch at Luo Zheng's chest.
Boom!
A huge force exploded in Luo Zheng's chest, and the dense bones in his chest shattered. His entire body was embedded in the trunks of the three Blazing Maple Trees.
Hei Meng clenched his fists, and his expression was somewhat disappointed. "If you fought me fair and square, you would probably be able to last a little longer. Unfortunately …"
"It's over …"
"This human brat's body actually did not explode after taking Hei Meng's punch head-on. He is really powerful!"
"Hei Meng's strength does not belong to the Grand Pure Heaven at all!"
Even if Hei Meng walked out of the Blazing Maple Forest now, he could still stand at the peak of the Grand Pure Heaven with his physical strength alone.
However, Hei Meng was afraid of Yuan Ling civilization and the Wu Kong Clan working together to kill him.
Fuxi, Nuwa, Rat King Sheng Tian, Donghuang and the others did not say a word. They stared nervously at Luo Zheng.
They could not interfere in this contest of physical strength. They could only place their hopes on Luo Zheng. Just as Hei Meng turned around, Luo Zheng, who was embedded in the tree trunk, moved. Luo Zheng coughed and spat out a mouthful of blood. However, this mouthful of blood seemed to have a life of its own. It bounced from the ground and returned to Luo Zheng's mouth.
In mid-air.
The others saw that Luo Zheng's blood was so strange, and they all frowned.
"Lord Hei Meng has not had his fill yet. How can I fall?" As Luo Zheng spoke, he suddenly slapped his arms on the Blazing Maple Tree trunk. He was like a falling meteor, and he used his head to crash into Hei Meng.
Boom!
Caught off guard, Hei Meng was also knocked senseless.
This was also because Hei Meng's head was indestructible. If it were anyone else, their head would have exploded into pieces. And Luo Zheng took advantage of this opportunity to attack. Countless fist shadows rained down on Hei Meng's body.

Chapter 3544
Luo Zheng's body, which had been strengthened by the Origin Divine Blood, also possessed shocking explosive power!
Every punch that landed on Hei Meng's body caused a shockwave to spread in all directions. The surrounding Blazing Maple Trees shook violently.
Boom! Boom! Boom!
Those Outsiders and Immortal Realm experts hid behind the Blazing Maple Trees to watch the battle. They were all shocked.
"This tall human's strength is already very terrifying!"
"Wu Meng's judgment is indeed accurate. This human's strength is above his."
"But no matter how strong this human is, he can't shake Hei Meng at all …"
In the long years that the He Meng Clan had controlled the Blazing Maple Forest, many Outsiders had wanted to eliminate them.
Among them, the most powerful were the Wu Kong Clan and the Yuan Ling Civilization.
Due to the special form of the Yuan Ling Civilization, they couldn't enter the Blazing Maple Forest to fight. However, as they controlled a large amount of resources in the Sanqing Heaven, they could use extraordinary methods.
The Yuan Ling Civilization had once created a powerful Dark Blood Puppet. This puppet was personally created by Mu Ling and consumed a lot of precious resources of the Yuan Ling Civilization. Its strength was indeed much stronger than the average He Meng Clan member.
However, it was torn to pieces in just one round against Hei Meng.
Since then, the Yuan Ling Civilization had never tried to attack the He Meng Clan in the Blazing Maple Forest.
The Wu Kong Clan had also tried something similar. Because the Wu Kong Clan knew how powerful Hei Meng was, they had targeted Hei Meng from the beginning.
Tongtian created his substitute and soaked it in the Golden Body Pool in the Sanqing Heaven for several years. After that, he went to the Blazing Maple Forest alone to challenge Hei Meng.
The result of the Wu Kong Clan's challenge was slightly better than the Yuan Ling Civilization's. Tongtian relied on his strange movement technique to contend with Hei Meng for several rounds.
However, even though Tongtian's substitute was extremely fast, its attack technique wasn't enough to break through Hei Meng's defense. In the end, it was still smashed by Hei Meng's punch.
Since then, no force dared to challenge Hei Meng. Hei Meng defeated his opponents in one blow. Most Outsiders and Immortal Realm experts also thought that Hei Meng didn't need a second punch to kill people. However, something unexpected happened to Luo Zheng. Not only did he take a blow from Hei Meng, he even retained his combat strength.
He counterattacked.
However, such a counterattack was futile.
Hei Meng didn't dodge too much. Although he was pushed back by Luo Zheng's fists, his legs plowing out two gullies in the ground, his expression didn't change at all. A trace of unconcealable mockery appeared in his eyes.
"Your body's toughness is not bad, but your strength is too weak." Hei Meng shook his head.
Luo Zheng realized that this level of attack had no effect on Hei Meng, so he stopped and said, "Did you hear that? You're too weak."
Luo Zheng's words confused Hei Meng. What did Luo Zheng mean by "we"?
Could it be that this kid wasn't alone?
Luo Zheng's words were naturally meant for the Origin Divine Blood … Soon, Luo Zheng felt the blood in his body surge like waves. The Origin Divine Blood also understood that it couldn't use energy in the Blazing Maple Forest, so it used a strange method to express its protest. At the same time, it transmitted a different kind of emotion to Luo Zheng.
I can still become stronger.
This kind of emotion couldn't be expressed in words, but Luo Zheng could clearly feel it.
"Since you admit that you're weak, you should also understand the fate of the weak." Hei Meng opened his arms.
"Of course I understand." Luo Zheng smiled slightly. "But you have to wait."
After saying that, Luo Zheng's body suddenly disappeared. At the same time, he chanted the Breaking Illusion Spell, which attracted the reaction of the Blazing Maple Forest. Several maple leaves lit up and flew towards him.
However, just like before, Luo Zheng had disappeared before the maple leaves exploded.
Fuxi, Nuwa, Rat King Sheng Tian, Donghuang, and Elder Ying also chanted the Breaking Illusion Spell and instantly left the other side. They chanted the Breaking Illusion Spell at the same time to retreat and attract more maple leaves.
"Boom boom boom boom …"
A part of the maple leaves exploded in front of Hei Meng, but Hei Meng didn't move at all. He was completely unafraid of these maple leaves.
"Wait?" Hei Meng muttered.
"Most likely they ran away! The monk can run away, but the temple can't run away. I'll go find a member of the Zhi race to drag them back to the other side! "Xian Kan stepped forward and said.
The Roar of Terror was a member of the Zhi race. Pulling creatures back from the Chaotic World was also a Divine Ability of the Zhi race. In addition, this Divine Ability wasn't unique to the Zhi race in the Three Clear Heavens. It wasn't difficult for the Elemental Spirit civilization to find help.
Hei Meng glanced coldly at Xian Kan. "He told me to wait for him. He should be back."
"Lord Hei Meng, these scoundrels must be hiding in the Chaotic World and don't dare to return. Before their shadows disappear, I have to …"
Xian Kan was halfway through his sentence when he felt Hei Meng's murderous gaze. He couldn't continue his sentence.
His legs were trembling, and he kept retreating backward in fear.
When he retreated to Wu Meng's side, Wu Meng sneered. "If you say another word, you won't have the chance to stand beside me anymore. Hei Meng doesn't need your guidance on how to do things."
Hei Meng just stood there without moving. The atmosphere among the many other races and Immortal Realm cultivators in the forest was a little tense. Now, no one dared to salvage the Divine River in front of Hei Meng. Everyone waited with Wu Meng.
Inside Li Mountain …
When Nuwa, Fuxi, Elder Ying, and Donghuang returned, their expressions were extremely ugly.
With the previous example, they knew very well that even if they returned to the Chaotic World, it wouldn't be safe.
The four of them simultaneously looked at Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng looked at his own body and said, "Everyone, take a step back …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's stomach suddenly swelled up like a round ball.
This huge stomach was like a pill furnace. The temperature in his stomach rapidly rose, rose, and rose …
Even Donghuang, Elder Ying, and the other Immortal Realm cultivators couldn't bear it and could only retreat. As for Nuwa, she quickly evacuated the Sacred Soul Realm cultivators and led them out of the palace.
Not long after they left, the huge palace collapsed from the high temperature.
From the collapsed palace, a dazzling small sun rose. At the center of this small sun was a black dot.
"Luo Zheng … is pregnant with a freak?" Feng Ge watched from afar.
"I don't know what's in his stomach, but it should be able to make him stronger …" Donghuang felt even more incredulous when he saw this scene.
Fuxi and Nuwa's expressions were abnormally solemn.
They had seen Luo Zheng strengthen many times. It could be said that after a few transformations, he had completely broken away from his human state and was much calmer.
However, this time, Luo Zheng had gone to such great lengths to strengthen himself. It would probably be very difficult for him to deal with Hei Meng …
"Phew …"
Floating in midair, Luo Zheng felt the Origin God Blood in his body continuously surge toward his stomach. He looked at the black dot with a puzzled expression. "This can't really be a pill, right?"
Luo Zheng also vaguely understood that Hei Meng was too powerful. If the Origin God Blood wanted to continue strengthening his body, it would have to use more complicated methods. However, using his stomach to nurture a pill in front of so many people still left Luo Zheng speechless.

Chapter 3545
The sun-like radiance lasted for an hour …
As the radiance gradually faded, Luo Zheng's round belly returned to normal.
However, the pill was still in Luo Zheng's stomach. Just as Luo Zheng was curious about what the Origin Divine Blood was going to do with the pill, the pill suddenly merged with Luo Zheng's body.
Luo Zheng immediately felt thousands of needles rapidly moving through his meridians. The extreme pain made Luo Zheng's face turn blue. He couldn't help but let out a low roar.
After the pill completely merged with Luo Zheng's body, the pain gradually disappeared. At the same time, Luo Zheng obtained a strange body!
His skin turned blue and translucent. His muscles, blood vessels, and even his bones were the same. They all turned into some kind of extremely hard crystal. His bones had the darkest color, followed by his muscles …
Luo Zheng raised his hand and examined it carefully. His dark blue fingernails were glowing. A vigorous and heavy force was moving through his limbs and bones …
"How do you feel?" Fuxi asked.
"I feel like I've opened up a new world. Not bad," Luo Zheng answered with a smile.
"Are you confident you can defeat Hei Meng?" Nuwa asked. This was the key question.
Luo Zheng shook his head. "I'm not sure."
The Origin Divine Blood bestowed Luo Zheng with powers and abilities that were not fixed. Although most of them were very strong, Luo Zheng couldn't calculate their specific strength. Naturally, he wasn't confident he could defeat Hei Meng.
However, with the Origin Divine Blood's nature, it could construct almost anything. If it wanted Luo Zheng to defeat an opponent, it would definitely bestow him with enough power.
"Then, shall we return to the Paramita now?" Donghuang asked seriously.
"Yes!"
Luo Zheng answered affirmatively. He landed in the ruins of the palace and prepared his True Meaning of Dao before returning directly to the Paramita.
Donghuang, Elder Ying, Fuxi, Nuwa, Rat King Sheng Tian, and the others were all veterans. They didn't hesitate and returned to the Paramita together with Luo Zheng.
…
In the Blazing Maple Forest.
Hei Meng was still standing in the same place like an iron tower, motionlessly looking ahead.
The Immortal Realm cultivators under the Blazing Maple Tree in the distance were whispering to each other.
"It's been almost four hours!"
"Coming back is just courting death. Those humans aren't fools."
"Waiting like this is just a waste of time …"
"Shut up!"
Luo Meng stepped forward and scolded.
Although Luo Meng also felt that it was impossible for Luo Zheng's group to come back, he still had to maintain Hei Meng's authority and not allow these people to whisper.
The other He Meng clansmen were still solemn, waiting with Hei Meng.
At this time, not far in front of Hei Meng, a huge monster appeared again.
Compared to the state he was in before he left the Nirvana Realm, Luo Zheng had undergone a huge change. The giant humanoid figure formed by the blue translucent crystal was completely different from before!
"Are you done waiting?" Hei Meng asked.
Seeing Luo Zheng's miraculous transformation, Hei Meng also felt a bit strange. However, Hei Meng remembered Luo Zheng's aura, so he instantly confirmed that he was that human brat.
"All right," Luo Zheng answered.
"Wasting my time? That's another crime. Then … die!"
As Hei Meng spoke, his huge fist was already smashing down on Luo Zheng's head.
Luo Zheng stretched out his crystal-like right hand and blocked it lightly. With a clang, the two huge forces collided.
The ground under Luo Zheng's feet sank rapidly, almost half of his body sinking into the ground, but he still managed to block Hei Meng's attack!
"He, he blocked it …"
"This is the first time someone has blocked Hei Meng's attack?"
"That human's body is so strong?"
Previously, Luo Zheng was still alive and kicking after taking Hei Meng's attack, which had already shocked all the Outsiders present.
Now, this brat had directly blocked Hei Meng's attack after coming back?
Their eyes almost popped out of their sockets …
This meant that Luo Zheng had the power to fight Hei Meng!
Hei Meng's eyes also flashed with surprise. He began to get serious, constantly punching with both hands.
"Boom!"
"Boom!"
"Boom …"
The speed of his attacks was not fast, and he maintained a stable frequency throughout.
Every time Luo Zheng took a hit, he would take a step back, and the ground behind him would constantly tremble, as if an earthquake was occurring.
What was interesting was that the Blazing Maple Trees were still standing steadily, and they did not break or collapse due to the tremors in the ground …
"Luo Zheng's body has become even stronger!" Nuwa said seriously.
"I really want to know what kind of bloodline he fused with, to be able to make him so powerful in such a short time." Donghuang said with admiration and curiosity.
If they were to face Hei Meng, they probably wouldn't be able to withstand even a tenth of his punch.
Meanwhile, Luo Zheng steadily retreated, his hands like two doors, blocking all of Hei Meng's attacks.
After blocking over thirty punches in a row, Luo Zheng retreated to the three Blazing Maple Trees. This time, Luo Zheng did not choose to run away.
Taking advantage of the moment when Hei Meng punched, Luo Zheng leapt backwards, his legs stepping on the branches of the Blazing Maple Trees, and at the same time, he charged towards Hei Meng!
Not only did he avoid Hei Meng's punch, but he also attacked Hei Meng with even more ferocious attacks!
"Boom boom boom boom boom!"
In the time it took for a spark to fly off a flint, Luo Zheng had already punched eight times.
After the eight punches, eight deep holes appeared on Hei Meng's broad chest, and eight huge fist imprints protruded from his back!
At this time, Luo Zheng and Hei Meng both showed astonishment on their faces!
Generally speaking, no matter how strong the force was, the person being attacked would be sent flying backwards, and at the same time, a considerable amount of the force would be dissipated.
But now, Hei Meng was standing motionless, which meant that he had to take the force of Luo Zheng's eight punches!
However, Hei Meng's feet did not have any footholds, and he couldn't just stand there and take it …
Was it a special ability of this body?
Luo Zheng himself was also puzzled.
"A very strong attack," Hei Meng praised after spitting out a mouthful of black blood.
Hei Meng felt that in the instant Luo Zheng punched, another force had fixed him in place, making him look like an immobile target.
Even someone as strong as Hei Meng couldn't help but suffer injuries after taking the brunt of these punches.
Luo Meng, Wu Meng, and the other members of the He Meng Tribe all looked at this scene with horrified expressions!
The invincible Hei Meng in their eyes had actually been injured, and he had even been beaten to the point of spitting out blood by a human!
There was nothing more terrifying in the world than this!
The mutated beasts and Immortal Realm cultivators all sucked in a breath of cold air.
In addition to their shock, they were also overjoyed.
If this human brat could really kill Hei Meng, it would be great news for everyone present!
"Then take a few more punches!"
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate. His body swayed slightly, and sharp fist projections flew towards Hei Meng from all directions.
"Dong dong dong dong dong dong …" The sound of these punches was extremely dull. If one looked closely, they would see that every time Luo Zheng's fist landed on Hei Meng, Hei Meng's body would tremble slightly, as if space had been displaced.

Chapter 3546
After the spatial dislocation, Hei Meng was embedded in a spatial wall.
He was unable to retreat, advance, or dodge left and right. He could only take on Luo Zheng's fist radiance!
Some of the sharp-eyed Sempiternal Realm aliens in the forest also saw some clues. The shock in their eyes intensified.
"He used a spatial ability?" one of them asked.
This kind of spatial dislocation seemed to be created by some kind of spatial god art.
However, spatial abilities belonged to the category of energy. That was clearly not a spatial ability. Otherwise, the maple leaves in the Blazing Maple Forest would have already danced and attacked Luo Zheng.
One had to know that these maple leaves had an extremely magical characteristic. They would follow their target to the ends of the earth at a constant speed.
Some people used a divine ability at the edge of the Blazing Maple Forest and escaped as fast as they could after triggering the attack of the maple leaves. After about ten breaths of time, the maple leaves would catch up to the target and explode.
There were also people who used a spatial shift at the edge of the Blazing Maple Forest to move to a further area. However, the maple leaves would also arrive after ten breaths of time!
As far as they knew, the only way to avoid the attack of the maple leaves was to leave the other side.
Those maple leaves probably wouldn't be able to chase them to the other side …
"It shouldn't be a spatial ability." Another alien shook his head and said, "It's some kind of unique energy wave. It can move the characteristics of the energy wave to an unimaginable extreme!"
"When did such an alien appear in the human race!" Another alien said with a face full of shock.
At this time, a Sempiternal Realm expert from the Three Generations Second Level Pingyi Heaven said, "That should be Luo Zheng. He was once the target of the Wu Kong Race and Yuan Ling Civilization at all costs. However, he rejected them."
Luo Zheng was considered famous in the Pingyi Heaven. No matter which faction it was, they all knew that this kid's potential was terrifying.
After hearing about his background, everyone's eyes were filled with envy.
One had to know that in the Three Clear Heavens, these two factions were the strongest. With the protection of these two factions, many things would become much easier.
"Bang, bang, bang, bang, bang …"
Under Luo Zheng's continuous attacks, Hei Meng had almost no room to retaliate.
The surface of his pitch-black body was filled with washbasin-sized holes. The fist imprints on his back were even more prominent.
In the blink of an eye, Hei Meng's body was smashed full of holes. His arms and legs were twisted, unable to support his body.
However, Luo Zheng's continuous "space walls" firmly fixed Hei Meng in place. Only when Luo Zheng withdrew his fist did Hei Meng fall to the ground with a loud bang …
Hei Meng's enormous, twisted body was like a puddle of mud on the ground. It was a tragic sight.
Blazing Maple Forest was extremely quiet …
Wu Meng and the other He Meng clansmen all looked at Hei Meng with confused eyes.
They couldn't accept it, and they couldn't believe that it was true.
Hei Meng was defeated just like that?
Fuxi, Nuwa, Elder Ying, Donghuang, and the others heaved a sigh of relief.
In the end, he still won …
Even Hei Meng had fallen under Luo Zheng's fist. What level had Luo Zheng's strength reached now?
On the other hand, the Immortal Realm foreign clansmen were overjoyed. After Luo Zheng defeated Hei Meng, he would become the new ruler of Blazing Maple Forest. However, no matter who became the ruler of Blazing Maple Forest, their situation wouldn't be worse than the He Meng clansmen's!
They had all been oppressed to the limit by the He Meng clansmen, and they hated them to the core.
The silence lasted for several breaths …
Hei Meng, who was lying on the ground, suddenly moved.
A red circular imprint about an inch wide lit up in Hei Meng's right palm. A red band of light extended from the red circular imprint and extended along his arm to his shoulder. Then, it extended to his chest, neck, spine, back, legs …
The red band of light seemed to be outlining Hei Meng's skeleton.
"Buzz!"
A miraculous scene appeared.
As if someone was blowing air into Hei Meng's body, the originally deflated and sunken parts of his body quickly swelled up. The torn wounds were healing nonstop, and his twisted arms were twisting in the direction of the red band of light.
"Crack crack crack …"
With the sound of bones cracking, Hei Meng's body quickly recovered.
Wu Meng, who was standing behind Hei Meng, saw that Hei Meng had recovered completely. His face was brimming with excitement. He suddenly raised his hand and shouted, "Meng!"
The other He Meng clansmen also raised their hands and shouted, "Meng!"
"Meng! Meng! Meng … "
The vigorous voices of the He Meng clansmen echoed in the Blaze Maple Forest. Even the Blaze Maple Forest seemed to be responding to them, and the leaves hanging on the maple trees swayed in unison.
The alien clansmen were shocked by the uniform roars, and none of them could say a word.
Hei Meng slowly climbed to his feet amidst the roars of the He Meng clansmen. A trace of depression flashed in his calm eyes. At the same time, he took a deep breath and said, "It's a pity that I let you see my strongest form …"
The He Meng clansmen were a clan with strong physical bodies. However, according to their innate talent, the stronger they were, the darker their skin color would be.
Ordinary He Meng clansmen's skin shone with a golden color. When they cultivated to a certain level, their bodies would become bronze or even dark bronze.
If they cultivated their bodies to the extreme, their bodies would be as dark bronze as Wu Meng's. That would be the limit.
However, Wu Meng had always regarded Hei Meng as his goal. He hoped to surpass this limit and cultivate to Hei Meng's level. Unfortunately, even after exhausting all his means, Wu Meng still couldn't make a breakthrough …
Hei Meng was very clear that the source of his strength was that burnt arm.
After he swallowed the arm, his physical body underwent a shocking change. The arm he swallowed easily helped him break through his limits and reach a new level!
Hei Meng was also very curious about the owner of this arm …
Since it had such a miraculous effect, the owner of this arm must be very powerful. Who could cut off the arm of such a powerful being …
The confusion in his heart must have come from the upper reaches of the Divine River. It must have come from the Clear Jade Sky, the deepest part of the Three Pure Sky!
Like the other alien clansmen in the 33 Heavens, Hei Meng regarded entering the Clear Jade Sky as his ultimate goal. Therefore, he had always kept his true strength, hoping to use it at a more critical moment. He never expected that today, he would be forced out.
A human was forced out.
"Whoosh!"
After Hei Meng finished speaking, he suddenly stretched out his hand and grabbed one of Luo Zheng's arms. With a flip, he pressed Luo Zheng to the ground. Then, all of the red ribbons of light on his body gathered towards his right arm.
"Boom!"
A fist smashed down.
The surrounding ground rippled like water. Even the dense and long roots of the Blazing Maple Forest were unable to calm the surging energy.
The alien clansmen who were close to the Blazing Maple Trees were still fine, but those who were standing in the open space directly fell to the ground. As the earth rippled outwards, they couldn't even crawl back up.
"Boom!"
A second fist smashed down.
A massive crater had already swallowed Hei Meng and Luo Zheng. Even more earth ripples began to spread.
"Boom!"
A third fist smashed down!
The earth rippled violently, toppling the nearest Blazing Maple Trees. Even the Hei Meng clansmen were sent flying by the earth ripples.

Chapter 3547
The earth rippled for a while before stopping. Then, everyone saw a body being thrown out of the deep pit. It was Luo Zheng.
Countless cracks had already appeared on Luo Zheng's azure transparent crystal body. Cracks of various sizes extended inwards. Even his dark blue bones were cracked. His powerful body couldn't withstand Hei Meng's three punches!
Those Outsiders who were originally a little active were all silent out of fear after feeling the power of these three punches.
Originally, there were spies of the Wu Kong Clan and Yuan Ling Civilization mixed in with these outsiders. These spies had all planned on reporting the news that Blaze Maple Forest might have "changed owners." The sudden change of ownership made them even more terrified and uneasy!
Although Hei Meng was defined as almost invincible, his true strength was still far beyond their expectations!
It was too terrifying!
"Hahaha …" Hei Meng stepped out of the deep pit and made a gesture to Wu Meng.
Wu Meng understood tacitly. He quickly took out a basin of Soul Avoiding Water from the Divine River and poured it on Luo Zheng's body. This would prevent Luo Zheng from escaping again after waking up.
Then, Hei Meng stepped forward and looked down at Luo Zheng's cracked body. "No one can challenge my physical body. You are an anomaly in the Three Pure Heaven. You have indeed surprised me, but you are still not qualified!"
As he spoke, Hei Meng stepped on Luo Zheng's leg. As Hei Meng continued to exert force, the cracks on Luo Zheng's leg continued to increase.
"Crack, crack, crack …"
As more and more cracks appeared, Luo Zheng's leg suddenly exploded. It turned into thousands of pieces and scattered all over the ground.
Nuwa, Fuxi, and the others' eyes twitched.
Luo Zheng's body had been strengthened twice, but he was still defeated …
His journey in the Three Pure Heaven came to an abrupt end today?
"Alas, how can my old life be worth Luo Zheng's saving? Luo Zheng's life is much more important than my Heavenly Palace. I'm so foolish, so foolish!" Donghuang suddenly blamed himself with regret.
Although Elder Ying was silent, he probably had the same thought.
Now it seemed that Luo Zheng was the real hope and the only one who could complete the Path of the End. However, this person had sacrificed his life to save them. It was not worth it.
Nuwa didn't say anything. She was still looking at Luo Zheng with a dignified look.
She believed that Luo Zheng would not give up until the very last moment.
"Crack!"
Hei Meng then stomped on Luo Zheng's right leg until it exploded, shooting out countless blue crystal fragments.
Then, he lifted his leg again and was about to crush Luo Zheng's body.
Just as Hei Meng's foot was about to land on Luo Zheng's abdomen, Luo Zheng suddenly opened his eyes, revealing two demonic red dots in his eyes.
When Hei Meng made contact with that demonic red gaze, his heart couldn't help but tremble. A sense of crisis rose from the depths of his heart. Hei Meng almost instinctively wanted to strangle Luo Zheng. This foot of his was going to crush Luo Zheng's body!
But the moment he stepped down, Luo Zheng's body rippled with a burst of spatial fluctuations, and quickly disappeared under Hei Meng's feet. Hei Meng's foot naturally missed.
Buzz, buzz, buzz …
The moment the spatial fluctuations spread out, circles of maple leaves in the Blazing Maple Forest lit up. Like butterflies migrating on a large scale, they swarmed in one direction.
"Ten breaths of time." Hei Meng shook his head with a smile.
Many people of the He Meng Clan, aliens, and Immortal Realm sighed when they saw those maple leaves.
No matter how far or how fast they ran, no matter how hard they hid, these maple leaves would always arrive on time and take away the life of those who violated the rules. The only exception was to escape from the other side. However, Wu Meng had just splashed the water of the Divine River.
This way was blocked.
Therefore, in everyone's eyes, Luo Zheng only had ten breaths of time left to live. They would see how Luo Zheng struggled in the last ten breaths of time.
The people of the He Meng Clan loved to enjoy the last ten breaths of time. In the last ten breaths of time of life, both aliens and Immortal Realm experts would try their best to struggle, but the final result was always the same.
There was a time when the people of the He Meng Clan madly forced the aliens and Immortal Realm experts to activate their energy just to enjoy the last ten breaths of time.
"Buzz!"
After Luo Zheng disappeared, he immediately created a space tunnel. The maple leaves along the tunnel quickly glowed and left the Blazing Maple Tree, flying into the sky!
"That brat actually attracted so many maple leaves!"
"A little more or a little less doesn't make much of a difference. He can't escape death …"
"There are so many maple leaves. It's enough for him to die ten times over!"
The maple leaves in the Blazing Maple Forest had several magical properties. One of them was that they could catch up to their target in ten breaths of time no matter how far they were. Another special property was that the damage caused by the explosion of the maple leaves could not be blocked.
No matter how powerful the barrier was or how ingenious the space was, it could not block the damage caused by the explosion of the maple leaves.
After Luo Zheng came out of the space tunnel, a green light surged on the surface of his body, and his broken legs quickly recovered. Then, he looked back at the maple leaves and immediately went back into the space tunnel.
He drew an invisible path in the Blazing Maple Forest, attracting all the maple leaves below him …
"Six breaths of time left …"
"This brat runs fast. He made a big circle in such a short time!"
"Five breaths!"
"Four breaths!"
"Three breaths!"
"Two breaths!"
"One breath … Bang!"
Next to him, Luo Meng made an expression of shock.
According to the way everyone calculated the time, those maple leaves would undoubtedly explode.
With so many maple leaves, no matter where Luo Zheng escaped to, the sound of the explosion would be transmitted quickly. However, the explosion that everyone expected to hear did not appear.
On the contrary, everyone saw Luo Zheng floating in the air. This time, he did not even go back into the space tunnel. Thus, he attracted even more maple leaves!
Large patches of maple leaves were still chasing after Luo Zheng. They still maintained a distance, neither closing in nor shaking off Luo Zheng …
When Hei Meng saw this scene, his pupils contracted. He almost gritted his teeth and said, "How is this possible!"
Then, Hei Meng slightly tilted his head. A trace of energy fluctuation appeared on his shoulder. Although this energy fluctuation was very faint, a maple leaf hanging on a nearby Blazing Maple Tree still sensed it. After the maple leaf lit up, it floated over.
Hei Meng's legs fiercely kicked, and he quickly pulled away from the maple leaf. His entire body was like a ghost as he spun in circles.
After ten breaths of time, the maple leaf accurately closed in on Hei Meng and exploded on his shoulder.
"Pa!"
Hei Meng's physical defense was astonishingly strong. However, when the maple leaf exploded, a crack appeared on the surface of his body!
"There's nothing wrong with these maple leaves." Hei Meng looked up in confusion. "Does that mean there's something wrong with that brat?"
Luo Zheng was still dragging the maple leaves in circles. The maple leaves were still unable to close in on Luo Zheng!
"What the hell? Are these maple leaves stupid?"
"Nothing like this has ever happened in all of history!"
"How did he do it?"
The outsiders and Immortal realm cultivators were all dumbfounded.

Chapter 3548
Originally, they were all counting down Luo Zheng's life, but when the time was up, Luo Zheng was still alive and kicking …
Fuxi, Donghuang, and the others, who were originally worried, were now slightly relieved.
"This kid, haha …" Fuxi was somewhat speechless.
Donghuang also shook his head. "The danger is not over yet."
Rat King Sheng squeaked. "It's impossible that the maple leaves can't catch up with him. What method did Luo Zheng use?"
Nuwa stared at Luo Zheng's movements. "The maple leaves in the Blazing Maple Forest do not move according to normal rules. The way these maple leaves pass through space is different from what we imagined, and Luo Zheng should be moving in exactly the same way as the maple leaves."
"Then Luo Zheng's movement is different from ours? Why can't I see it? "Fuxi asked.
"As creatures in the Chaos, you and I are born with limitations. Naturally, we can't see through it," Nuwa replied. "Some kind of change should have occurred in Luo Zheng's body, and this change has a lot to do with his bloodline …"
Luo Zheng didn't completely explain the origin divine blood, and Nuwa didn't ask in detail.
But from Luo Zheng's performance after returning from the Immortal Realm, the strength of his bloodline should have reached a level that ordinary people couldn't understand.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
Luo Zheng suddenly sped up while floating in the air, circled in the air several times, and then landed on the open space in the forest, and walked toward Hei Meng as if nothing had happened.
The red light in his eyes was still full of wildness and killing intent, and even his aura changed greatly. But when he spoke again, it was still Luo Zheng's voice, but it was a little shrill and hurried. "This is your strongest form? Then can you block all of them?! "
Luo Zheng said as he walked toward Hei Meng.
He didn't walk fast, and looked like an old man walking at a leisurely pace.
When Hei Meng heard Luo Zheng's words, he immediately understood why Luo Zheng attracted those maple leaves!
He retreated into the Blazing Maple Forest at a speed that couldn't be seen by the naked eye, as if he had been bitten by a poisonous snake. But just as he retreated a distance, he heard Luo Zheng's laughter. "Haha, running away? I'll give you ten breaths … "
"Ten!"
"Nine!"
"Eight …"
Everyone saw Luo Zheng walking slowly like an old man taking a stroll.
Hei Meng, on the other hand, used a speed that the naked eye could not capture to fly through the forest.
Even so, the distance between the two of them was still rapidly closing!
"Ten breaths … That kid has grasped the circulation pattern of the maple leaf?"
"If that's the case, it's too terrifying!"
"Hei Meng won't be able to dodge it no matter what!"
Everyone had originally seen Luo Zheng as a challenger, but now the situation was completely beyond their imagination. Luo Zheng obviously had some unimaginable ability.
"Five!"
"Four!"
"Three!"
"Two …"
Previously, when everyone saw the maple leaves fluttering about, they didn't feel anything. They only felt that the maple leaves were sometimes fast and sometimes slow.
Now that Luo Zheng was walking, they could see it clearly.
His steps didn't change, but his stride had changed.
In front of Luo Zheng, the space that was originally one foot had become one inch, and the distance of ten feet had become one foot.
At the last second, any distance in this world was only a step away for Luo Zheng.
With one step, he would be everywhere in the world.
Even though Hei Meng's speed was astonishing, he was still caught by Luo Zheng.
After Luo Zheng caught up to Hei Meng, he only lightly patted him on the shoulder, then said to Hei Meng, "You still have … ten breaths of life."
Then, Luo Zheng turned around and walked towards Fuxi and Nuwa.
The maple leaves fluttering in the sky originally circled around Luo Zheng's path, but at this time, they changed direction and madly rushed towards Hei Meng like a swarm of bees.
The first ten breaths of time was the time Luo Zheng approached him …
Now, these ten breaths of time were the time the maple leaves approached …
Previously, Hei Meng thought that he could escape, but when he saw the maple leaves coming towards him, he stopped running.
"Ten breaths of life, never fail," Hei Meng muttered.
He had once said that if he offended the Blazing Maple Forest and attracted the maple leaves to attack him, he definitely wouldn't run away in vain, nor would he put on a final struggle for others.
"Six …"
"Five!"
"Four!"
"Three!"
"Two!"
"One!"
Luo Zheng was originally quite a distance away from Fuxi, but he still took ten breaths of time to leisurely walk over.
After counting, he arrived at Fuxi and Nuwa's side and turned his head to look at them.
The maple leaves also touched Hei Meng …
One by one, the maple leaves began to explode.
Hei Meng's physical body was tyrannical to a peak state.
A single maple leaf could explode a person into a bloody mess, but it could only cause an extremely small wound to Hei Meng.
However, there were too many maple leaves!
Luo Zheng had just created a spatial passage and was running all over the place, attracting almost a third of the maple leaves in the Blazing Maple Forest.
Previously, no one had been able to enrage the Blazing Maple Forest to this extent, because from the time the first maple leaf was attracted, only ten breaths of time would pass. However, Luo Zheng ignored this time.
As long as he wanted, he could attract all the maple leaves in the entire Blazing Maple Forest.
"Boom boom boom boom boom …"
Soon, the light from the exploding maple leaves completely enveloped Hei Meng.
The sound of explosions constantly reverberated through the forest.
After a full ten breaths of time, the explosions suddenly stopped!
After the light disappeared, everyone looked at the place where Hei Meng had been. There was nothing left there except for the massive crater created by the explosion of the maple leaves!
Hei Meng's physical body had been completely obliterated by the explosion of the maple leaves …
This was a common occurrence in the Blazing Maple Forest. However, many people were still very shocked. Hei Meng's physical body was so terrifying that it could withstand the explosion of the maple leaves for so long.
After killing Hei Meng, there was still a large portion of the maple leaves left. These maple leaves would not return to the Blazing Maple Tree. Instead, they flashed with a dazzling light before exploding in unison!
The Blazing Maple Forest still maintained its silence.
The He Meng tribesmen stared blankly at the place where Hei Meng had disappeared. They were still unwilling to believe that Hei Meng had died.
As the tribe leader, Wu Meng's face was ashen.
Although the other He Meng tribesmen were powerful, they were still able to stand firm in the Blazing Maple Forest because of Hei Meng.
Now that Hei Meng was dead, the greatest taboo of those large factions was gone.
If the Wu Kong Race and Yuan Ling Civilization wanted to defeat Wu Meng in the Blazing Maple Forest, or even kill him, it would not be too difficult. This meant that the status of the He Meng Tribe was in danger, and all the deeds of the He Meng Tribe in these years would be dealt with and avenged …
A heart-piercing coldness rose from Wu Meng's back as though he had been stabbed by a dagger. He stood stiffly at his spot.
"Luo Zheng, how did you do it, this … … that … …" Fuxi gestured, not knowing how to ask.
Nuwa stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "Is it the ability bestowed upon you by your bloodline?"
Luo Zheng nodded. "I can sense another path. That path is very unique. It's another kind of space, but it's different from the spatial divine ability we understand." As he spoke, he stretched out his hand toward a blazing maple tree in the distance.

Chapter 3549
After the maple leaves in the Blaze Maple Forest fell off, new spores would quickly sprout on the Blaze Maple Trees. Then, new maple leaves would grow at a speed visible to the naked eye.
The maple leaves on the Blaze Maple Tree were rapidly growing. Luo Zheng reached out and gently plucked the maple leaves, holding them in his hand.
He didn't extend his arm, nor did he construct a spatial tunnel, but he was able to pluck the maple leaves just like that.
"You can pluck anything?" Nuwa's eyes lit up slightly.
If Luo Zheng could always use this ability without anyone noticing, it would be extremely powerful. After all, it was impossible to guard against.
"No." Luo Zheng shook his head. "I can only pluck the maple leaves." After Luo Zheng suffered three terrifying punches from Hei Meng, he was basically unconscious. Then, Luo Zheng's physical body suddenly used a spatial divine ability to avoid Hei Meng, relying on the instincts of the Origin Divine Blood. After the Origin Divine Blood used its divine ability, it also gave Luo Zheng a special ability.
Luo Zheng had this miraculous ability.
When he woke up, he was shocked to see so many maple leaves chasing after him. However, in his field of vision, the maple leaves were flying very slowly, and they were moving according to a fixed path. Luo Zheng was also on this path …
Now that he could see through these paths, everything became simple.
Others might find it inconceivable, but Luo Zheng could easily understand.
When someone activated their energy in the Blaze Maple Forest, all the maple leaves around them would form a path with that person. No matter how far the target ran, the maple leaves only needed to move according to the path to hit the target.
More interestingly, Luo Zheng discovered that he could change these paths!
Thus, after Luo Zheng circled around, he made the path behind him into a closed circle. The maple leaves could only rotate in the circle. As long as he didn't break this circle, the maple leaves would rotate until the end of time.
When he approached Hei Meng again, he lightly patted Hei Meng's shoulder and guided the maple leaves to Hei Meng.
Actually, after Luo Zheng saw through these paths, he was no longer afraid of the Blaze Maple Forest. He could use his divine ability to kill Hei Meng, but guiding the maple leaves was clearly the simplest method …
After the foreign races and Immortal Realm experts in the Blaze Maple Forest digested the shock in their hearts, they finally regained their composure.
Three short Yaru Tribesmen took the lead and stepped forward. The leader of the Yaru Tribesmen raised a complete gold and silver skeleton above his head and said, "This complete gold and silver skeleton is extremely rare, please kindly accept it, Sir!"
When Wu Meng, Luo Meng, and the rest of the Meng Clan's people saw this, their faces darkened. Normally, when a complete set of gold and silver bones was salvaged, the non-humankind would not be allowed to keep it for themselves. The He Meng people would definitely want a share of the loot. Under normal circumstances, complete sets of gold and silver bones were extremely rare, and the He Meng people would keep them for themselves.
Then, he returned the other pieces of gold and silver bones to the other party.
These Yaru Tribesmen had actually hidden away the gold and silver bones.
It was common for outsiders and Immortal Realm experts to hide items from the Divine River. If that was all, the He Meng people would not be so angry.
But now, the Yaru Tribesmen were offering the golden and silver bones to Luo Zheng in front of the He Meng Tribesmen …
Hei Meng had just died, and they were already disregarding the people of He Meng Clan?
"Ha Lei, what do you mean? We are the true masters of Blazing Maple Forest!"
Luo Meng was still young and impetuous, so he could not help but scold the Yaru Tribesmen.
Who knew that the Yaru Tribesman called Ha Lei would not buy it. He turned his head and looked at Luo Meng with contempt, "Hei Meng is dead, and you want to be the masters of Blazing Maple Forest? What a joke! "
Although these Yaru Tribesmen were short in stature, they were not ordinary people.
Just like the He Meng Clan, they were born with powerful bodies. But because the He Meng Clan had the advantage and Hei Meng's existence, the number of Yaru Tribesmen entering the forest was limited.
Even if the Yaru Tribesmen outnumbered the He Meng Clan by a few hundred times, Hei Meng alone was enough to wipe them out.
The Yaru Tribesmen were afraid of Hei Meng, so they could only swallow their anger.
But now, this human had killed Hei Meng!
Hei Meng was dead!
The Yaru Tribesmen believed that their chance had come!
The human race was a rare race, and they were all made up of Immortal Realm experts. Over the long years, the human race had been glorious, but once they disappeared for a long time, only a few of them appeared in the Grand Pure Heaven recently.
A few humans were definitely unable to manage the huge Blaze Maple Forest, so the Yaru Tribesmen were very happy to do this for Luo Zheng.
"Is this thing useful?" Luo Zheng asked.
He was asking about the origin divine blood in his body, but the origin divine blood did not respond to Luo Zheng. Obviously, it was not interested in this.
"Of course this thing is useful! It's a sacred object for body cultivators, especially a complete gold and silver skeleton, which is rarely seen in ten thousand years … "Donghuang suddenly said with an excited expression.
But when he suddenly saw everyone staring at him, Donghuang's old face turned red for the first time.
He was Donghuang, the master of the Heavenly Palace, and the backer of the human race in the Center God Province …
In front of a treasure, especially in front of the junior Luo Zheng, he indeed lost his composure, but this was not his fault.
Although Donghuang had fished up a lot of good things in the divine river these years, the so-called "good" here was ultimately limited. From time to time, some unimaginable things would float down from the upper reaches of the divine river, but there were many people who coveted them.
Donghuang could bring back the Nirvana Realm keepsake of the Saint Soul Realm of the Heavenly Palace from the 32 Layers of Heaven, which was basically trash that no one wanted.
Once, Donghuang fished up a gold and silver skeleton, but this gold and silver skeleton was only the size of a fingernail. Even so, Donghuang was excited for a long time. After all, after reaching the Immortal Realm, there were many treasures that could strengthen the body.
Limited.
Now that he saw a complete gold and silver skeleton, he could not suppress the excitement in his heart.
"Since Senior Donghuang is definitely a good thing, please accept it," Luo Zheng said.
He might not need it, but it was extremely important to the Immortal Realm experts like Donghuang, Fuxi, Nuwa, and the Heaven Winning Rat King.
The Yaru Tribesmen's action not only caused the He Meng Tribesmen's dissatisfaction, but also reminded the other races that relied on the divine river to make a living!
Now, Luo Zheng's importance was self-evident. If they could have a good relationship with Luo Zheng, wouldn't it be …
But just as those alien races were about to line up to "give" Luo Zheng a "gift", all the maple trees in the Blaze Maple Forest fluttered.
"Hua … …"
As if a wind was blowing through the Blaze Maple Forest, all the maple leaves began to clamor at this moment. All the Blaze Maple Trees came to life at this time, using a unique method to communicate with each other.
"Gudong, gudong!"
Suddenly, waves appeared in the divine river.
Countless thick tree roots suddenly emerged from the river. These were the roots of the Blaze Maple Trees!
The roots of the Blaze Maple Trees were very developed and almost spread all over the ground. It was not strange for them to emerge from the river.
But what was strange was that these tree roots wrapped around two charred limbs and sent them to the shore. One of the two charred limbs was sent to Wu Meng, and the other was sent to the Yaru Tribesmen.

Chapter 3550
Of the two charred black limbs, one was a leg, and the other was an arm.
When the two black limbs floated to the surface of the divine river, the breaths of the non-humans and Immortal Realm cultivators present became ragged.
It was no secret that Hei Meng had devoured an unknown charred limb in the Blaze Maple Forest. Many non-humans believed that this was the reason why Hei Meng was so powerful.
When these non-humans roamed the divine river in search of all kinds of strange treasures, they would naturally keep an eye out, hoping that they would be lucky enough to obtain the charred limb one day.
What was disappointing was that no one had ever obtained a second charred limb in all these years …
Who would have thought that the Blaze Maple Forest was actually hiding two black limbs!
