The roots of the Blaze Maple Forest were extremely developed, and both sides of the divine river were filled with Blaze Maple Trees. Anything that happened in the divine river naturally couldn't escape the Blaze Maple Forest's control. It wasn't difficult for them to fish something out of the divine river.
"After devouring that black limb, one's physical strength will increase explosively …"
"It can make me even stronger than Hei Meng!"
"This is a holy artifact, a holy artifact of our race!"
The crowd went wild.
Obtaining a black limb would increase one's strength to an unimaginable level. To the Nirvana Realm beings, this was an astonishing temptation.
Perhaps the Blaze Maple Forest had sensed the non-humans' restlessness, but branches covered in maple leaves surrounded the He Meng and Yaru Tribesmen. At the same time, all of the maple leaves shone with a red light.
The warning was very strong!
A few non-humans saw the red maple leaves and instinctively stopped in their tracks.
They hadn't forgotten that this was the Blaze Maple Forest and the consequences of being attacked by the maple leaves.
However, most of the non-humans felt that as long as they didn't use any energy, the Blaze Maple Forest wouldn't attack them. If they made the right bet and obtained the black limb, the benefits would be self-evident!
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
The non-humans swarmed toward the He Meng and Yaru Tribesmen.
"You're courting death!"
"You dare to steal from us?"
The He Meng and Yaru Tribesmen's expressions darkened.
These two races were the overlords of the Blaze Maple Forest, but they still felt pressured when faced with so many non-humans attacking them.
Just as the strange races at the front approached, the red maple leaves fell from the branches.
Previously, these maple leaves would approach the target with a light flutter, but the red maple leaves were completely different. They flew towards the target like sharp arrows.
"The route … is very short," Luo Zheng said.
The length of the path, which was invisible to ordinary people, determined the time it took for the maple leaf to reach the target.
In this extremely short path, the maple leaves naturally exploded quickly.
"Boom boom boom boom …"
The strange races at the front were blasted into smithereens by the maple leaves.
Most of the remaining Outsiders were finally jolted awake by reality when they saw how miserable the situation was.
If they rushed over like this, other than courting death, there was no other possibility. After thinking it through, they retreated one after another. On the other hand, the He Meng clansmen and Yaru clansmen were nervous for nothing.
After seeing this scene, Nuwa seemed to understand something. "It seems that the Blaze Maple Forest is the true master of this place. The He Meng clansmen don't have the ability to control the Blaze Maple Forest. Hei Meng is just a hired thug."
When Donghuang and Heaven Winning Rat King heard this conclusion, their faces revealed astonishment. But after thinking about it, Nuwa wasn't wrong. The black limb that Hei Meng had eaten back then was probably "unintentionally" bestowed to him by the Blaze Maple Forest.
"So the Blaze Maple Forest chose both the He Meng and Yaru clansmen?" Fuxi asked.
"That should be the case," Nuwa smiled. "Luo Zheng easily killed Hei Meng, which may bring a lot of pressure to the Blaze Maple Forest, so the Blaze Maple Forest will expand their team."
"They expanded their team just to deal with me?" Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes slightly. "If I snatch the black limb, what will the Blaze Maple Forest do?"
As soon as Luo Zheng said this, three Blaze Maple Forest trees not far away suddenly bent down towards Luo Zheng and stretched out their branches. The maple leaves on the branches flickered with a green light. The Blaze Maple Forest was actually showing goodwill to Luo Zheng!
The Blaze Maple Forest was afraid that Luo Zheng would really try to snatch it!
In the entire Blaze Maple Forest, the only one they couldn't do anything to was Luo Zheng.
"I was just saying," Luo Zheng shrugged.
He naturally couldn't stay in the Blaze Maple Forest for a long time, not to mention that Luo Zheng wasn't interested in the Blaze Maple Forest or the Divine River.
"However, this black limb can strengthen the body considerably. Although I can't use it, but …" Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes again.
Nuwa, Fuxi, Elder Ying, Donghuang, and Victorious Sky Rat King were all shaken when they heard Luo Zheng's words. If their bodies could be strengthened to Hei Meng's level, their strength would also be greatly enhanced.
In the distance, the members of the He Meng Tribe and the Yaru Tribe were also paying attention to the situation here. At this moment, their faces were deathly pale.
They naturally wouldn't be afraid if other people came to snatch it, but if Luo Zheng came to snatch it, they couldn't escape or resist. They had no other choice but to be bound.
However, the black limb was related to the fate of their tribe. How could they be willing to let others snatch it away?
However, before Luo Zheng could finish speaking, the Divine River beside them began to churn violently.
Countless thick and thin meridians lifted up the objects at the bottom of the Divine River. The gold and silver bones flickered with two kinds of light, the azure Crystal of Physique, and the resplendent body membrane …
Even Luo Zheng could tell at a glance that this pile of objects was very valuable. Although it couldn't be compared to the charred black limb, it was still a huge fortune for the strange races of the Supreme Pure Heaven.
Nuwa, Fuxi, Donghuang, and the others were also moved …
"This wealth, squeak … It's a lot, a lot …" The Winning Sky Rat King was the most calculative. With just a glance, it could roughly determine the value of this pile of objects.
Wu Meng's expression didn't relax at all. He knew that although this pile of objects was a lot, their combined value wasn't as high as the black limb. What if Luo Zheng didn't agree?
Perhaps the Blazing Maple Forest himself was aware of this. One of the maple trees suddenly stretched out a branch and began to write on the ground. "The Immortal Realm can't contain the charred corpse. Swallowing it will result in death. If you don't believe me, you can try."
This line of words was like a basin of cold water that extinguished the fantasies of Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others.
There was a clear difference between the Immortal Realm and the strange races. The strange races were the creatures of the Nirvana Realm born in the Three Pure Heaven, while the Immortal Realm was the people who came up through the painting scroll.
Luo Zheng didn't express his opinion. Instead, he asked, "Then what's the origin of this charred corpse?"
Swallowing the black limb could strengthen one to a terrifying degree. How powerful was this charred corpse when it was alive? Its origin was probably not simple.

Chapter 3551
Luo Zheng was not the only one who was curious. Nuwa, Fuxi, and even the Yaru Tribesmen and He Meng Tribesmen in the distance all stretched out their necks.
"Swish swish swish … …"
The tree branch danced on the ground again.
"A long time ago, a ball of fire rushed down from upstream. The fire was extremely strong. The divine river that was originally filled with water was burned dry by the ball of fire. When the fire was extinguished, only these three pieces of bodies were left. Their identities are unknown."
Everyone was a little disappointed when they saw the answer written by Blaze Maple Forest. After all, everyone wanted to see the secrets of Clear Sky and Clear Jade Sky.
According to what Blazing Maple Forest said, the fireball must have descended extremely long ago. In that case, the three black limbs had actually been preserved by Blazing Maple Forest for many years. That was why Blazing Maple Forest had secretly given Hei Meng that black limb.
"How do you know that the burnt corpse is poisonous to Immortal Realm cultivators?" Luo Zheng asked.
"You can try," Blaze Maple Forest stretched out the tree branch again and wrote on the ground.
"No need, I believe you," Luo Zheng shook his head. Luo Zheng could indeed try, but at this point, there was no need to doubt it.
"Swish swish … …"
Blaze Maple Forest's tree branch retracted, while the He Meng Tribesmen and Yaru Tribesmen breathed a sigh of relief.
Especially the patriarch of the He Meng Tribesmen, Wu Meng, his heart was filled with ecstasy.
He had tried countless times to reach Wu Meng's level, but every time he tried hard, he was disappointed.
Although it was his turn to lead the He Meng Tribesmen after the death of the previous patriarch, Wu Meng knew very well that his so-called patriarch title was not worthy of the title.
If he devoured the black limb now, he would become the next Hei Meng!
"Crunch, crunch, crunch … …"
Before the other He Meng Tribesmen could think of anything, Wu Meng grabbed the black limb and started to chew it.
Blaze Maple Forest only designated the black limb for the He Meng Tribesmen, not Wu Meng. If the other He Meng Tribesmen did not agree, the situation would become even more troublesome.
On the other side, the Yaru Tribesmen also had similar thoughts.
The Yaru Tribe had a much larger population than the He Meng Tribe. The entire Yaru Tribe had more than 40 branches. Every once in a while, one of the branches would lead a team into the Blazing Maple Forest. However, the Yaru Tribe's patriarch rarely came here.
According to reason, this clan branch should bring back the black limb and offer it to the patriarch, but this was obviously impossible. The leader of this clan branch was also devouring the black limb, waiting for his strength to explode and return to the Yaru Tribe.
He was afraid that another bloody storm would be set off.
Luo Zheng didn't have much interest in the disputes between these foreign races. He still had to deal with the small mountain of resources gifted to him by the Blaze Maple Forest …
…
On the other side of Blazing Maple Forest.
An energy fluctuation appeared out of nowhere. Then, the energy fluctuation was like a wave of water, and it was about to outline the shape of a door.
However, as soon as the door took shape, countless maple leaves exploded on the surface of the door, blasting the unformed door into smithereens.
Inside the door …
Dozens of long-haired monsters gathered at the door.
They felt the power of the maple leaf explosion outside the door and were all shocked.
"The explosion of these maple leaves is the energy of reality!"
"Our fur can't withstand the damage of reality energy."
"If we rashly go out, we'll die!"
The long-haired monsters were somewhat helpless.
They had finally caught a foreign race member with great difficulty. After hearing some information, they had directly rushed to Blazing Maple Forest. They didn't expect that this Blazing Maple Forest would be so strange. The maple leaves contained a terrifying amount of reality energy!
This kind of reality energy could practically ignore all defensive methods. The amount of energy released would be the same amount of damage to the target. There was almost no way to eliminate or reduce it.
"I don't care. In any case, do whatever you can to find my grandmother!" A crisp voice ordered. "If my grandmother dies, don't even think about undoing the curse on your body for the rest of your lives!"
Not far away, a human woman was leaning against the corner of a wall. Her slender, snow-white legs were pressed to the side. It was Ling Shuang.
Apart from her appearance, her body had undergone a significant change.
Her originally black hair had turned into purple crystals. Even her eyebrows were made of thin purple crystals. Her eyes flickered with a purple light, as if they were embedded with two purple gems.
Compared to before, Ling Shuang's aura was countless times stronger.
Even the foreign race members of the 33 Heavens could not be compared to her.
Many of the long-haired monsters had good tempers. They were afraid of hearing Ling Shuang's threat.
"We've already worked very hard!"
"We've already heard that she should be in Blazing Maple Forest …"
"But what if your grandmother died long ago?" A long-haired monster asked.
"It's the same even if she died long ago. As long as my grandmother is alive, I will help you!" Ling Shuang curled her lips.
At this moment, the white-haired monster held a black square brick and walked towards Ling Shuang.
A hole was dug in the middle of the black square brick. The hole was filled with purple liquid. As the liquid boiled, white fog flowed out. The white-haired monster said, "Your grandmother is alive. You also need to have enough strength to save her.
Your grandmother is dead. You also need to have enough strength to avenge her. Hurry up and drink. "
Ling Shuang originally wanted to refute, but she felt that the white-haired monster's words were not wrong. After pausing for a moment, she took the black square brick and began drinking the purple liquid in the brick.
"Since the door cannot be opened in Blazing Maple Forest, we will open it outside Blazing Maple Forest. You can enter and search," the white-haired monster suggested.
"But if we are exposed, won't we …" One of the long-haired monsters said worriedly.
The white-haired monster chuckled. "We can hide for a while, but not forever. They will find out sooner or later."
Thus, the long-haired monsters followed the white-haired monster's instructions and moved the door outside Blazing Maple Forest.
After the door was opened, four long-haired monsters quietly entered the Blazing Maple Forest, and the door disappeared.
Four hours later …
Four long-haired monsters returned from Blazing Maple Forest. One of the long-haired monsters patted the air lightly. The door appeared again. The four long-haired monsters opened the door and entered. The door disappeared again.
"How is it?"
Ling Shuang, who had been waiting for a long time, asked anxiously.
"We found out! Your grandmother is not dead, "the long-haired monster in the lead replied.
Ling Shuang suddenly stood up. A bright purple light spread out from her eyes. It spread along her eyebrows and hair. She released a strong and fierce aura.
Even though the long-haired monsters present were all very powerful, they were still suppressed by this aura. Especially the long-haired monster she was facing. It let out an ear-piercing sound from its throat. It was almost unable to speak.
"Cough, cough, cough …" The long-haired monster in the lead pointed at its throat.
Ling Shuang then realized her problem. She quickly retracted her aura. "So, sorry …"
She did not have full control of her power …
The long-haired monster panted a few times before telling Ling Shuang what happened in Blazing Maple Forest.

Chapter 3552
There were many different races in Tai Qing Heaven, and Blazing Maple Forest had just undergone a huge change.
These long-haired monsters did not encounter any trouble when they sneaked in, and they easily obtained information.
Ling Shuang was also surprised after hearing this.
"A seven to eight feet tall human saved Granny?"
"Yes, without a doubt. This human is said to be very powerful." The eyes of the leading long-haired monster flickered. These long-haired monsters were all very powerful. After hearing about such a powerful being, they wanted to compete with him.
"There are no seven to eight feet tall humans. Did he activate the Rage God's body?" Ling Shuang asked again.
"The power of the Rage God can't be used in Blazing Maple Forest, so it's definitely not the Rage God's body. No matter what, your Granny must be safe. That human defeated all the living beings in Blazing Maple Forest!" The long-haired monster continued.
The other long-haired monsters also focused their attention on Ling Shuang.
Ling Shuang had always rejected their request because she wanted to save her Granny. Therefore, all the long-haired monsters treated saving Elder Ying as their top priority.

White fog flowed like milk in a huge depression.

Standing at the edge of the depression and looking down, one's gaze could only see three feet. It was impossible to see what was inside.
Some curious Outsiders and even Sempiternal realm experts had tried to enter the white mist to investigate. However, none of them had ever come out of the white mist alive.
For countless years, what was inside the crater remained an unsolved mystery.
If someone were to risk their life and jump into the pit, they would find that they could not control their body in the white fog. Their entire body would fall to the bottom of the valley like a stone. There was a layer of bright yellow gelatin there, like a huge piece of amber.
This soft gel could slowly absorb everything that was attached to it, and then digest it bit by bit.
The living beings who were lucky enough to see this scene could see all kinds of living beings in the "amber." Some of them had just had a part of them corroded, and most of them were just hard skeletons …

Taboo.

Chapter 3555

Chapter 3556
About a hundred miles away from Luo Zheng, there were strange creatures hanging from vines.
The lower half of their bodies were like half of a gourd, while the upper half had bones that curved upwards. From the ends of the bones, there was a shining golden mane.
The bones were the bow frame, and the mane was the bowstring. These short creatures were the Arrowhead Essence Demons.
At this moment, they were taking out long green arrows and shooting them out without any hesitation.
The vines beside the Arrowhead Essence Demon leader had already borne a huge fruit. This fruit looked exactly like an eyeball, with a pupil and a sclera. This vine with an eye was called the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine.
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine could bear a fruit that looked like an eye. Its eyesight was extremely strong, and it could see tens of thousands of miles away.
The Victorious Sky Rat King said that the Arrowhead Essence Demons used the Shadow Imprint to lock onto them, but he was only half right. The Arrowhead Essence Demons really relied on this eyeball. After finding Luo Zheng's group through the Shadow Imprint, they could directly see Luo Zheng's group.

The relationship between the Arrowhead Essence Demons and the Heaven's Eye Vines was similar to the relationship between the He Meng Tribe and the Blazing Maple Forest. They were all associated races.

When the hard bone was pulled out, there were traces of black gas lingering around it. Then, they used the hard bone to string the bowstring. These bones were actually the Black Jade Bone Arrows that their leader had mentioned!
Every Arrowhead Essence Demon would grow six Black Jade Bones in their lifetime. These Black Jade Bones would not regenerate. If all six Black Jade Bones were pulled out, their lives would also wither.
In other words, they were using their lives to shoot out the Black Domain Bone Arrows!
Normally, Arrow Sprites would only use the Black Jade Bone Arrow when facing a mortal enemy. For example, when their clan's mortal enemy, the Sky Stork, attacked, Arrow Sprites would not hesitate to use it.
However, the temptation of Jie Yan's bounty for the "Secret Soul of Broken Bones" was too great, and the target happened to be passing through the territory of the Arrow Cluster Sprites. Of course, they were unwilling to let it go.
Under the leader's guidance, all the Black Jade Bone Arrows turned into a rain of swords.
At the same time the black jade bone arrow was shot, the leader of the arrowheads suddenly let out a long howl!
"Shh —"
Amidst this long whistle, all of the black jade bone arrows strangely stopped in the air.
"Feng, Feng, Feng …"
A strange shadow began to appear above the black jade bone arrow.
It was a strange beast head with green skin and fangs. It looked ferocious.
The arrow-headed demons possessed the unique bloodline of the "Winged Ghost". When the black jade arrow was fired, the leader also activated their bloodline ability.
"Whoosh —"
As the leader's voice became shrill, the long arrows with the heads of the "Winged Ghosts" let out wails and flew toward Luo Zheng's group a hundred miles away …
When the arrow-headed demons changed the Black Jade Bone Arrow, the green arrow rain on Luo Zheng's side naturally stopped.
However, Luo Zheng did not slow down at all. He was shot by the Arrow Cluster Sprites. He did not intend to let these guys go!
"The arrow-headed demons only shot three rounds before stopping?" Donghuang asked suspiciously.
"This is not in line with their way of doing things …" Elder Gall shook his head.
"The arrow-headed demons are not a powerful clan in Taiqing Heaven. The reason why they have a place is that they have a very powerful arrow. It is said that this arrow can only be shot by consuming their life force …"
Just as Nuwa was speaking, the Heavenly Hound of Trigram roared fiercely again.
Previously, the Heavenly Hound of Trigram would immediately warn when facing all kinds of dangers, but it was far less intense than this time.
Then, Luo Zheng saw a large group of black dots floating in the distance. They were heads with sharp teeth.
"Be careful, Luo Zheng. It's the Black Jade Bone Arrow."
This warning came from the Primeval Lord of Heaven in his mind.
Although Elder Gall, Nuwa, and Fuxi had heard of the arrow-headed demons, they had never provoked this clan. They did not have a deep understanding of the Black Jade Bone Arrow. Even Nuwa had only heard of it.
"Are these bone arrows very powerful?" Luo Zheng asked.
"They are arrows shot at the cost of one's life. They can directly consume the opponent's life force. This is the same as Ji's Purple Rain of Decay," the Primeval Lord of Heaven continued to explain.
There was a great ability in the Truth of Life called the Purple Rain of Decay. When this rain fell, it could corrode all the life in several large regions. It was a great killing weapon for harvesting lives.
In this battle at Li Mountain, Ji basically faced experts at the Immortal Realm. Their life force was extremely vigorous, so they naturally did not fear the corrosion of mere raindrops.
However, the Black Jade Bone Arrow was different. On one hand, it was more powerful than the Purple Rain of Decay. On the other hand, it was not scattered like the Purple Rain of Decay. It was enough to devour a single target.
In the process of understanding, the Black Jade Bone Arrow at the front had already shot over. Its target was Nuwa.
Luo Zheng did not hesitate. His right hand grabbed the Black Jade Bone Arrow at lightning speed.
With this grab, the powerful force crushed the bone arrow. However, the head of the Feathered Ghost attached to the bone arrow bit over, leaving a shallow mark on Luo Zheng's arm.
"Whoosh …"
Then, the head of the Feathered Ghost disappeared. At the same time, Luo Zheng felt a slight chill in his body. It was as if something had disappeared with the head of the Feathered Ghost.
It was the life force …
Luo Zheng instantly understood.
"Luo Zheng, are you alright?" Nuwa sharply sensed that this bone arrow was not simple.
"I'm fine!"
Luo Zheng relied on his vigorous life force and did not hesitate too much.
Facing the dense Black Jade Bone Arrows, his arms, which were as long as three feet and as wide as a wooden barrel, waved like two doors.
These Black Jade Bone Arrows also had a certain level of consciousness. Their target was still Nuwa and Donghuang on Luo Zheng's shoulder. However, once the body of the bone arrow was destroyed, the head of the Feathered Ghost could only attack from close range.
Luo Zheng swept over and destroyed dozens of bone arrows. Those Feathered Ghost heads bit Luo Zheng's arm.
Every time a Feathered Ghost head disappeared, Luo Zheng felt his body turn cold. It was as if a part of his life force had been taken away.
In the beginning, Luo Zheng did not change much. However, as Luo Zheng destroyed more and more bone arrows, his body finally showed signs of decline.

Chapter 3557
"Luo Zheng, why are you doing this?"
Elder Gall was the first to notice the abnormality in Luo Zheng's body.
Existences like them had an unlimited lifespan. As long as there was no external intervention, they would be immortal.

The inexplicable change in Luo Zheng's body was obviously due to the black jade bone arrows …

Luo Zheng's life force was being devoured. His speed, strength, and reflexes continued to decline.

The four black jade bone arrows found a gap and went through Luo Zheng's hands, heading straight for Donghuang.

How strong was Luo Zheng's life force?
"Luo Zheng, let's retreat from the Nirvana Realm first! Squeak! "Sky Conquering Rat King said excitedly.

Under the situation where the enemy was waiting for them, leaving the Nirvana Realm was a more dangerous choice.
Choice.

Actually, it was a piece of cake for the Origin God Blood to replenish his life force.
However, his bloodline was not under his control, and the Origin Divine Blood would sometimes not work, completely depending on his mood. Right now, he had no choice but to retreat.
Everyone returned to Mount Li once again. The white-haired Donghuang could no longer hold on and fell to the ground with a thud.

Fuxi didn't delay at all, and his figure flashed and teleported away.

"Buzz buzz!"
Two beams of green light shot out from its left and right pincers.
After a short while, Donghuang's aura gradually became thicker. His originally white hair quickly turned black, and he regained his vitality.

"Buzz buzz buzz …"

Ji's eyes narrowed slightly, and he immediately understood.
It wasn't that his Truth of Life wasn't effective. It was just that Luo Zheng had consumed too much of his life force. His body was like a bottomless abyss, and it wasn't easy to fill it up.
Ji naturally wouldn't give up. More life force continuously poured into Luo Zheng's body …
…
On the Nirvana Realm.

The arrow demons weren't surprised by the sudden disappearance of Luo Zheng's group. These Immortal Realm experts from the Chaotic World would usually use this move when they were in danger.
To deal with them, the Nirvana Realm creatures often prepared Soul-Avoiding Water. However, this time, it was too sudden, so the arrow demons didn't have the chance to apply the Soul-Avoiding Water on them.
"Cha cha cha cha …"
Those green Heaven's Eye Rock Vines had many arrow demons hanging on them. They threaded their way through the ground and moved forward at a surprisingly fast speed.
The arrow demons almost didn't have the ability to move. They relied on the Heaven's Eye Rock Vines to move.

Behind the hill not far away, a distorted space suddenly moved slowly. As the distorted space gradually recovered, three people appeared. They looked almost the same as Terror Roar. They were three Zhi people!

As soon as the three Zhi people appeared, all the arrow demons had already loaded their arrows and aimed at them.

Our Zhi people can't last more than one round in front of him. His life force is so strong.
His recovery speed must be amazing. It won't be long before he recovers his life force. I wonder if you have the confidence to deal with him? "

Chapter 3558

In Li Mountain.

Luo Zheng's body recovered from an old man to a middle-aged man and then gradually turned into a youth. His life force was replenished by about eighty to ninety percent. But in the process of replenishing life force, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly closed and he fell to the ground.

Within Grand Pure Heaven …

The Arrow Essence Devils and the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine were also astonished!

While Luo Zheng was killing the dark-kind warriors, the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine dragged the Arrow Essence Devils and quickly escaped.

The seventy to eighty black jade bone arrows that were originally hovering in the air shot down at Luo Zheng.
At the same time, thorny vines broke out from the ground under Luo Zheng's feet and wrapped around Luo Zheng layer by layer.

The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine also understood this point. It kept extending its thorny vines, wrapping around Luo Zheng layer by layer.

Just as he tore apart these vines, more black jade bone arrows fell like raindrops.
The Arrow Essence Devils were really risking their lives. Some Arrow Essence Devils would die after pulling out their last black jade bone, but they still gave their black jade bones to their companions to shoot. They were using their own lives to exchange for Luo Zheng's life, so from time to time …
Some Arrow Demons pulled out the Black Jade Bone and fell to the ground.
The large number of black jade bone arrows also had an effect. Luo Zheng's body was aging at a speed visible to the naked eye.
However, everyone on Li Mountain could also see this scene. "Ji" extended his pair of pincers again, continuously replenishing Luo Zheng's life force.
While consuming, he was replenishing …
The Arrow Essence Devils consumed their life force, while "Ji" only consumed the power of truth of life …
Luo Zheng withstood the rain of arrows and flew onto the nearest vine. He grabbed the vine with both hands and shook it fiercely. The Arrow Essence Devils that were not large in size all fell to the ground. Then, Luo Zheng closed in and used a finger to shatter them.
These Arrow Essence Devils were not only able to shoot fatal arrows, but their combat strength was extremely weak.
Luo Zheng followed the thick Heaven's Eye Rock Vine all the way to the top and arrived in front of the Arrow Essence Devil leader.

During this period of time, Luo Zheng had been hit by four to five hundred black jade bone arrows, but he looked like he was fine. This was impossible. The Arrow Essence Devil leader looked at Luo Zheng in despair.

Chapter 3559

In order to survive, how could it dare to hide anything?
After Luo Zheng touched the source of life, he could feel the vigorous life force of the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine. Even the memories of the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine were transmitted clearly. This small sprout indeed controlled the life and death of the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine.

After that, Luo Zheng returned to the Nirvana Realm. Then, Nuwa, Rat King Sheng Tian, and the other Immortal Realm cultivators also returned.

"Whoosh …"

The Arrow Demons were extremely violent. Usually, no one could get close to them. Therefore, even Nuwa knew very little about them.

The Heaven's Eye's eyesight was very strong. It could see everything within 10,000 miles.
On the side of the Blazing Maple Forest, flying snakes with transparent wings were skimming across the ground. Judging from the direction, they were coming straight for him …
On the southwest side, dozens of bull-headed men with golden axes on their shoulders were steadily advancing toward them.
Northwest …
Under this observation, Luo Zheng's expression became serious. He even saw the only two surviving Nightmare Demon Kings in the Nightmare Demon Impasse.

Luo Zheng ordered the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine.

Ah Huo, Lei Sha, and Jie Yan, the three members of the Essence Spirit Race, naturally saw what had just happened.

"Now that you mention it, it's indeed possible."
Both Lei Sha and Ah Huo were very surprised. There were many paths leading to the Clear Sky Heaven, and the Essence Spirit civilization could smoothly pass through as many as thirteen paths. The Wailing Path wasn't one of them. This path wasn't easy.

Chapter 3560
Every living being who could enter the Shangqing Heavens had their own unique methods.
It wasn't strange that Fuxi and Nuwa had methods that even the Elemental Spirit civilization didn't have.

Hearing Jie Yan's words, Ah Huo tilted his head and calculated in his heart. He then said, "The wind is about to rise in the Purple Wind Stream Gorge?"

"Even if you're proud, please don't laugh so creepily, okay?" Lei Sha couldn't bear it any longer.
Jie Yan wasn't an individual, but the Elemental Spirit people used him to express their feelings, making Lei Sha feel very tormented.
…
There was a wide passage on the lower left side of Mount Shuang Ji.
This passage connected to the north of Shangqing Heaven and was called the Silent Plank Road by the different races.
The Silent Plank Road was very safe. As long as one followed the rules here, they wouldn't be attacked and wouldn't be in any danger.
However, just like the name of the Silent Plank Road, one had to maintain absolute silence and not make any noise when passing through it.
Silence was the biggest rule here.
Some fist-sized creatures floated on the Silent Plank Road. These creatures were shaped like jellyfish and looked like cotton wool, light as if they were weightless. They floated in every corner of the Silent Plank Road all day long.
These white flock-like creatures were the Yakong tribe.
The Yakong tribe had extremely low intelligence. They ate space and never contacted the outside world. It was also very difficult for the outside world to capture or interfere with them.
Some people even claimed that the Yakong Tribe was not a living creature. They were just another form of space.
The biggest weakness of the Yakong Tribe was that they were timid. Once they were alerted by loud noises, they would transform from their cotton-like form to their spatial form.
Their space form was not so cute. That special space form squeezed together and crushed many aliens into mud. Only a few people could walk out of the Silent Plank Road alive after enraging the Yakong Tribe.
After Jie Yan gave the order, six people from different races came to the Silent Plank Road.
They maintained absolute silence as they flew along the silent plank road.
Every once in a while, they would place a silver tube on the ground. It was hard to tell how many of them had been placed along the way.

"Killed, squeak, they were all killed by him." Winning Sky Rat King pointed at Luo Zheng.
Blood Lion and Blood Crow both raised their eyebrows slightly.
They were already shocked that Luo Zheng could force his way out of the Black Sun. Hearing that Luo Zheng dealt with Arrow Cluster Demons alone, the twins were even more surprised.
Blood Wolf curled his lips. He was naturally suspicious. Unless he saw it with his own eyes, he would never believe it …
"Rumble …"
Soon, the Heaven's Eye Vine drilled into the ground and carried everyone quickly.
…
Ah Huo, Lei Sha, and Jie Yan were also very speechless when they saw this scene through the people from Hidden Shadow.
When the Immortal Hunters first appeared, Ah Huo and the others were a little excited.
A few years ago, Ah Huo had personally tracked down the remaining three Immortal Hunters, but he failed in the end.
There was no doubt about the strength of these three Immortal Hunters. If they were careless, they could threaten existences like Ah Huo, Lei Sha, and the others.
When the Immortal Hunters attacked, Ah Huo even told Jie Yan that if they could really get rid of Luo Zheng and the others, there was no need to reward them with the "Bone Fragment Secret Soul".
Although the Elemental Spirit Civilization and the Immortal Hunters had hatred for each other, the Elemental Spirit Civilization never went back on their words.
Who knew that not long after they attacked, the three Immortal Hunters inexplicably stood together with Luo Zheng and the others …
"Looks like your method isn't useful." Ah Huo glanced at Jie Yan.
The Elemental Spirit Civilization had suppressed the Path of the End for so many years. There should still be many Immortal Realms in Pingyi Sky who yearned for the Path of the End.
If Luo Zheng really rose in power, these Immortal Realms would probably choose to follow Luo Zheng.
This was definitely not what the Elemental Spirit Civilization wanted to see.
"If these Immortal Hunters are willing to go to the Path of the End with them, then let them be." Jie Yan was very calm.
Lei Sha laughed dryly and said nothing.
…
All the way north, the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine carried them through the soil layer continuously.
After traveling for about three thousand miles, they entered a hilly area with uneven heights.
These hills were extremely sturdy and could withstand the pressure of more than 100,000 Godly Jun. It was difficult for the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine to travel freely in them. It could only crawl along the hilly terrain.
"Ga ga!"
A series of strange cries came from the sky.
Seven or eight strange birds lined up in a row, circling and swooping down.
Crimson Wah Shadows whipped toward the sky like octopus tentacles. These Wah Shadows were not accurate, but they forced the strange birds to gather even more. Then, an Eight Trigrams Seal appeared on the bodies of these strange birds.
"Boom!"
Accompanied by a thunderous explosion, the corpses of the strange birds in the sky had already been blasted away, leaving only pieces of bird feathers fluttering slowly.
Fuxi blew on his fist and said indifferently, "Even these strange birds dare to come and join in the fun …"
Along the way north, some of the foreign race creatures that were extremely fast were killed by Fuxi. Fuxi's methods were very fierce. Those foreign race creatures were almost unable to react before they were smashed to death by him.
Blood Wolf quietly sized up Fuxi. These human Immortal Realms did not seem to be as weak as he thought. At least this Fuxi was qualified to join the Immortal Hunters even in the past.

Chapter 3565
After walking north for some distance in the hilly area, the height of these low hills continued to rise and gradually turned into towering mountains.
Fortunately, the distance between these mountains was very close, and the Sky's Eye Rock Vine could easily move on the top of the mountain.
After the Sky's Eye Rock Vine climbed over the largest mountain, a plank road appeared in the northwest direction. This plank road was like a natural moat, extending all the way into the distance, and the end could not be seen at a glance.
On one side of the plank road was a bottomless ditch, also known as the Purple Wind Canyon.
It was said that this huge canyon was forcibly blown out by the wind over the years.
When Fuxi and Nuwa saw this plank road, their faces suddenly changed. Donghuang, Elder Ying, and Sky Winning Rat King's faces also sank slightly.
"This road seems to be filled with white fog?" Luo Zheng frowned and asked.
"It's not white fog," Fuxi said bitterly. "This is the transformed Yakong Tribe …"
"Aliens?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
In fact, Luo Zheng also sensed the extraordinary power of space, and in this power of space, there was a breath of life passing out.
But this feeling was very strange, so Luo Zheng did not ask.
Nuwa nodded and said, "This natural moat-like road is called the Silent Plank Road. There are a large number of Yakong Tribe people on the Silent Plank Road. When passing through this plank road, you must not make a sound, or you will anger the Yakong Tribe people. Now the Yakong Tribe people should be angry, so they turned into this form to block the road …"
Luo Zheng walked to the entrance of the plank road and reached out to touch the layer of "white fog", but his fingers were blocked by a layer of invisible space wall.
"Hey, since the Elemental Spirit civilization knows your location, of course, they will infer where you are going. It is inevitable that the Yakong Tribe people will be angry and close this passage." Blood Wolf came forward and also reached out to press on the space wall.
"Just clean up the Yakong Tribe people," Luo Zheng said.
"You can try," Fuxi shrugged.
Luo Zheng did not hesitate and directly bombarded the space wall with a punch.
The power of his punch was not weak. The huge power fluctuation directly broke the space wall, creating a hole of fifty or sixty feet deep.
"Wu wu …"
At this time, two white dots appeared in the space wall above. These white dots could reflect the surrounding scene and could also make sounds. They could be regarded as the eyes and mouth of the Yakong people.
Seeing that his body was damaged, the Yakong tribesman kept whining, and the rest of his body began to squeeze toward the middle. After a while, the hole was repaired automatically.
The Yakong tribesmen had no sense of pain, nor did they have the concept of injury. When a part of their body was destroyed, they would fill it with other spaces.
In other words, Luo Zheng's punch had only made the Yakong Tribe slightly smaller …
Boom!
Luo Zheng once again erupted with a powerful force.
He used almost all of his strength in this punch. Even the three Immortal Hunters, who had seen a lot of things, were shocked.
They already knew that Luo Zheng's physical body was strong enough to kill Hei Meng, but they didn't expect his explosive power to be so terrifying. If this fist landed on their bodies, it would turn them into ashes.
"Crack …"
A long and thin hole appeared in front of him. It was at least a thousand feet long.
"Wu wu …"
The Yakong tribesman made a sound again, and his body shrank a little.
However, as his body shrank, the Yakong tribesman on top of him expanded …
Luo Zheng was speechless when he saw this scene. He had been busy for a long time in vain.
"Don't waste your time. Once the Yakong tribesmen expand, the Silent Plank Road will not be able to pass," Blood Crow said behind Luo Zheng.
Cleaning up the Yakong tribesmen was equivalent to cleaning up the space on the Silent Plank Road. It would take a long time.
"Is this the only way to go northwest?" Luo Zheng asked.
"There is another way," Nuwa stood up and said, "Continue to the north and change the route to go through the Purple Wind Stream Gorge!"
"Squeak, the Purple Wind Stream Gorge is indeed an alternative route, but once the wind blows, it will also be a dead end," Sky Winning Rat King said.
"But now you have no choice," Blood Wolf shrugged.
In Blood Wolf's opinion, these humans were seriously unprepared and had underestimated the ability of the Yuan Ling civilization.
The Yuan Ling civilization had cultivated in the Sanqing Heaven for so long, so how familiar were they with this place?
From the movements of Luo Zheng and the others, the Yuan Ling civilization could easily judge and respond.
Only in a truly dangerous place could they possibly avoid the Yuan Ling civilization's capture.
When the Immortal Hunters were chased by the Yuan Ling civilization, they had no choice but to dive directly into the "Evil Water Territory," and the Yuan Ling civilization gave up the pursuit.
Only in an almost unexplorable area like the "Evil Water Territory" could the Yuan Ling civilization be wary. However, the Immortal Hunters also paid a considerable price and were almost completely wiped out in the "Evil Water Territory."
Fuxi, Nuwa, Donghuang, and the others all looked at Luo Zheng. Although Luo Zheng was the lowest in seniority, he had obviously become the decision maker among this group of people.
Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment, and at this moment, a voice suddenly sounded in his mind.
Hearing this voice's reminder, his eyes flashed with a touch of light. "Then change the route to the Purple Wind Stream Gorge!"
At this moment, there was no time to hesitate. After climbing up the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine again, they slid down from the top of the mountain toward the east.
Continuing to the north for a distance, a plateau appeared in front of them.
The plateau went up a level every few hundred miles, like a huge staircase, called the Heaven Staircase Plateau.
The Immortal Realms of Pingyi Heaven would be scattered at the edge of Taiqing Heaven after passing through the "Heaven's End Path." They hoped to land near the Heaven Staircase Plateau the most because this area was very desolate and there were almost no powerful foreign tribes.
The Heaven Staircase Plateau had a total of seven steps. At the end of the seventh step was the famous Purple Wind Stream Gorge.
After the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine carried everyone up to the Heaven Staircase Plateau, Fuxi looked again with the Heaven's Eye, and his expression suddenly became more serious. "It seems to be even worse …"
"What's wrong? More foreign tribes gathered together? "Donghuang asked.
Fuxi shook his head. "They're gone. All the foreign tribes have disappeared …"
Hearing this, Nuwa, Elder Ying, and the others frowned. Beside them, Xue Ya also asked, "Even the Heaven's Eye can't see them?"
It was not unusual for foreign tribes to move forward in concealment.
However, the Heaven's Eye could see through almost all disguises and concealment methods. Now that it could not see any movement, it meant that the foreign tribes' concealment methods were extremely brilliant.
Fuxi shook his head and extended the vine from the Heaven's Eye to Xue Ya. Xue Ya looked around with the Heaven's Eye, but it was also empty.
Just as Xue Ya was about to speak, the Immortal Realms suddenly felt a powerful spatial energy coming from above. Then, a huge golden ax slashed through the space and came down.
This was a huge golden ax that was dozens of feet long. The edge of the ax was engraved with mysterious Sanskrit words. Rings of golden light spread out, emitting an extremely powerful pressure.
"Crack!"

Chapter 3566
The Heaven's Eye Vine's only weakness was its "Heaven's Eye."
As a plant, any part of its body could grow wildly after being cut.
Once, the Arrow Clan had fought against the "Flash Demons" of the Clear Sky. These Flash Demons could advance through space from thousands of miles away.
In the process of advancing, the Heaven's Eye Vine was cut into dozens of sections by the Flash Demons.
However, each section of the Heaven's Eye Vine became an independent part in a short time. Under the wild growth, it even gained more combat power.
However, this round of golden axes that came from the void was different. The part of the axes that were cut was plated with a circle of golden light.
If one looked carefully, they would find that this circle of golden light was a small golden flame.
This flame quickly spread along the cut of the vines, like a golden mouth that wanted to burn the vines clean.
Whoosh —
A sharp golden sword light cut the vine, cutting off the small section of the vine that was on fire. It was Donghuang who attacked.
The small section of the vine that was cut off was burned clean in the blink of an eye …
"Who attacked the golden axes?" Elder Ying asked with a solemn expression.
"I can't find the target. This spatial power appeared very strangely." Fuxi used his Heaven's Eye and spread out his Divine Sense.
After the giant axes appeared, they broke through the void and disappeared in front of everyone's eyes. Fuxi couldn't trace their origin at all.
"The golden axes that just appeared are the bull-headed people's weapons," Blood Lion said.
The bull-headed people were one of the most dangerous aliens in the Clear Sky. Their individual combat power was extremely terrifying, but their mobility was relatively weak. As long as they kept a distance from the bull-headed people, they could ensure their own safety.
"Hmph, those aliens aren't idiots. Since they have the same goal, of course they'll help each other. This is the most troublesome part." Blood Wolf curled his lips.
A single alien wasn't scary.
Many aliens in the Clear Sky were very strong, but almost every one of them had their own limitations.
The bull-headed people were known to have the strongest combat power. If they were surrounded by them, they would die without a doubt.
At that time, the Immortal Hunters were not afraid of the bull-headed people at all. They could easily play with them by relying on the advantage of speed.
But now it was different. All the aliens had to deal with Luo Zheng's group. Their abilities complemented each other and became extremely terrifying. Not to mention Luo Zheng's group, even the Wu Kong Clan and Yuan Ling Civilization would have to suffer when they encountered this "alien alliance".
"Buzz …"
Suddenly, there was a spatial fluctuation above everyone's head. Then, a golden light bloomed, and another golden axe came chopping down!
This time, the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine was prepared. Its snake-like vine body gently twisted and accurately avoided the chop of the golden axe. The golden axe only left a pure golden crack in the ground.
The small golden flame burned fiercely in the crack, but the golden flame did not seem to be able to burn the stone. It was quickly extinguished.
"No matter where they are, climb to the top of the Stairway Plateau first," Luo Zheng said.
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine didn't dare to waste any time. It twisted its vine body and began to dash across the plains.
"Buzz …"
While the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine was running, there was another spatial fluctuation above. This time, two golden axes appeared at the same time!
These two golden axes, one on the left and one on the right, completely blocked the escape route of the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine!
"To the left!"
Luo Zheng looked up and stared at the golden axe and roared loudly.
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine didn't think much and twisted its vine body to the left.
Seeing the golden axe chopping down, Fuxi, Nuwa, Victorious Sky Rat King and the others scattered. Only Luo Zheng faced it head-on!
"This kid …" The Blood Lion's pupils suddenly widened.
"Quickly dodge, do you want to die?" The Blood Crow loudly reminded.
The bull-headed man's axe was not only powerful, but the small golden flame on the axe was called "Golden Spirit Devouring Flame". If it was a dead object, it wouldn't be afraid, so it couldn't burn the stone. But if it was a living thing, it would be burned clean in a short time.
"Ka!"
Luo Zheng stood sideways with his hands over his shoulders and forcibly blocked the golden axe!
The extremely sharp golden axe couldn't cut through Luo Zheng's body.
"What a strong body!"
"His body is even stronger than Hei Meng's!"
"The problem is that the Golden Spirit Devouring Flame is very troublesome. It will devour him …"
The three Immortal Hunters stared nervously at Luo Zheng.
The small golden flame quickly spread along the blade of the axe to Luo Zheng's shoulder and burned with a sizzling sound.
Luo Zheng's almost indestructible body quickly wrinkled, cracked, and turned black.
Charred black.
"You'll let me burn it completely?" Luo Zheng murmured, holding the axe with both hands.
The Golden Spirit Devouring Flame spread and burned faster and faster.
If it wasn't restrained, Luo Zheng's entire body would be burned clean, of course, including the Origin God's Blood.
In fact, Luo Zheng's only bargaining chip against the Origin God's Blood was to "die together". If Luo Zheng wanted to help his companions and didn't threaten himself or the Origin God's Blood in his body, the Origin God's Blood wouldn't give in at all.
But in this situation, the Origin God's Blood reacted very quickly …
"Hum!"
Luo Zheng's shoulder suddenly swelled, forming a big bump. Then, this big bump suddenly exploded, revealing a big mouth full of sharp teeth inside!
"It's here again …"
Seeing the mouth on his shoulder, Luo Zheng showed a little helplessness on his face.
The Origin God's Blood was indeed powerful. At least until now, Luo Zheng hadn't seen its limits and shortcomings.
But every time this thing showed its magical power, it would make some strange and even terrifying things on his body …
"Whoosh …"
This big mouth sucked in all the Golden Spirit Devouring Flame, including the golden light on the axe blade.
In the blink of an eye, the shining golden axe turned into a piece of dull ordinary iron …
Luo Zheng exerted a little force with both hands. With a "crack" sound, he broke the huge axe into pieces!
Seeing this scene, the Immortal Hunters' eyes almost fell out of their sockets.
What's with the mouth on this guy's right shoulder?!
How could it devour the Golden Spirit Devouring Flame directly?
Among the three Immortal Hunters, Blood Wolf was the most sophisticated and knowledgeable one.
He guessed a little bit that Luo Zheng seemed to be able to adjust the structure of his body to deal with the crisis.
Since he could absorb the Golden Spirit Devouring Flame so easily, did he have the corresponding means to deal with other magical powers?
If he could really do it, wouldn't he be omnipotent?
However, Blood Wolf was just guessing in his heart. Maybe Luo Zheng's body just happened to restrain the Golden Spirit Devouring Flame.
The problem was that Luo Zheng had already broken free from the "Black Sun" in a strange form. There couldn't be so many coincidences in this world. Maybe Blood Crow and Blood Lion made the right choice?

Chapter 3567
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine was also a monster that had lived for countless years.
It felt the dense spatial fluctuations in the sky and was greatly shocked.
Blindly running away was a dead end. Now it could only pin its hopes on Luo Zheng. After all, Luo Zheng had just crushed a golden giant axe with his bare hands!
So while the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine was dodging, it also used Luo Zheng as a shield to face the golden giant axe!
In fact, even if the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine did not do this, Luo Zheng would still move on the vine and take the initiative to attack!
Every time the golden giant axe chopped down, the big mouth on Luo Zheng's shoulder suddenly sucked all the Golden Spirit Devouring Flame into its mouth. The originally shining golden giant axe suddenly lost its luster and turned into ordinary iron.
Although this ordinary iron still had the power to chop, Luo Zheng just pulled and twisted, and the giant axe was directly torn from the middle. Then, Luo Zheng casually threw it aside.
"Clang!"
"Ka ka ka!"
"Boom!"
Some of the giant axes were torn apart by Luo Zheng's bare hands, some directly blasted a huge gap, and some smashed a big hole.
In short, any golden giant axe that appeared out of nowhere was smashed into a piece of scrap iron by Luo Zheng.
It was unknown who used spatial magic. At first, the broken giant axes were collected, but soon the other party realized that the smashed giant axes were useless and simply gave up. So, wherever the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine passed, a pile of broken giant axes lay scattered on the ground …
In the beginning, when Luo Zheng attacked, Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others were shocked. Seeing that Luo Zheng was becoming more and more skilled and treated these giant golden axes as if they were nothing and easily dealt with them, their mood relaxed.
And right in front of them, at the end of the first step of the Stairway Plateau, there were hundreds of creatures with transparent wings floating. These creatures were only about half the height of a person, with big heads and small bodies, and looked funny.
A layer of spatial membrane floated around their bodies. It was this layer of spatial membrane that changed the surrounding light, allowing them and the bull-headed people to be completely "hidden".
Even if one's Spiritual Consciousness swept over this place, it would pass through it quietly.
These short creatures were the Sky Destroying Elves of the Grand Clear Heaven. Their race's talent was to manipulate space.
After the Air Destroying Elves and the bull-headed man met halfway, they did not fight openly and secretly like in the past, but chose to join forces for the first time.
The two foreign races were determined to get the "Secret Soul of the Broken Bone". After a discussion, the Air-destroying Elves and the Ox-headed Man lurked at the end of the first level of the Heaven Stairway Plateau to ambush.
The fighting power of the bull-headed people was extremely strong, and if they cooperated with the Air Destroying Elf, it would be icing on the cake, completely making up for their shortcomings.
In addition, they were in the dark while Luo Zheng's group was in the open. They should be able to easily destroy Luo Zheng's group if they were caught off guard.
But in the end, the difference between what they had imagined was too great …
The Air Destroying Elf passed the Ox Head Man's axe over, which was easily caught by Luo Zheng, and then smashed into a pile of scrap iron.
The axes of the bull-headed people were not casually forged. After the bull-headed people grew into real warriors, they would get a Golden Spirit Battle Axe. Most bull-headed people may only have this one in their lifetime.
Only when they made great contributions could they be rewarded with a second Golden Spirit Battle Axe in the clan.
The bull-headed people who came on this trip were the most powerful elites in the clan. Almost all of these elites had two Golden Spirit Battle Axes, and some even had a third.
At the beginning, when the Golden Spirit Battle Axes were thrown, the bull-headed people were still okay. But after seeing Luo Zheng tear the Golden Spirit Battle Axe apart, these bull-headed people were all heartbroken. Those who only had two Golden Spirit Battle Axes refused to throw, but instead quarreled with the Air Destroying Elves.
"What kind of idea are you dwarfs coming up with!"
"That kid is not afraid at all …"
"My axe, roar!"
The Air Destroying Elves did not show weakness and retorted.
"We overestimated you trash!"
"Don't you claim to have the strongest fighting power in Tai Qing Tian?"
"So many elites, but you can't take down one person …"
When the two sides were fiercely arguing, a blue-haired Air Destroying Elf said, "Shut up."
A golden-horned bull-headed person among the bull-headed people also roared, "Don't make unnecessary arguments!"
The blue-haired elf and the golden-horned bull-headed person were the leaders of these two elites. Under the scolding of the two aliens, the two alien races who were still arguing finally quieted down.
The golden-horned bull-headed person continued, "They still don't know where we are and are coming towards us. In a while, we will kill them directly!"
"Yes," the blue-haired elf's eyes flashed, "I will use the space maze to trap that human, and the others can easily strangle him."
Luo Zheng had the ability to swallow the Golden Spirit Devouring Flame, which was indeed beyond the calculations of the bull-headed people and the Air Destroying Elves.
But in their eyes, Luo Zheng's strength was still limited to his physical body, and was not difficult to deal with.
These two alien races were still lurking at the end of the first level of the Stairway Plateau, waiting for Luo Zheng and the others to slowly approach.
One thousand miles …
Three hundred miles …
One hundred miles …
When Luo Zheng and the others entered their field of vision, they were only thirty to forty miles away.
"Buzz buzz …"
The wings of the Air Destroying Elves trembled gently, and the rich power of space dissipated.
The fighting spirit of the bull-headed people was also ignited little by little, holding the Golden Spirit Battle Axes in their hands and breathing out hot breaths.
Twenty miles …
Ten miles …
Five miles …
With their existence, a few miles of distance was no different from facing them face to face.
"Do it!"
The golden-horned bull-headed person roared and issued an order.
The Air Destroying Elves instantly put away their space camouflage and shook their transparent wings. One by one, triangular space fragments were like raindrops, hitting the Heaven's Eye Vine.
The Golden Spirit Battle Axes of the bull-headed people shone brightly again, and the bull-headed people carrying the Golden Spirit Battle Axes also rushed up!
"Infinite Corridor …"
The blue-haired elf gently waved her hands, and a winding space corridor appeared in front of her.
"Buzz!"
With a wave of space fluctuations, the blue-haired elf had appeared above the Heaven's Eye Vine. Her hands suddenly slammed down, and the space corridor shrouded Luo Zheng. In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng disappeared and fell firmly into the space corridor.
"Da da da da da …"
The Heaven's Eye Vine was unable to dodge the triangular space fragments released by the Air Destroying Elves, and the huge vine body was instantly smashed to pieces.
The three Immortal Hunters, Fuxi, Nuwa, Donghuang, Heaven-Winning Rat King, and Elder Ying, were almost unable to fight back in the face of the bull-headed people's axes, and were directly chopped down in a pool of blood.
The entire process couldn't have gone any smoother …
"It's that simple?" The blue-haired elf glanced at the slaughtered humans in surprise, and then began to size up Luo Zheng in the "Infinite Corridor".
The Infinite Corridor had a powerful space force constantly distorting, but it shouldn't have much effect on Luo Zheng …
However, its goal was only to trap Luo Zheng …
When the blue-haired elf stared, her eyes suddenly widened. Within the Infinite Corridor, Luo Zheng's body had already been twisted into pieces by space.
An existence with a body strong enough to kill Hei Meng would be twisted to death by space force?
"It's fake!"
The blue-haired elf immediately reacted and roared loudly.

Chapter 3568
The bull-headed people and the Air Destroying Elves were already suspicious.
After all, Luo Zheng and his group were the targets of the Yuan Ling civilization's bounty. No matter how weak they were, they were still in the Immortal Realm.
Even if it was a surprise attack, it was impossible for these Immortal Realm experts to not even be able to fight back and be killed like chopped vegetables …
"What's going on?" the golden-horned bull-headed person asked.
The blue-haired elf shook the Infinite Corridor in his palm gently, and a distorted corpse was thrown out. Then, his transparent wings spread out gently. As his body rose vertically, the blue light in his eyes kept flashing.
Its Space Force spread out like a tide. The speed at which this Space Force dissipated was not fast, but the area it covered was quite large. Moreover, everything within the range of this Space Force could not deceive it, so it was much better than the probing Divine Sense.
One breath …
Three breaths …
Ten breaths …
The blue-haired elf's eyes showed confusion. On the first level of the Stairway Plateau, they did not find the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine. How was this possible?
"Stop looking, they've already gone up to the second level!" the golden-horned bull-headed person suddenly said.
"How do you know?" the blue-haired elf asked as he looked back, but he was immediately stunned.
He saw the golden-horned bull-headed person holding an ice mirror in both hands. This ice mirror was given by the Yuan Ling civilization. Now, the people of the Phantom Palace were still staring at Luo Zheng and his group. Through the ice mirror, they could easily find the position of Luo Zheng and his group.
The blue-haired elf's face showed embarrassment, mainly because they were too confident in their space talent. They did not expect Luo Zheng and his group to be able to do something under their noses. Instead, they forgot about this.
"Huff, huff, huff, huff …"
The blue-haired elf spiraled up again until he reached a height of more than ten thousand feet.
Each level of the Stairway Plateau was more than ten thousand feet high. The blue-haired elf's height was just enough to see the second level of the Stairway Plateau.
"Buzz …"
As the blue light in his eyes flashed, he said in a low voice, "They're running pretty fast …"
As his voice fell, he stretched out his hand and gently pushed forward. A slender space corridor also flew forward at an extremely fast speed. At the same time, he held the end of the space corridor tightly and pulled down fiercely, pulling down a space corridor at a right angle.
Through this space corridor, everyone could pass through. Its eyes seethed with killing intent. The price of playing with it was not small …
The second level of the Stairway Plateau.
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine was still twisting its body like a snake, crawling on the plain at an extremely fast speed.
In Xue Ya's hand, he was holding a small object. This object was the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine, but it had been shrunk ten times. Of course, Luo Zheng, Fuxi, Donghuang and the others had also shrunk according to the same ratio.
"How pitiful." Fuxi's eyes twitched as he stared at his double.
Even though he knew that these were fake goods, he still felt a little uncomfortable when he saw his body being dismembered.
"Squeak, as expected of an Immortal Hunter. His methods are so exquisite that it makes one gasp in amazement," the Heavenly Rat King praised.
Xue Ya said indifferently, "You must be joking. We've been active in Grand Clarity Heaven for so long, so we're very familiar with this place …"
The Immortal Hunters didn't join any faction. They just wandered around Supreme Clear Sky. Other than facing the pursuit of the Wu Kong Race and the Elemental Spirit Civilization, they also had to face a huge number of aliens.
To be able to survive in the cracks required considerable ability.
After they were attacked just now, Xue Ya quickly made a judgment. The other party was the Minotaur and the Air Destroying Elves. Only the Air Destroying Elves could throw those huge axes accurately.
Furthermore, Xue Ya accurately judged the direction of the Air Destroying Elves and the Minotaur Race. They were very likely guarding in front.
Therefore, Xue Ya used a treasure unearthed in Grand Clarity Heaven, a kind of white soil that could imitate everything. Whether this kind of white soil was a living thing was still debatable, but it did have the ability to imitate everything.
This kind of white soil was also a very popular Nirvana token in Grand Clarity Heaven. The Immortal Realm who carried this kind of white soil could almost transform into any appearance with the help of the white soil.
When Xue Ya used the white soil, Xue Ya also took out another treasure. It was a round mirror that was almost transparent and had an extremely powerful concealment effect.
After the white soil transformed into them completely, Xue Ya also used the round mirror to cover everyone. The two were connected seamlessly, thus forming a complete replacement.
Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others saw the means of the Immortal Hunters, and their expressions were full of amazement.
The Immortal Hunters had used this trick to avoid fatal pursuit twice. One time, they were besieged by the Corpse Fire Man in Grand Clarity Heaven, and the other time, they were chased by the Wu Kong Clan. Both times, the pursuers were easily fooled.
But this time, there was a huge loophole in this trick. The Air Destroying Elves and the Minotaur Race in the distance might not be able to detect it, but the two people from Phantom Palace were hanging on it, still marking Nuwa and Donghuang.
When Luo Zheng asked this question, Xue Ya just waved his hand. In this case, they could only bet that they would not look at the ice mirror of the Elemental Spirit civilization.
In the end, Xue Ya made the right bet.
"If they find out that it's fake, I'm afraid they will try their best to catch up," Nuwa said.
Even if they deceived the other party once, the crisis was still not over.
The Air Destroying Elves' spatial talent was terrifyingly strong. It was naturally not difficult to catch up with them.
"With the habits of the Air Destroying Elves, they will definitely chase after us, and they will definitely build a space channel …" Blood Wolf said as he took out a handful of brown ashes from his Sumeru Space.
Almost at the same time, a blue thin line extended from the horizon in the distance, approaching at a speed that could almost not be caught by the naked eye. It was the Infinite Corridor released by the blue-haired elf.
"Hoo!"
Blood Wolf blew gently.
The handful of ashes in his hand spread backward.
"Hoo hoo hoo hoo …"
It was obviously just a small handful of dust, but it covered a considerable area. The nature of the space in this area was changed. The large space seemed to be solidified, and then it collapsed into thousands of fragments!
The blue-haired elf originally intended to drive the Infinite Corridor to the front of Luo Zheng's group, and then send the Minotaur Race and the Air Destroying Elves.
Its Infinite Corridor was slightly different from the space channel. The space channel only narrowed the distance between two sections, but there was no concept of "distance" in the Infinite Corridor. It was completely defined by the blue-haired elf, so no matter how long the Infinite Corridor was, it could be crossed in one step.
But at this moment, the Infinite Corridor drilled into the area of dust …
If it was an ordinary space channel that touched that handful of ashes, only the front section would solidify and then collapse.
But the moment the Infinite Corridor touched the ashes, the whole body solidified, and with a "whoosh", it was completely broken from the beginning to the end.

Chapter 3569
The blue-haired elf was controlling the infinite corridor, putting the bull-headed man and the other air destroying elves inside.
But as soon as they stepped in, the infinite corridor collapsed, and all the bull-headed men and the air destroying elves fell from it.
Because the infinite corridor itself was equivalent to the distance of "one step," even if these bull-headed men and the air destroying elves had just entered, some of them had reached the second floor of the Heaven Stairway plateau, and more fell from a height of thousands of feet to the ground of the plateau.
This height could not hurt these aliens, but the bull-headed men and the air destroying elves were in a straight line, scattered on the ground …
The blue-haired elf's face was as ugly as it could be. It stared blankly at the collapse of the infinite corridor, and the space debris scattered down.
"What's the matter? You can't hold on? "The golden-horned bull-headed man asked.

Most space magical powers would fail under the effect of bone-condensing powder, and the infinite corridor of the air destroying elves naturally could not withstand it.
"These people's tricks are endless, quite tricky," the golden-horned bull-headed man frowned and asked.
The other party's series of actions clearly played them.
"Tricks will always be tricks, when it's time to see the real thing, they will die!" The blue-haired elf's eyes were filled with a layer of blue light, and the killing intent spread with the power of space, making the surrounding air destroying elves and bull-headed men feel a chill.
…
The height of each step of the Heaven Stairway plateau was the same, but the width was different.
With each step, the width was about one-third less, that was to say, the width of the second step was one-third shorter than the first.
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine certainly knew the power of the bull-headed men and the air destroying elves.
They were basically the two most troublesome alien races in Tai Qing Tian, and once caught, they would die.
This vine almost squeezed its own potential, running on the Heaven Stairway plateau, and it did not take long to cross the second level of the plateau, the third level …
The Heaven Stairway plateau was getting narrower and narrower with each step, and the time to cross was getting shorter and shorter.
When they reached the fourth level, the bull-headed men and the air destroying elves finally gathered the scattered people, and then the blue-haired elf once again used the infinite corridor.
With the previous example, the blue-haired elf did not extend the infinite corridor to the back end of Luo Zheng's group, but extended it from the other side until it overtook Luo Zheng and stopped.
On the back of the Heaven's Eye Vine, Fuxi was observing closely with his Heaven's Eye. Naturally, he saw that thin line.
"That thin line is catching up again. It's on the other side of the plains," Fuxi warned everyone.
Xue Ya squinted his eyes and stared into the distance. The Infinite Corridor was just a thin blue line. Separated by a considerable distance, it was naturally impossible to see clearly with the naked eye. Xue Ya shook his head and said, "It's too far. It's impossible to rush over and destroy that passage."
"We can only speed up and rush over," Nuwa said.
Hearing their words, the Heaven-Eyed Rock Vine that was moving forward with all its might sped up again. The three Immortal Hunters were surprised by its speed. Blood Crow and Blood Lion knew the Arrow Demons relied on the vine to move, but they did not expect it to be so fast …
However, just as they were about to rush to the fifth level of the Stairway Plateau, a large number of densely packed things suddenly rushed out from the plateau.
Everyone took a closer look, and their faces darkened. It was a large swarm of Winged Flying Snakes that covered the sky and earth.
These Winged Flying Snakes were nicknamed Dragonflies. They were not big, only as long as a human baby's arm. However, each Dragonfly contained a strange poison. More importantly, they covered the sky and earth every time they appeared. There were so many of them that it was terrifying.
"Do the three of you have a way to deal with the Dragonflies?" Victorious Rat King asked directly.
These Immortal Hunters were too experienced. Naturally, the Immortal Realms present placed their hopes on them.
"The Dragonflies are most afraid of the smell of sulfur. As long as we have one, it'll be enough to protect us," Blood Wolf said.
"But we don't have it." Blood Lion spread his hands. "I wonder if you have it with you?"
The Dragonflies were located in the west of Tai Qing Sky. Unless there was an urgent matter that required them to break into the Dragonflies' territory, the Immortal Hunters would only dig for sulfur for backup. Under normal circumstances, they would not prepare for it.
Donghuang, Elder Ying, and the others shook their heads …
They had never even heard of sulfur. How could they have it?
The three Immortal Realms' expressions turned ugly. If they did not have that thing, they might not be able to escape today.
Seeing the Dragonflies in the sky getting closer and closer, they could almost see the poisonous jaws of the Dragonflies. Everyone's hearts were pounding.
"You can use the Black Sun to suck these Dragonflies in," Nuwa suddenly suggested.
Blood Lion shook his head. "We can't do it."
Blood Lion did not explain why they could not do it.
After all, they had just joined this team. They did not completely trust Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng knew that the space inside the Black Sun was not big. Moreover, the creatures that were sucked in would not die instantly. Instead, the Black Sun would wear them down bit by bit.
The real power of the Black Sun was that it was difficult to break free from it.
As long as the two brothers were separated, the Black Sun would not have an exit. No matter how powerful an existence was, it would be worn down to death sooner or later.
Just now, the two brothers had sucked so many Zangma people into the Black Sun. They did not know if they had destroyed them. Even if they had killed all the Zangma people, the Black Sun could not withstand so many Dragonflies.
"It is said that these Dragonflies have very weak defenses. Just kill them," Fuxi suddenly said from the side.
"Kill so many Dragonflies one by one?" Blood Wolf glanced at Fuxi as if he were looking at something.
"Even if we can do it, it will take a long time," Blood Crow said.
Right now, all the Outsiders in Grand Clarity Sky were gathering towards them. Not to mention the difficulty of killing the Dragonflies, the time it would take would be enough for the other Outsiders to surround them.
"It won't take too long!" Fuxi smiled. The Sumeru Space in his hand flashed with blue light, and a large amount of brown powder poured out.
When Blood Wolf saw the powder, his eyebrows shot up. "What powder is this?"
Even though the Immortal Hunters were experienced and knowledgeable, they did not know how to use Prison Lock Lightning. Naturally, they did not recognize this powder.
"Lightning Solidifying Powder." Fuxi chuckled.
Then he shook his hand gently. An invisible force fixed the powder in place, forming it into the shape of a palm. The Lightning Solidifying Powder was as tall as a person.
In the Sky Thunder Plains, Luo Zheng had collected a large amount of Lightning Solidifying Powder, just in case he needed it.
Fuxi had taken out half of it!
"What is Lightning Solidifying Powder?" Blood Crow asked curiously.
Fuxi raised his hand and looked at the sky. An Eight Trigrams mark appeared in the void, which was also the central area where the Dragonflies were the most concentrated. At the same time, he said, "Have you heard of the Sky Thunder Plains?"
"Of course." Blood Crow nodded.
"This is the lightning of the Sky Thunder Plains!"
After saying that, Fuxi pushed his palm upward.
The Lightning Solidifying Powder palm suddenly disappeared, and the next moment, it appeared on the Eight Trigrams.
"Pop!"
The moment they collided, the Eight Trigrams shattered. Along with the Eight Trigrams was a ball of blinding lightning.

Chapter 3570
There was no doubt about the power of the lightning powder. The Heaven's Wrath Beast that had chased the human race into a dead end was killed by the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning. Sui Meng.

Even if the two alien tribes were traveling side by side, they still maintained a considerable distance from each other.

The temperature at the top of the Stairway Plateau was getting lower and lower. Thick snow was piled up on the plains, and everything was white.
After the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine crossed the sixth level, it quickly climbed up the mountain wall of the seventh level.
The three Immortal Hunters, Nuwa, Fuxi, and the others all seemed rather nervous.
If there was a gust of wind in the Purple Wind Stream Gorge, the Stairway Plain would be a dead end. There would be no way to escape!
After the Heaven's Eye Vine reached the seventh floor, Blood Lion, Blood Crow, Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others held their breaths and looked into the distance.
In the distance, there was a huge and open canyon …
In this canyon, wisps of faint purple wind fluttered up and down like purple silk.

Nuwa frowned. There was an unsolvable problem in front of her.
Donghuang, Elder Ying, and the Immortal Hunters' expressions turned from anticipation to disappointment.
The Purple Wind Stream Gorge was insurmountable when there was a gust of wind.
All creatures that tried to fly over would be sucked into the gorge. There was no chance of survival …
The Purple Wind Stream Gorge contained the law of wind. It was almost unbreakable.
"The wind is in the Purple Wind Stream Gorge. Can't we fly over from above?" Luo Zheng stared at the purple wind and asked.
"Haha …" Blood Wolf sneered. He didn't know where Luo Zheng got his skills from, but he knew very little about the Three Pure Sky. "If you fly over from above, the purple wind will also sweep you up. As long as there's a gust of wind, it's a dead end. You shouldn't have chosen this path just now!"
Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others frowned. Their faces were gloomy. They had made the wrong bet this time.

"What do you mean by that …" Blood Wolf was even more dissatisfied. He regretted that he cared for the Blood Lion and Blood Crow brothers. He shouldn't have followed these humans.
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine certainly knew what everyone was talking about, but it kept silent. From the moment it handed over its life origin, its life was no longer in its hands.
Not long after everyone arrived at the edge of the Purple Wind Stream Gorge, the other races climbed up one after another from the other side.

Three or four giants more than two hundred feet tall, like withered trees …
