The bull-headed man and the Air Destroying Elves also appeared soon.
These aliens didn't rush up rashly.
The blue-haired elf had a good plan. He wanted the other races to wear him down, but the other races weren't stupid.
Luo Zheng and the others were wanted by the Elemental Spirit civilization. They were definitely not weak!
"Brat, you destroyed my Ominous Earth and killed my people. Let's see where you can run today!" One of the Nightmare Demon Kings took two steps forward. He couldn't wait to swallow Luo Zheng.
When the other aliens heard this, their faces changed slightly.
The withered tree-like giant asked in a slow tone, "Nightmare Demon King, has the Ominous Earth been destroyed?"
Now the Ominous Earth was dead silent. Outsiders didn't dare to go in and inquire. There weren't many people who knew the situation.
"Humph!" Nightmare Demon King didn't answer.
"Was it destroyed by them?" The withered tree-like giant was the type to get to the bottom of things.
"It's that brat!" Nightmare Demon King gnashed his teeth and pointed at Luo Zheng.
Hearing this, the strange expressions on the faces of the aliens became more and more intense.
The Nightmare Demons were very strong, at least in the top three. It was impossible to destroy them.
Wasn't this guy who defeated Hei Meng just physically strong?
It seemed that the information they got was completely wrong …
Surprised, these aliens didn't retreat. After all, there were so many of them.
But these aliens generally hoped that others would make the first move. Even Nightmare Demon King controlled his hatred and stared at Luo Zheng.
The situation was deadlocked.
Everyone on the Heaven's Eye Vine looked dignified. On the contrary, Luo Zheng's face was much more relaxed.
The deadlock lasted for about half an hour. Luo Zheng seemed to sense something. He glanced at the Purple Wind Gorge behind him and suddenly said, "Senior Fuxi, prepare the lightning fixing powder."

Chapter 3571
In such a situation, there was indeed no way to retreat.
Fuxi did not hesitate and took out the lightning-solidifying powder.
"Crash!"
With a gentle shake of Fuxi's hand, the scattered lightning-solidifying powder was fixed by an invisible force and completely gathered together, turning into a huge palm.
This palm was bigger than the previous one.
When the golden-horned cow-headed man and the blue-haired elf saw this palm, their pupils could not help but shrink slightly.
The other cow-headed men even subconsciously took a few steps back …
They did not understand how this thing got the thunder of the Heavenly Thunder Plain, but the power was really terrible.
In this case, the only consequence of being the leader was to be blasted by the thunder and not even a trace of dregs would be left.
The cow-headed man and the air-destroying elf now had the advantage. They had seen the power of the lightning-solidifying powder, and now they were waiting for the idiots who had not seen it to rush up.
However, no one who could survive in the harsh environment of the Three Pure Heavens was stupid.

Not to mention those huge fellows, even this palm-sized rat-shaped creature was not easy to deal with.
These mouse-shaped creatures were called "terror rats". The destructive power of their seemingly small bodies was almost one-fifth of the cow-headed men's. The problem was that there were more terror rats than cow-headed men!

The Elemental Spirit civilization did not put a bounty on the three of them. If things really got chaotic later, he would find a way to save the Blood Lion and Blood Crow brothers. As for these humans — he was already very considerate of them by not hitting them when they were down.

The earthworm flies suffered a great loss from the lightning-solidifying powder. When they saw the lightning-solidifying powder in front of Fuxi, they instinctively showed fear.
However, the earthworm flies did not reveal the information about the lightning-solidifying powder. Instead, they very cautiously retreated to the side and hid their poisonous jaws.
After the Ground Dragonfly, a few more groups of foreign tribesmen came up one after another. The originally small seventh floor of the Stairway Plain seemed to be a little cramped. When the last group of foreign tribesmen came up, everyone's eyes lit up.

These creatures made of stone had very strong bodies and infinite strength. Strictly speaking, the Stone Spirit Clan completely belonged to the Elemental Spirit civilization. The Elemental Spirit civilization had always treated the Stone Spirit Clan differently and had a very good relationship with them.
However, the Stone Spirit Clan's characteristic was that their spiritual intelligence was extremely low. They had almost no strategic ideas in their minds and were very easily deceived, so they were not very useful.
After the Elemental Spirit civilization issued the bounty, the Stone Spirit Clan hurried over. However, they did not come for the bounty, but purely to help the Elemental Spirit civilization.

After these Stone Spirit Clan members climbed up one by one, their eyes locked onto Luo Zheng and the others.
No one spoke, no one gave them orders.
They then took a step forward and charged towards Luo Zheng's group.
"Dong dong dong dong …"
When Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others saw these stone people, they also knew that things were not good.
The other mutated races were still in a stalemate because they were suspicious of each other, but these stone people were born with a screw loose. If they were bullied by the other mutated races, how would they deal with the first wave of attacks?
However, the situation was worse than they had imagined. These stone people did not think much at all and rushed directly toward them!

This was a matter of life and death, so Fuxi also became very serious.

It was naturally not easy to corrode existences like the Stone Spirit Clan members. Moreover, the fog people were very gentle. Their target was the spider silk that was wrapped around the Stone Spirit Clan members.
Blood Wolf's body turned into such thin spider silk, and it was corroded in the blink of an eye.

Chapter 3572

All of the strange races stared at Fuxi at the same time …
It seemed that this lightning was the human's trump card.
Such a powerful brown powder should be limited in quantity. At the very least, it should be very rare.
If Fuxi couldn't take it out, the situation would become simple.
Fuxi realized that all the strange races' gazes were focused on him, and he felt a little uncomfortable.
Of course, he knew what these Outsiders were thinking!
The problem was that he had already used up all the lightning-fixing powder in the last two times. Where was he going to get more?
When Fuxi's body stiffened, Goddess Nvywa suddenly said to Blood Wolf, "Immortal Hunter, do you still have some brown Divine Thunder Powder? Please take it out!"
What the hell was Divine Thunder Powder?
Before the Blood Lion and Blood Crow brothers could understand, Blood Wolf had already understood. He turned over his hand and poured out a steady stream of brown powder.
The brown powder was not Lightning Solidifying Powder, but the Bone Congealing Powder that had been used to destroy the Infinite Corridor of the Air Destroying Elves.
The two powders looked exactly the same. They were both brown, but their auras were completely different. One contained the power of thunder, while the other contained the power of space. If one used their divine sense to sense them, they would be exposed instantly.
However, as an Immortal Hunter, Blood Wolf was very experienced. When he poured out the Bone-Condensing Powder, he concealed the aura in it. His technique was very clever.
Seeing Blood Wolf's series of reactions, Nuwa's eyes also projected a look of appreciation.
These Immortal Hunters could move around calmly in the Sanqing Sky. Their reactions were indeed agile.
Coincidentally, they had just obtained the Bone-Condensing Powder, and there was a lot of it. The endless Bone-Condensing Powder turned into a palm that was more than ten feet tall, standing in front of Fuxi.
"…"
When the aliens saw the huge brown palm, they were also speechless.
The brown palm just now was not even half of this one, but it had destroyed all the Stone Golems with strong defensive power. How terrifying was the power of this brown palm?
So the situation returned to before, and the two sides were in a stalemate again.
However, although Blood Wolf and Fuxi cooperated very cleverly, some people still saw some doubts.
The golden-horned bull-headed man knew the identities of Blood Lion, Blood Wolf, and Blood Crow from the beginning. These three Immortal Hunters were not of the same race as human beings. When Nuwa asked Blood Wolf for the powder, the other two Immortal Hunters, Blood Lion and Blood Crow, were stunned. They seemed to know nothing about it, which was too strange.
What was even more strange was that Blood Wolf concealed the aura of the brown powder, which was even more obvious.
The golden-horned bull-headed man secretly transmitted his analysis to the blue-haired elf. The blue-haired elf also agreed, but he sneered and asked, "Do you dare to rush up? If you do, I'll send you over. "
The golden-horned bull-headed man glared at the blue-haired elf and did not say anything.
Although he was suspicious, he did not want to risk his life.
However, the situation could not continue like this …
Soon, some aliens began to come up with ideas. They asked each alien race to gather a few people and attack together.
This idea was soon accepted, and it was considered an acceptable plan.
Seeing that the aliens had started a new round of preparations, Fuxi and the others were gloomy again.
After all, they were still in a desperate situation. Although they could still hold on for now, they could not hold on until the wind in the Purple Wind Gorge stopped, right?
Very quickly, the non-human races selected their men of sacrifice …
The purpose of these warriors of sacrifice was to test the "bomb" in Fuxi's hand. Once it was confirmed to be real, the warriors of sacrifice would definitely die.
However, the orders of the patriarch could not be disobeyed, and these warriors of sacrifice did not complain. After mixing into a new team, the warriors of sacrifice were ready to attack.
Fuxi frowned. The Eight Trigrams Seal had already floated in front of him, and the huge palm was about to attack at any time.
The warriors of sacrifice were trembling violently. After the palm exploded, their fates were self-evident.
"Are you looking for death?!"
Fuxi roared, and the Eight Trigrams Seal began to rotate.
Seeing that Fuxi was really about to unleash this palm strike, these people who had gritted their teeth and were prepared to die suddenly stopped. Some of them even retreated …
However, how would Fuxi dare to attack?
If he did, he would be exposed.
From the beginning, Luo Zheng had also changed his calm expression. There was a faint trace of anxiety flashing in his eyes. Although he hid it well, Nuwa still caught it.
His item had not arrived yet.
In the current situation, they could only hold on for a few breaths …
"It's fake, squeak!"
"He doesn't dare to use it. This is their last hope, quickly attack!"
"There are no cowards in the bull-headed race!"
The alien race leaders began to encourage and force them.
The men of sacrifice were extremely unwilling, but they still gritted their teeth and charged forward.
No matter how Fuxi acted, he could not scare the warriors of sacrifice away …
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly sensed something.
At the far end of the Purple Wind Gorge, there was a purple wind mixed with a touch of green "wind", and this green wind was rolling and dancing, approaching at an extremely fast speed.
"Senior Fuxi, attack with this palm!" Luo Zheng suddenly said.
"Why?" Fuxi was stunned.
"Listen to me!" Luo Zheng ordered in a deep voice.
Fuxi was very unhappy with Luo Zheng's tone, but at this critical moment, he did not care. He attacked with the giant palm formed by the Bone Congealing Powder.

Chapter 3573
Bone Congealing Powder and Lightning Solidifying Powder were similar in appearance, but their properties were worlds apart.
After the Bone Congealing Powder was thrown out, it spread out with a "boom".
Generally speaking, a small amount of Bone Congealing Powder was enough to solidify the spatial passageway and then collapse it. However, after a large amount of Bone Congealing Powder exploded, it actually affected the normal spatial passageway as well.
The spatial passageway around the group of death warriors suddenly became extremely viscous. These foreign race death warriors also seemed to be moving forward in heavy oil. Their movements were slow and even somewhat comical.
That was how it looked to outsiders, but the death warriors in the spatial passageway did not think so.
They felt the changes in their surroundings and were completely disheartened...
It's over, it's over, it's all over...
We're dead for sure!
For our race, sacrifice yourselves!
Every foreign race had different requirements for their own clansmen. Their inner thoughts were all different.
However, this "viscous" feeling only lasted for two breaths of time before it disappeared.
Everything returned to normal...
They looked at their own bodies and then at their own hands. A trace of unconcealable ecstasy appeared on their confused faces.
"It's fake!"
"That's Bone Congealing Powder! It's used to destroy spatial passageways! "
"The powder from before is already gone!"
The death warriors immediately reacted. The eyes of the foreign race people in the distance also shone with a fiery light.
"Squeak squeak..."
The Terror Rats let out squeak squeaks. Their originally messy positions suddenly became orderly. According to a certain pattern, three, five, seven Terror Rats stacked together and then charged forward...
"Squeak, squeak, squeak..."
The Withered Tree Giants also took a step forward.
Although their movements were slow, each step covered a considerable distance. A distance of several tens of thousands of feet was only a few steps to them.
The fog people directly floated forward...
The blue-haired elf and the golden-horned ox head were also anxious at this time.
They didn't think that the brown powder in the hands of these guys was really fake!
Especially the blue-haired elf, he wanted to slap himself a few times. After all, he had suffered from the "Bone Congealing Powder" before. He should have thought of this in advance.
However, there was still a chance. In any case, Luo Zheng and the others could not escape.
However, they had never thought that the so-called "unable to escape" idea was not reliable.
As the Outsiders rushed forward, a green light floated up from the Purple Wind Stream Gorge.
Along with the green shadow came the wisps of purple wind in the Purple Wind Stream Gorge. These purple winds were the deadliest catchers in Grand Clarity Heaven, but they could not catch the green shadow.
It was only when the green figure rose into the air that the Outsiders were able to see what it was.
It was a huge eagle!
The feathers, which looked like they were carved out of green jade, were crystal clear and fluttering in the wind.
When the purple wind blew over, it actually slid past its feathers, unable to do anything to this giant eagle …
"Wing, Wing King!"
When Blood Wolf saw this behemoth, he looked very excited.
Luo Zheng found it strange that Blood Wolf knew Yi Wang, but it was not difficult to understand after thinking about it.
The Green Jade Civilization that the Wing King once led was also an extremely powerful dominator level civilization. However, during the battle with the Wu Kong Race, the Wing King had no choice but to lead the Green Jade Civilization to hide in the Color Realm Dark Domain.
Their choice was similar to that of the Human Race, but there were more elites of the Human Race trapped in Grand Clarity Heaven.
When Nuwa, Fuxi, Donghuang, and the others saw this huge eagle that looked like it was carved out of green jade, their eyes were filled with awe. It could fly freely in the Purple Wind Stream Gorge. So this was where Luo Zheng's confidence came from?
"Ka, ka!"
The Wing King stretched out a pair of sharp eagle claws and grabbed the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine's body like an eagle catching a snake. The difference was that the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine was very clear about its situation and was very "obedient" and did not move.
"Stop this bird!"
"Squeak, squeak, squeak …"
The Terror Rats roared and threw the weapons in their hands madly.
The fog people also waved their black hooks, wanting to hook Luo Zheng and the others back …
Looking at the aggressive attacks of these foreign tribesmen, the Winged Monarch spread his wings and flapped them forward fiercely.
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
The seventh level of the Stairway Plateau was suddenly filled with strong winds.
If it was just strong winds, it would not be a problem. The Outsiders present were not necessarily afraid of the Wing King.
Over the years, the foreign races had seen many experts from the Sovereign level civilizations.
The problem was that this was the Purple Wind Stream Gorge!
It was fine if the Wing King could avoid the purple winds, but when he flapped his wings to create strong winds, he also attracted the purple winds in the Purple Wind Stream Gorge!
Almost all of the Outsiders' eyes were filled with fear.
Those death warriors from before had been lucky enough to escape with their lives, but they were at the front and could not avoid the purple winds this time.
These purple winds were like silk. After wrapping around these death warriors, they were dragged towards the Purple Wind Stream Gorge.
Those death warriors were not the only ones who were unlucky. The terror rats and fog people who had just charged forward were also tragically caught by the purple winds.
No matter how they struggled, no matter what ability they used, not a single one of them was able to break free.
The other Outsiders saw that the situation was not good and could only frantically retreat. In the end, they could only watch as the Wing King soared into the sky and glided towards the Purple Wind Stream Gorge …
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
The purple winds in the Purple Wind Stream Gorge seemed to have their own consciousness as they crazily chased after the Wing King.
However, the Wing King was extremely familiar with the flight pattern of these purple winds. He did not see the Wing King dodge. These purple winds could not touch him at all. Of course, they could not touch the Heaven's Eye Vine in his claws either.
"Brat, you scared me to death. I didn't expect you to have such a trick up your sleeve." Fuxi took a deep breath.
Nuwa, Heaven Winning Rat King, Donghuang, Elder Ying, and the others also relaxed. Nuwa originally thought that the Primeval Lord of Heaven would make a move. She did not expect that at the crucial moment, the Wing King of the Green Jade Civilization would be attracted.
The upper end of the Heaven's Eye Vine was raised high. The Blood Wolf stood at the top and was communicating with the Wing King.
The Blood Lion and Blood Crow brothers had already answered everyone's questions.
When the Green Jade Civilization was roaming the Grand Clear Heaven, a Green Jade Spirit had gone missing.
Later, the Wing King fled with his clan and hid in the 13th Heaven. This "Green Jade Spirit" did not know and became a remnant of the Green Jade Civilization.
Due to fate, this Green Jade Spirit became a member of the Immortal Hunters and had a very good relationship with the Blood Wolf. They were like brothers.
Unfortunately, this Green Jade Spirit eventually died in the pursuit of the Wu Kong Clan. Before dying, this Green Jade Spirit entrusted the Blood Wolf, hoping that he could help find the Green Jade Civilization.
The Blood Wolf, who had been active in the Grand Clear Heaven for a long time, naturally could not find the Green Jade Civilization in the 13th Heaven.
However, the Blood Wolf did not expect to meet the Wing King of the Green Jade Civilization on this day and at this time. One could imagine the excitement in the Blood Wolf's heart.

Chapter 3574
The Blood Wolf told the Winged King everything about the Green Jade Spirit.
The Winged King was silent for a while before letting out a sigh. "So 'Dong' did so many things after we left. It's my fault. I shouldn't have been separated from him back then."
The status of "Dong" was almost the same as the Green Jade Spirit in Luo Zheng's body. He was also a core figure of the Green Jade Civilization and the left and right arms of the Winged King.
"Dong doesn't regret it, nor does he blame you. He just wants me to tell you everything he has done," the Blood Wolf said again.
The Winged King nodded. "I understand. If there's a chance, I hope you can take me to the place where it fell."
"There will be such a day!" The Blood Wolf cupped his hands.
The Blood Wolf was also a righteous person. Otherwise, he wouldn't have risked his life to stay with the two brothers just because of the thoughts of the Blood Lion and the Blood Crow. After all, Luo Zheng and the others were being hunted down by all the outsiders in the Grand Pure Heaven. There was no place more dangerous than staying by their side.
However, there were more important things to do right now, so they couldn't immediately go and pay their respects to "Dong".
"Whoosh …"
More purple wind swept over.
In the middle of the Purple Wind Gorge, the purple wind had completely occupied the space.
It was unknown what method the Winged King used, but he could always find space in the purple wind. No matter how close the purple wind was to him, he couldn't be entangled.
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine was also trembling slightly in his claws. Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others, with their keen senses, could also sense that this "trembling" was actually a kind of strange movement technique. It contained the secret to avoid the purple wind.
Luo Zheng didn't care about these details. He stared at the purple wind and asked, "These purple winds don't usually exist. What kind of scene is it at the bottom of the Purple Wind Gorge?"
"It's a kind of shellfish," the Winged King replied. "This kind of shellfish is very weak. After taking root at the bottom of the Purple Wind Gorge, they can't move for the rest of their lives, so they lack the means to find food. Fortunately, these shellfish are creatures of the 33 Heavens, so they also have strange talents."
"Purple Wind is their Bloodline Talent?" Luo Zheng blurted out.
"That's right." The Winged King continued to answer, "The reason these shellfish released the purple wind is not to seal the Purple Wind Gorge, nor is it to target the strange races above. They are only hunting for food, and when they start to reproduce, they will release the purple wind to start hunting."
"Those pagans who fell down all became the food of these shellfish. It's really amazing..." Luo Zheng murmured.
As for how the Winged Monarch avoided the purple wind, Luo Zheng didn't ask.
When he first decided on the route, Luo Zheng knew that Yi Wang had entered Sanqing Heaven through the Spirit of Green Jade in his body. The Instrument of Civilization of Green Jade could sense Yi Wang's location.
The Green Jade Spirit told Luo Zheng with certainty that the Yi Emperor could easily bring everyone across the Purple Wind Stream Gorge.
Now it seemed that although there was a bit of trouble in the middle, the result was good.
"The path you chose is the Wailing Path?" The Wings King asked again.
At that time, the Wings King led many Immortal Realm cultivators of the Green Jade Civilization to advance toward Shangqing Heaven, but they were defeated four hundred miles away.
It wasn't that the Green Jade Civilization was weak, but that the path they chose was different.
At that time, the Wings King was eager to prove himself, so after entering Sanqing Heaven, he led his troops into Shangqing Heaven through the Path of Flames.
The Path of Flames was a very difficult path. At least, there were very few who chose this path to enter Shangqing Heaven. The Wings King had overestimated the strength of the Elemental Spirit Civilization, so he suffered a great setback.
In fact, if the Wings King wasn't so anxious, it wasn't impossible for him to enter Shangqing Heaven.
"Yes, Senior Wings King," Nuwa said with cupped hands.
"I heard that the Wailing Path is a strange path and is extremely dangerous. Are you confident?" the Wings King asked again.
Being defeated at the entrance of the Path of Flames was the pain in the Wings King's heart for the rest of his life. It also made him very sensitive to these paths.
"We successfully passed through it," Fuxi said proudly.
The Wings King nodded slightly, then said with some loneliness, "That's good. Back then, I failed …"
"Failure wasn't a matter of strength. After all, at that time, we didn't have a thorough understanding of these paths," Nuwa continued.
The Nirvana Realm creatures' understanding of Sanqing Heaven was always increasing, just like a mysterious veil being pulled off bit by bit. The latecomers became more familiar with these paths, so naturally, it would be easier.
"This time, I'm basking in your glory and entering Shangqing Heaven with you," the Wings King said again.
Nuwa's face revealed a trace of a smile. "Senior Wings King, you're too modest."
"Whoosh …"
As the Wings King's broad wings flapped fiercely, the group sped up in the purple wind again.
After a few minutes, the Wings King began to climb higher. After flying upward for a while, he finally made his way out of the purple wind.
On the left of the group was a cliff that stretched for thousands of miles, and on the right was a plain with no end in sight.
After the Wings King put down the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine, Luo Zheng began to look at the scenery in the distance. After a while, he revealed a strange expression.
"I'm standing in the north, facing the south." Luo Zheng pointed to the plain in front and said, "If I keep moving along this plain, won't I be able to reach the south of Taiqing Heaven?"
To put it more simply, if the center of Taiqing Heaven was this plain, then when they came out of the Blazing Maple Forest, they should go up along the Divine River and reach this place by crossing it. There was no need to spend so much effort to make a big circle.
"That's not a plain." Nuwa walked forward and said, "It's just an illusion, an illusion. The entrance is there."
Nuwa pointed not far away.
There were two rows of inconspicuous fences.
One on the left and one on the right, the two rows of fences built a very simple entrance.
After looking at these two rows of fences, Luo Zheng frowned slightly. Although the fences weren't eye-catching, Luo Zheng couldn't have ignored them. However, he didn't see them just now. This was very strange.
"Through the plain, the road to Shang Qing Heaven can only be through those two rows of fences." Nuwa stretched out a thin white finger and drew a line in the air. "If you don't walk into the middle of the fences, once you cross from both sides, you will be forever lost in this endless plain."
"This illusion is so powerful?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
At the same time that Nuwa nodded, the voice of Heavenly Lord Yuanshi sounded in Luo Zheng's mind, "This plain is not an illusion!"
"If it's not an illusion, we can just turn around and walk out. Why bother to be so powerful?" Luo Zheng asked back in his mind.
Heavenly Lord Yuanshi chuckled and said, "Those who enter it have no ability to turn back. All the people who enter the plain have their thoughts changed and no longer have the ability to 'turn back'."
"Turning back is also an ability?" Luo Zheng was speechless.
"Yes, it's not changing your memory, but directly making you not realize this ability of yours. You can only walk forward forever. You know what's wrong, but you'll never be able to detect it." Heavenly Lord Yuanshi shrugged and said, "This is indeed a very magical plain. It can logically restrict the thoughts of all creatures."

Chapter 3575
Luo Zheng didn't dwell too much on this issue. He went to the two rows of fences with the others.
These fences looked like they had been there for a long time. They had decayed thoroughly from the inside out.
The surface of the fences used to have exquisite carvings, but they had long decayed beyond recognition.
Luo Zheng noticed that there was a complete carving on the side of one of the fences, but part of it was covered by dust. Just as Luo Zheng was about to reach out and brush off the dust, Fuxi's expression changed. "Luo Zheng, don't touch this fence!"
Luo Zheng's fingers stopped an inch before touching the fence. "Why?"
"There's something wrong with this fence. Anyone who touches it won't be able to walk out of the Wailing Path," Fuxi said with certainty.
"Is it that magical?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
He didn't believe Fuxi, but anything could happen in the Nirvana Realm. Maybe this fence really had something to do with the Wailing Path.
In the end, Luo Zheng didn't touch these fences. At this time, the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine stretched out a thin vine and gently touched Luo Zheng's forehead, transmitting its thoughts to Luo Zheng. "I've sent you here. Can I go now?"
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine was forced here by Luo Zheng. Its life source was still in Luo Zheng's hands.
Luo Zheng glanced at the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine's big eye and said, "As far as I know, if you retreat from here, you can only walk on the Silent Plank Road. Do you think the other aliens will let you go?"
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine was also a member of the alien race. The problem was that in the eyes of the other aliens, the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine was already on Luo Zheng's side.
Even in normal times, it was difficult for the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine of the Goblin Clan to survive on its own after losing its arrow cluster. Moreover, the aliens were now in a rage. It was very unlikely that they would let the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine go.
The vine thought very seriously for a while and replied, "No …"
"Then what are you going to do?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"I … I'd better follow you …"
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine was very unwilling to follow Luo Zheng and the others.
The Wailing Path was originally very dangerous and full of strangeness.
Moreover, in its current state, it would be difficult for it to survive even if it reached the Shang Qing Heavens.
"If you're ready, please come with us. This Wailing Path is rumored to be very mysterious and terrifying. Presumably, besides Luo Zheng, everyone here should have heard of it, right?" Fuxi said in a clear voice.
Luo Zheng's eyes twitched and he glared at Fuxi.
"The Wailing Path is indeed dangerous, but once you master the secrets, the Wailing Path will be the easiest. This is why I chose the Wailing Path," Fuxi said as he turned and walked to the road between the two rows of fences. At the same time, he continued, "Everyone, please follow closely behind me. We will definitely pass through safely!"
"That's right, that's right …"
The Heaven's Eye Rockvine actually arched behind Fuxi at the first moment. Clearly, it was afraid of falling behind.
But once it moved over, there was no space for the others.
Fuxi looked at the Heaven's Eye Vine helplessly and said, "Do you want to enter my Spirit Beast Pouch?"
The space in Fuxi's Spirit Beast Pouch was not small. It was more than enough to store the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine.
However, most foreign races were not willing to enter the Spirit Beast Pouch unless the other party trusted them very much.
The Heaven's Eye Rock Vine may have been frightened by the horror of the Wailing Path. It quickly stretched out its thin vines to touch Fuxi's head, passing the thought of "consent" to Fuxi.
After releasing the Heavenly Hound of Trigram from the Spirit Beast Pouch, Fuxi put the Heaven's Eye Rock Vine inside. The Heavenly Hound of Trigram was a necessary item to pass through the Wailing Path.
Next, everyone walked into the road between the fences one after another. The Winged King's body was too large. In order to make it easier to enter the Wailing Path, he had shrunk his body before entering.
When everyone entered, the situation did not change.
In the distance, there was still the plain where there was no turning back. On one side was the cliff, and on the other side was the end of the Purple Wind Gorge.
As for the two rows of fences, they were like a row of wood on the plain, meaningless.
But after walking for a while, a faint white mist began to appear around them. The further they went, the thicker the white mist became.
Luo Zheng looked back again. The cliff was gone, and the Purple Wind Gorge had also disappeared. However, the two rows of fences were still there, tenaciously proving their position.
"Is the middle of the fences closed?" Luo Zheng asked in a low voice.
Originally, there were two rows of fences, but now there was only one row. It was like magic.
"Don't ask," Nuwa said in a low voice.
Her eyes were dyed with a hint of crimson light. It was as if she had poured Nuwa's shadow into her eyes. It was unknown what this action would make her see.
Since Nuwa told Luo Zheng not to ask, Luo Zheng did not ask anymore.
Donghuang, Elder Ying, and the three Immortal Hunters were also confused, but they obediently followed in front of Nuwa and Fuxi.
After walking for a short distance, a steepled house appeared in the thick fog.
After getting closer, Luo Zheng could see clearly that it was a straw hut made of straw.
The straw hut did not have a door, so they could not see the situation inside clearly.
However, when they got closer, everyone had an extremely unpleasant feeling in their hearts.
For Donghuang, Elder Ying, and the others who had been in high positions for a long time, this unpleasant feeling was enough to make them kill the other party.
However, in this mysterious place, they restrained themselves.
When they passed by the first straw hut, everyone could endure it.
But soon, a second and a third straw hut appeared in front of them …
These straw huts were all closed, and the creatures inside all gave them this feeling.
Driven by curiosity and uneasiness, Rat King finally could not help but ask, "What's inside?"
"Don't ask," Nuwa replied again.
Rat King's soybean eyes flashed, and he could only continue to move forward helplessly.
But when they passed by a row of neat straw huts again, Blood Wolf suddenly stopped. "You have to tell us what it is before we decide whether to move forward or not."
It was not that Blood Wolf did not trust Nuwa, but the Immortal Hunters were never willing to completely pin their hopes on others.
Knowing in advance was helpful for them to deal with unexpected situations.
Nuwa was a little regretful. She should have told them the rules outside, but now there was no chance.
"It's a kind of trick," Nuwa replied.
Hearing this answer, Blood Wolf just frowned. He had already guessed it. Now he was just forcing Nuwa to say it, so he was not very surprised.
However, the faces of Blood Crow and Blood Lion suddenly turned pale, and a trace of unconcealable fear was released from their eyes.
On the contrary, Donghuang, Elder Ying, Rat King, and Luo Zheng did not have any reaction.
Even in Shang Qing Heaven, there were not many creatures who knew about the existence of "trick". Of course, they had never heard of it in Shang Qing Heaven.
However, when they saw the faces of Blood Crow and Blood Lion, they immediately understood that it was not something easy to deal with.

Chapter 3576
When they passed by a row of neatly arranged straw sheds, Fuxi picked up the Celestial Hound by his feet.
The Celestial Hound was extremely sensitive. Now that it was on the Wailing Path, the extremely unpleasant feeling almost swallowed it up. It now looked sickly.
Every time Fuxi brought it past a straw shed, its listless eyes would reveal a hint of hostility.
Perhaps it understood that the thing in the shed was too strong, so it didn't dare to bark. It just whimpered softly.
After passing through the first row of straw sheds, the Celestial Hound only whimpered softly. There was nothing unusual about it.
When they reached the bottom of the straw shed, Fuxi asked in a low voice, "Is there not a single straw shed that can be entered?"
"No." The Celestial Hound shook its head.
Fuxi and Nuwa looked at each other quietly. There was a hint of worry in their eyes.
The last time they entered the Wailing Path, they found a straw shed in the first row that could be entered.
This time, their luck didn't seem to be so good. All 26 straw sheds in the first row were full.
"The second row!"
Fuxi knew that he couldn't delay any longer. He carried the Celestial Hound to the second row.
There were also many straw sheds in the second row. Just like before, the Celestial Hound sniffed hard when passing by the straw sheds, trying to find a place to settle down.
However, the Celestial Hound still couldn't find a straw shed in the second row.
Nuwa's big eyes flickered and she didn't say anything. Fuxi's face became very ugly. "The third row!"
The people behind him also remained silent, but the worry in their hearts grew deeper and deeper.
Fuxi and Nuwa had indeed gone to Shang Qing Sky from this path, but they had been able to pass through so smoothly before. There seemed to be a certain element of luck.
They were lucky once, but they couldn't be lucky every time.
If they couldn't find a suitable straw shed this time, the situation would be troublesome. It would be even more troublesome than when they were besieged on the seventh level of the Stairway Plateau.
There were seven rows of straw sheds in this area. It was like a large barbaric village.
Fuxi took the Celestial Hound from the first row to the last straw shed in the seventh row, but still couldn't find a suitable one. Fuxi's face seemed to be smeared with a layer of ash. It was extremely ugly.
Luo Zheng looked up and said, "The sky seems to have darkened a lot."
Although this area was shrouded in white fog and it was impossible to tell if it was morning or evening, it was a fact that the light outside the fog was dim.
Fu Xi was startled. He raised his head and looked over, feeling even more anxious.
Once the sky turned dark, he would be in deep trouble …
Seeing that Fuxi was like an ant in a hot pot, Nuwa calmed down a lot. "We can't find these straw sheds in the middle. There are still some scattered straw sheds around. We have to try. Huang Xi, don't be anxious."
In addition to the straw huts in this area, there were also quite a few scattered straw huts in the surroundings.
After Nuwa comforted him, Fuxi took a deep breath and nodded at her. Then, he carried the Celestial Hound and went to search the scattered grass huts.
After trying one straw hut after another, the answer he received was still no.
The sky was already turning dark, and night was about to fall.
"Since we're looking for a straw shed that we can stay in, we can just work together to pull Shift out. Why go through so much trouble?" Donghuang finally could not hold it in any longer.
From Donghuang's point of view, no matter how powerful Shift was, how powerful could he be?
Even without Luo Zheng, everyone present was still a force to be reckoned with in Sanqing Heaven.
But as soon as Donghuang said this, a low wail came from the nearest straw shed.
It was impossible to tell whether the wail was male or female. The voice seemed to contain a unique magic, and everyone could not help but cover their ears.
After the first wail came from the straw shed, more shrill wails came from the other straw sheds. One by one, Shift began to wake up!
"Woo woo …"
The Celestial Hound in Fuxi's arms kept whimpering. It was obviously terrified to the extreme.
"There are still a few straw sheds over there. We can still try." Nuwa pointed not far away, where there were still six or seven straw sheds standing.
At this moment, Fuxi had no more scruples. He strode over.
The Celestial Hound suppressed its fear and tried its best to distinguish. When Fuxi passed by the third straw shed on the left, the Celestial Hound's eyes lit up. "I've got it! This one is empty! Go in quickly! "
Luo Zheng, Donghuang, and the others behind them were also very anxious.
The Celestial Hound's words immediately cheered them up.
Fuxi lifted a corner of the straw shed and went in, followed by Nuwa, Luo Zheng, Donghuang, and the others.
The height of this straw shed was about the same as the shrunken Wing King, but it was still not easy to accommodate the Wing King. Seeing this, the Wing King could only shrink himself even smaller and then go in.
After the last Wing King entered the straw shed, Fuxi covered the corner of the straw shed.
The ear-piercing wail that had been lingering in everyone's ears suddenly weakened a lot, and the extremely uncomfortable feeling that lingered in everyone's hearts disappeared in an instant.
The interior of the straw shed was very simple, with only a bare wooden pillar.
This pillar was also quite old, and the surface had decayed into a fist-sized hole, as if it could break at any time.
"As expected of the Wailing Path. It gives people so much pressure without seeing anything," Rat King Sheng Tian said in a serious voice.
"If we really see something, we won't be able to talk here." Fuxi showed a helpless smile.
Elder Ying breathed a sigh of relief and asked, "How long do we have to stay on the Wailing Path before we can pass?"
"One day on the Wailing Path is 32 hours. We can stay for about three days," Nuwa replied.
Luo Zheng was still looking at the pillar, but when he heard this, he looked up and asked, "As long as we're safe for three days?"
"Yes." Nuwa nodded. "We'll stay here for three days and go back the way we came, then we'll naturally enter Shang Qing Sky."
"It turns out that the Wailing Path is so easy …" After hearing the rules of passing, Blood Lion looked very surprised.
The Immortal Hunters also had their own ways to go to Shang Qing Sky, and they even had more than one way.
But no matter which way they chose, they had to pay extraordinary courage and strength.
In contrast, the Wailing Path was too easy …
"It can't be that simple." Blood Crow shook his head. "Otherwise, so many alien races and Immortal Realm experts would choose this path, and this path wouldn't be called the strangest one. Don't forget that there are so many treacherous things outside."

Chapter 3577
The light in this village was already very dim, and it didn't take long for the sky to turn completely dark.
Originally, some light could be seen in the straw hut, but as the sky turned completely dark, the narrow straw hut also fell into darkness.
Fortunately, everyone's eyesight wasn't weak, and they could still see their surroundings clearly.
"Wuwuwu …"
After the sky turned completely dark, the howls outside grew louder and louder.
At this time, a ball of light appeared not far away. This ball of light was very bright, and even through the thick straw hut, everyone could see it clearly.

After the ball of light appeared, an ear-piercing scream was immediately heard. "Wuwuwu …"

Nuwa shook her head and smiled bitterly. "This is just the appetizer. These Shifts have a division of labor. Every night, they gather together and desperately howl. Even outside the Wailing Grounds, you can hear it."
The howls of Shifts were already so terrifying, and if all of them howled at the same time, it could drive people crazy.
This was no longer an ordinary howl, but a mental attack!
"How can we endure this?" Elder Ying said with a livid face.
"Just destroy your hearing," Fuxi said. "Everyone can use Divine Sense to communicate, and wait until the next morning to repair your ears."
Just as Fuxi said this, Elder Ying raised both hands and pierced her ears with two fingers.
With two streams of blood oozing out of her ears, Elder Ying's hearing had been destroyed. At the same time, her face began to show a comfortable expression, and the black and blue color quickly faded away.
Donghuang, Elder Gall, Sky Winning Rat King, Wing Monarch, and the three Immortal Hunters followed suit and destroyed their hearing.
Repairing a damaged sense of hearing was as easy as lifting a finger. Rather than saying that they were "destroying" their sense of hearing, it was better to say that they were temporarily "shutting off" their sense of hearing.
"Luo Zheng, can you still take it?" Nuwa glanced at Luo Zheng.
"It's still bearable." Luo Zheng nodded.

Donghuang and the others' sense of hearing had been destroyed. They could no longer hear anything, but they were fine with it.
However, Luo Zheng and the others were not used to such a quiet environment when the sound suddenly stopped.
Fortunately, this sudden stop didn't last for long. It only lasted for five or six breaths.
Through the straw hut, they could see that the white balls of light had gathered in groups of three or five. An even louder wail once again resounded through the sky.

After Fuxi's reminder, Luo Zheng nodded slightly and stretched out his fingers to poke his ears.
When two forces pierced his eardrums, one from the left and one from the right, there was a muffled rumble. Then, the whole world became quiet, and all the sounds had nothing to do with him.
However, at this time, Luo Zheng felt a cool sensation in his ears.
His sense of hearing was automatically restored in three breaths!
The extremely ear-piercing wails became louder and louder, quickly occupying Luo Zheng's mind. It was as if a chisel was drilling back and forth in his mind.
Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others were stunned when they saw Luo Zheng holding his head in pain.
"What's wrong, Luo Zheng?" Nuwa asked with her divine sense.
"My sense of hearing has been restored …" Luo Zheng endured the pain and replied with his divine sense.
"Restored …" Nuwa was also somewhat speechless.

"Wuwuwu —"
The Shifters outside the straw hut once again let out resounding cries. This time, the wails were ten times louder than before.
While Luo Zheng's mind was in a mess, he gently tapped his ears again with two fingers. He instantly lost his sense of hearing, and the whole world returned to silence.
But this time, the recovery was even faster …
The wails only paused for a moment before they resounded in Luo Zheng's ears again.
"Again!"
Luo Zheng stabbed his ears again.
But this time, he didn't even have time to feel pain before his ears were restored …
Everyone watched as Luo Zheng continuously stabbed himself with a baffled expression. They used their divine sense to ask Nuwa, and after they understood the general reason, they were also speechless. At the same time, they also looked at him with sympathy.
After continuously stabbing himself and restoring himself hundreds of times, Luo Zheng finally chose to give up and let those wails pour into his ears.
At this stage, the Origin Divine Blood couldn't replace him. Otherwise, it wouldn't have needed to use such a troublesome method. It must have other reasons for doing this …
Luo Zheng gritted his teeth and sat cross-legged with a serious expression.
Since he couldn't avoid it, he could only endure it.
Fuxi and Nuwa didn't know how powerful those Shifters' wails were. Every night, they destroyed their sense of hearing, so they hadn't really experienced it.
In less than an hour, the intense pain brought about by the wails was already unbearable for Luo Zheng. And this night, which lasted for twenty hours, was an unimaginable torment.
Everyone stared at Luo Zheng with worried expressions, and a trace of vigilance appeared on their faces.
If those Shifters discovered that there was an outsider here, they would undoubtedly die.
If Luo Zheng couldn't bear the torment and wanted to tear down the straw hut, they would have to act immediately to stop him.
But Luo Zheng's pain only lasted for about four hours. After four hours, the painful expression on his face gradually calmed down and was replaced by a suspicious expression.
Those wails changed and were replaced by a kind of low chanting sound!

Chapter 3578

The result was that the wailing lasted until the 20 hours until dawn.

Did something happen to the Shifters on the Wailing Path?

. "" No more wailing? "Nuwa frowned and stared at Luo Zheng." Maybe the sound you heard was different from ours? "

It turned out that these Shifters were not wailing, but communicating in Sanskrit that they couldn't understand!

After the Tianzhi civilization's Instrument of Civilization was ready, it began to decipher the chanting sounds.
At the same time, the Tianzhi core on the second floor of the Yu Sheng-tian system began to operate wildly …
This time, it took quite a long time to decipher. After a long time, the Tianzhi civilization's Instrument of Civilization transmitted a voice. "These sounds are indeed Sanskrit."

Come back quickly …
