That seemingly soft body was really like water. It strangely lost its shape, like a ball of sticky glue.
"Whoosh!"
Almost without any struggle, it was directly sucked into the black sun.
Then Blood Lion and Blood Crow separated their hands, and the black sun disappeared …
Such a terrible Shift was solved in a short time.
"That Shift was taken in?" Donghuang asked with his divine sense.
"It should be," Elder Ying nodded.
Wing King slowly retracted his spread wings.
When dealing with Shift, he would open up a space. If Shift let out a wailing sound to call its companions, he would use that space to block the wailing sound. Of course, whether it could really block the sound was another matter.
The spider threads once again formed Blood Wolf's body. He glanced at Blood Lion and Blood Wolf and said with a dignified expression, "You two, separate a little!"
In the past, the two brothers' black suns had never failed, but in the face of Luo Zheng, they finally had a chance to fail.
Although Shift was sucked in, it was better to be careful when dealing with something that was beyond common sense.
If Shift had the same ability as Luo Zheng and could force the separated black suns back together, the situation would become very bad.
Blood Crow and Blood Lion nodded and left in the opposite direction at the same time.
But before Blood Crow took a few steps, a white thing suddenly drilled out from behind him.
This sticky mass was none other than Eerie's body from before!
"Blood Crow!" Blood Wolf next to him pointed behind him and roared at the same time. In a hurry, Blood Wolf forgot that everyone present had damaged their hearing, and only Luo Zheng could hear it.
The Blood Crow didn't know what the Blood Wolf meant.
Behind him, the white mass gradually bulged and slowly extended to the top of the Blood Crow's head. Then, two hollow eyeballs appeared!
It was unknown what method Shift had used, but before the black sun closed, it had emerged from the space of the black sun!
"Up there! Above your head! "
Nuwa, who was relatively calm, transmitted the message to the Blood Crow.
Xue Ya raised his head to look, and his face instantly turned deathly pale.
This thing was actually attached to his back!
Eerie Shift was originally crawling slowly behind the Blood Crow like a snail, but when it realized that the Blood Crow had seen it, its head turned into a bolt of lightning and charged towards the Blood Crow's face!
"Swish swish swish …"
It actually went into the Blood Crow's eyes, nostrils, mouth, and ears.
In the blink of an eye, its huge body had completely entered the Blood Crow's body!
"Brother! No! "
The Blood Lion's eyes almost popped out of their sockets when he saw this.
With such a fast speed, not to mention others being unable to help, even if they wanted to help, it would be too late.
After a while, Xue Ya's pupils turned pale white, and he stood motionless on the spot.
Everyone present formed a circle and kept a certain distance from the Blood Crow.
Then, the Blood Crow suddenly opened its mouth to Luo Zheng, obviously wanting to make a sound!
"Buzz!"
Wisps of green light floated out of the Wing King's spread wings. These green lights formed a circular spatial passport that enveloped everyone present.
Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others looked around. Their only hope now was that the wails of the "Blood Crow" would not awaken the other Eerie Shifts.
After a while, there was no response from the straw huts …
"It's effective!"
Everyone's hearts relaxed.
Although they weren't sure if the space had really blocked out Shift's wails, it was a fact that no other Eerie Shifts had awakened.
However, the big trouble in front of them still hadn't been resolved!
Seeing Shift wailing at Luo Zheng, Nuwa transmitted her voice with her consciousness, "Luo Zheng, can you understand what it's saying?"
"I'm still deciphering it," Luo Zheng replied.
The space of the black sun couldn't trap Shift, and the situation suddenly became troublesome.
"Should we kill it?" Fuxi asked.
"No!"
Blood Lion immediately shook his head. At this moment, Blood Lion was the most desperate. Seeing his brother being possessed by Shift, he even wanted to die.
"Then what do you think we should do?" Fuxi asked.
Shift's strength was unfathomable. Even if they had the upper hand, they might not necessarily win.
If they really waited for Shift to make a move, everyone would only be more passive.
"I don't care. Whoever touches my brother is my enemy." Blood Lion stared coldly at Fuxi. He obviously wouldn't let anyone hurt Blood Crow.
"Blood Lion, calm down," Blood Wolf advised. "That's no longer Blood Crow!"
There were many monsters in Sanqing Heaven that could possess bodies.
Taiqing Heaven's Soul-Hooking Fire, Shang Qing Heaven's Blood Swamp's Soul Leeches, and …
Once these things possessed a body, the body would be declared dead.
Shift was much more terrifying than those things. Now that Shift had entered Blood Crow's body, how could Blood Crow possibly survive?
It was very likely that Blood Crow was now just a corpse!
"But, but …"
While Blood Lion stammered and didn't know what to do, Blood Crow, who had been standing in place and wailing, suddenly rushed toward Luo Zheng.
"Do it!"
Fuxi no longer hesitated. He stretched out his palm and suddenly pressed down.
An Eight Trigrams seal descended from Blood Crow's head.
Fuxi had used all his strength in this palm. He was determined to keep Blood Crow here.
Unexpectedly, Blood Crow just stretched out his hand and gently pointed upward. He didn't even see him touch the Eight Trigrams, but the Eight Trigrams had already shattered!
Fuxi's all-out palm was casually broken by Blood Crow.
"Swish, swish, swish …"
Nuwa's shadow also stretched out numerous tendrils and wrapped them around the Blood Crow.
Rat King Sheng Tian, Donghuang, and Elder Ying also used their unique skills.
In this small green space, the powerful energy released a terrifying pressure.
But Blood Crow, who was possessed by Shift, had an incredible ability. Blood Crow just stretched out his hand and pointed, and any energy that approached him dissipated with a loud bang. It couldn't hurt Blood Crow at all.
Luo Zheng saw Blood Crow coming straight for him. His eyes flashed with a fierce look. His body suddenly sank. Instead of retreating, he moved forward and punched Blood Crow's head.
"Da!"
Luo Zheng's body had been transformed by the Origin Divine Blood. How exaggerated was the power of this punch?
But Blood Crow just stretched out a finger and touched the surface of Luo Zheng's fist.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's power dissipated completely like an ox in a muddy sea …
"Retreat!"
Luo Zheng's pupils shrank slightly. He wanted to retreat.
But Blood Crow grabbed Luo Zheng's fist with a backhand. Blood Crow didn't seem to use much force, but Luo Zheng couldn't break free!
"Gurgle …"
The next moment, white things kept coming out of Blood Crow's eyes, nostrils, mouth, and ears. That was Shift's body!
Shift's empty eye sockets also appeared. He sized up Luo Zheng and revealed a strange smile. Soon after, he drilled into Luo Zheng's eyes, ears, mouth, and nose.

Chapter 3582
"Plop …"
Xue Ya, who had lost consciousness, fell to the ground without any support.
Luo Zheng hung his head and stood motionless. Xue Ya, who had been possessed by Treachery, also did not move. It seemed that Treachery was invading Luo Zheng's body.
"Brother!"
Xue Shi did not care about anything else. He went forward and dragged Xue Ya out.
The others' attention was not on Xue Ya. They were all staring at Luo Zheng.
Everyone present knew that Luo Zheng was the most important!
"What should we do?" Donghuang asked with a sullen face.
"We have to find a way to expel Treachery!" Rat King Sheng Tian said anxiously.
Nuwa's eyes narrowed. She took light steps and arrived in front of Luo Zheng. She opened her hands and a pink flower lantern appeared in her hands.
"Soul Seizing Flower Lantern!"
Everyone's eyes lit up when they saw the pink flower lantern.
The Soul Seizing Flower in the Soul Seizing Flower Lantern could forcibly draw souls out of the body.
Even a Holy Soul would find it difficult to resist.
Although the Soul Seizing Flower Lantern was powerful, it had a lot of limitations. It needed to be very close to be effective.
Naturally, there was no such opportunity in battle, so the Soul Seizing Flower Lantern could not be used in actual combat.
Luo Zheng was possessed by Treachery, so it was indeed suitable to use the Soul Seizing Flower Lantern …
But the biggest problem was whether Treachery was a kind of soul. Would the Soul Seizing Flower Lantern be effective?
Nuwa did not have much time to consider.
The pink Soul Seizing Flower in the Soul Seizing Flower Lantern began to flicker, and wisps of pink smoke floated out of the flower lantern.
"Whoosh!"
Nuwa blew a puff of air at the smoke.
The pink smoke blew towards Luo Zheng's face and entered Luo Zheng's eyes, ears, mouth, and nose.
Then, Nuwa paid attention to the Soul Seizing Flower Lantern.
The pink light in the flower lantern grew brighter and brighter, and the Soul Seizing Flower bloomed.
But when it bloomed to the extreme, the light suddenly dimmed, and the petals began to wither rapidly.
"Now!"
Nuwa grabbed the Soul Seizing Flower Lantern and pulled it back.
She pulled it with great force, as if it was connected to something in Luo Zheng's mind.
"Whoosh Whoosh Whoosh …"
Wisps of pink smoke came out of Luo Zheng's eyes, ears, mouth, and nose.
If everything went well, the pink smoke would pull Shift out as well.
But the result disappointed Nuwa …
Other than the smoke, nothing else was pulled out.
"Hu!"
Nvywa was unwilling to give up. She blew the pink smoke into Luo Zheng's eyes, ears, mouth, and nose. She wanted to try a second time.
After all, the pink smoke did "hook" something, but it just couldn't pull it out …
But this time, before Nuwa could pull him back, Luo Zheng suddenly opened his eyes.
His eyes had disappeared …
What replaced it was a pair of pure white eyes.
"Hu!"
Boom!
Luo Zheng casually threw a punch at Nuwa.
At such a close distance, Nuwa was completely defenseless. Naturally, she couldn't avoid Luo Zheng's punch.
Even though it was just a casual punch, the power contained within it was extremely terrifying.
Nuwa groaned and flew backward like a kite with a broken string. She didn't stop until she hit the circular space wall created by the Wing Monarch.
Luo Zheng opened his mouth and made the same action as the Blood Crow, as if he was wailing.
Then, he turned around and punched the green space wall.
"Whoosh!"
The Wing Monarch's space wall shattered.
"Whoosh …"
Then, Luo Zheng took a step and ran toward the exit of the village.
"What does he want to do?"
"This Shift doesn't want to wake up the other Shifts …"
"Is he going to leave the Land of Love?"
Everyone wanted to chase after him.
But on the one hand, Luo Zheng was astonishingly fast. On the other hand, Shift's strength far exceeded everyone's.
Previously, when Shift possessed the Blood Crow, it easily broke through many Immortal Realm supernatural powers. Now that it possessed Luo Zheng, its strength would only be stronger.
In just a few breaths, Luo Zheng had already rushed out of the two rows of fences on the Wailing Path and disappeared into the vast white fog.
Victorious Sky Rat King, Donghuang, and the others stood in place and stared blankly at Luo Zheng's disappearing figure for a while.
They couldn't accept such an unforeseen event …
"It's not the third day yet. If he rushed out like this, wouldn't he get lost in the wilderness?" Victorious Sky Rat King asked anxiously.
Fuxi hurried over to treat Nuwa's injuries. He looked back and said coldly, "In theory, that's the case, but there might be exceptions."
After all, the Wailing Path belonged to Shift. Perhaps Shift wouldn't get lost after leaving the Wailing Path.
But so what if it didn't get lost?
Luo Zheng was possessed by Deceit, so how could he possibly survive?
Nuwa was seriously injured. She lowered her pale face and her fine eyelashes kept trembling. Even with her wisdom, she couldn't think of any solution. Deep despair coiled around her heart.
Everyone stopped talking and the atmosphere became very gloomy. At this moment, a cough came from the side.
"Cough, cough. What's wrong with me …"
Just as everyone was unusually depressed, the Blood Crow lying in the Blood Lion's arms suddenly woke up.
"Brother, you're awake!" The Blood Lion's face was full of joy.
Everyone turned their heads in unison. Xue Ya also looked at the others with some surprise in his eyes. When Xue Ya recalled his previous experience, his expression suddenly changed. He touched his face and found that he was unscathed.
"Brother, you're fine. Shift came out of your body!" The Blood Lion quickly explained.
The Blood Lion thought that the Blood Crow was dead for sure. He never expected that Shift didn't devour his soul or destroy his body.
"The Blood Crow is fine …"
"If the Blood Crow is fine after being possessed by Shift, doesn't that mean Luo Zheng is also fine …"
"Luo Zheng still has a chance of surviving!"
After everyone saw the Blood Crow's physical condition, a glimmer of hope quietly ignited in their hearts.
At this moment, the silent Wing Monarch said, "I'm connected to the civilization tool of my Green Jade Civilization and can sense his location. When we get out of the Wailing Path, we'll go find Luo Zheng."
"That's how it should be," Fuxi nodded in agreement.
Although everyone felt that there wasn't much hope of finding Luo Zheng, there was still a glimmer of hope.
Now that there was one less Shift in the Wailing Path, even if the other Shift was really resurrected at night, there would be an empty straw shed here.
Several hours passed …
The Wailing Path fell into darkness again.
After Fuxi's treatment, Nuwa's injuries were mostly healed.
Everyone destroyed their hearing again and hid in the straw shed.
Through the straw shed, they saw Shift flickering with white light appear one after another.
Nuwa's eyes flashed, and she concentrated on counting the number of Shift.
She counted several times. There were 198 Shift monsters outside. She had lost two of them.
The one missing was the one that didn't resurrect and the one that was attached to Luo Zheng's body.
The night passed. When the sky began to brighten and the Shift returned to the straw shed, the number was still 198.
That Shift didn't resurrect tonight …
Donghuang, Fuxi, Rat King Sheng, and the others all looked regretful.
They shouldn't have chosen to take action.
Unfortunately, this kind of thing could only be known after experiencing it. The conclusion afterward was meaningless.
In this way, they only had one night left.
After this night, they would be able to leave and enter Shang Qing Sky tomorrow morning.

Chapter 3583
This was the first time Donghuang, Elder Ying, Rat King Sheng, and Yi Wang had set foot in Shang Qing Tian.
Especially Yi Wang, who had strength but had never entered Shang Qing Tian.
Logically speaking, they should be full of expectations.
But now that Luo Zheng had run away, everyone's hearts were very heavy, and naturally, they didn't have the mood.
Throughout the day, everyone was discussing the various possibilities after Luo Zheng left the Wailing Path, as well as how to find Luo Zheng.
Although discussing this matter in the village was somewhat of an armchair strategist, Luo Zheng's importance was self-evident.
Both Fuxi and Nuwa understood that the hope of the end now lay on Luo Zheng alone. If they could use their lives to exchange for Luo Zheng's, they would definitely not hesitate.
Twelve hours passed quickly.
The Wailing Path once again entered the night …
The Phantoms let out wails again.
On the third night, Nuwa was still counting the number of Phantoms.
At first, she counted one hundred and ninety-eight.
But after four hours, Nuwa suddenly found that a portion of the Phantoms were arranged in strange shapes on the Wailing Path.
They were circling in a circle!
Then, a little white light appeared in the circle!
Whether it was the Phantoms or the white light, the light emitted was very dazzling, like the sun in the dark night.
Through the thick straw hut, they could still see it clearly.
"What is that white light?"
"The white light is slowly growing bigger …"
After everyone saw the evolution of the white light, they immediately had a guess.
And when the growing white light became the size of the Phantoms, everyone was even more clear.
That Phantoms had been resurrected …
Luo Zheng's choice was right.
If they had a fluke mentality, they could have safely passed the second night.
But the third night was absolutely impossible!
With one less straw hut, the Phantoms would definitely discover the existence of this group of people and would definitely besiege them.
Facing two hundred Phantoms, they had no other choice but to die.
"Unfortunately, that Phantoms didn't come for me!" Elder Ying looked rather regretful.
She was willing to exchange her old life for Luo Zheng's safety.
This was not only for the sake of the End, but also for Ling Shuang.
Just as everyone was sighing, the Winged Monarch suddenly transmitted his divine consciousness. "These treacherous creatures don't seem to have any intelligence?"
Everyone looked at the Wings King strangely, not knowing why the Wings King had come to such a conclusion.
After all, Luo Zheng had already deciphered the conversation between the Shifters.
These Anomalies clearly had a mind of their own.
Moreover, they had even revived their companions. How could they not have intelligence?
"Why do you say that?" Nuwa raised her head and stared at the Winged King.
"There are two hundred Phantoms, and they don't know that one is missing," the Winged King said. "Moreover, when these Phantoms come across straw huts that they can't enter, they don't seem to enter the straw huts to verify. Instead, they directly choose other straw huts. This way of doing things is … very mechanical."
If it was a complete group, it would be impossible for the tribe to not react when Shift was missing. They would definitely try to search for it.
Previously, no one felt anything, but when the Wings King said this, everyone also felt that something was amiss.
"Now that you mention it, it's true," Fuxi said with a frown. "Every time a Shift comes to the straw hut, I gently suppress them and stop them from entering. None of them have ever entered the straw hut to investigate …"
"But how do you explain their revival of their companions?" The Blood Lion questioned.
"Perhaps reviving their companions is also their instinct?" The Wings King made a hypothesis.
"What about the Shift that attached itself to Luo Zheng? Doesn't it have intelligence? If it doesn't have intelligence, why did it run away? "The Blood Crow also asked.
Nuwa tilted her head and thought for a moment. "After that Shift discovered us, the best way was not to possess us and escape, but to directly wake up the other Phantoms and besiege us. This behavior is indeed very strange …"
In short, these Phantoms' behavior was indeed difficult to understand.
After safely passing the third night, everyone left the straw hut and once again walked to the entrance of the Wailing Path.
Two rows of fences still stood on the left and right, and in the distance, there was still rolling white fog.
"Let's go. Shang Qing Heaven is in front of us," Fuxi said confidently.
"Everyone, it's better for you to get on my back. I'm afraid that there will be some trouble guarding the entrance of the Wailing Path," the Wings King reminded them.
Although they had gotten rid of their pursuers in Tai Qing Heaven and the two Shadow people, the Shadow Imprint still remained on Nuwa and Donghuang.
This Shadow Imprint could last for five days, and during these five days, no method could remove it.
Once they entered Shang Qing Heaven through the Wailing Path, they would definitely be discovered.
"Wings King is right." Nuwa nodded.
Thus, everyone moved onto the Wings King's back again. The Wings King spread his wings and flew forward.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
After passing through the fence, the group of people traveled through the rolling white fog for a distance.
These two rows of fences were both the entrance and the exit.
One out and one in, they were two different worlds.
After the Wings King rushed out of the white fog, what appeared in front of him was a bumpy swamp.
Most swamps hid huge dangers, but the swamp in front of them was especially strange.
What was in those depressions was not ordinary water, but blood water.
"This is the Blood Swamp. Its area has expanded so much!" Nuwa looked surprised.
When the three Immortal Hunters saw the Blood Swamp, their expressions did not change much. Blood Wolf said, "The Blood Swamp begins to expand every 30 years, and after another 30 years, it will begin to shrink. Now is the expansion period, so it's normal for it to expand to the exit of the Wailing Path."
"It seems that the three of you have been to the Blood Swamp?" Fuxi asked.
Blood Wolf smiled with a hint of satisfaction. "Not only have we been here, we have hidden in the Blood Swamp for a long time, so we are very familiar with this swamp."
"If that's the case, please guide us." Nuwa cupped her hands at Blood Wolf.
"The most important thing is to find Luo Zheng," Blood Crow said.
The original purpose of the Immortal Hunters was to chase after Luo Zheng and the others.
The reason why they were willing to join this team was also because of Luo Zheng.
"Yes! Wings King said that he could sense the Weapon of Civilization. I wonder if he can sense Luo Zheng? "Nuwa asked.
"I'll try."
Wings King closed his eyes tightly, and a trace of green light slowly rose from the top of his head.
No matter how far away they were, he could communicate with the Weapon of Civilization. This was because the Weapon of Civilization of the Green Jade Civilization was special and was equivalent to a part of his body.
"Buzz …"
"Green Jade Spirit …" Wings King's voice came from the Weapon of Civilization.
"Wings King!" Green Jade Spirit responded immediately.
"Do you know where Luo Zheng is?" Wings King asked.
"After leaving the Wailing Path, he entered the Blood Swamp and ran all the way to the north. He hasn't stopped yet …" Green Jade Spirit's tone was a little anxious.
Obviously, Luo Zheng's current situation was also out of their control.

Chapter 3584
After Eerie entered Luo Zheng's body, Luo Zheng's body was no longer under his control.
Like flowing water, Eerie entered Luo Zheng's every meridian and bone, becoming a part of Luo Zheng's body.
Fortunately, Eerie didn't corrode Luo Zheng's soul …
He ran all the way out and stepped into the blood swamp.
Luo Zheng couldn't control his body. He couldn't do anything except watch "himself" run forward.
Splash … Splash … Splash …
Blood splashed in all directions in the Blood Swamp's depression.
While running, Luo Zheng could communicate with the world in his body.
"Senior Yuanshi Tianzun, what is this place? Why is there blood everywhere?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Blood swamp," Yuanshi Tianzun replied. "This place is very dangerous. If you're not careful, you might be buried here forever."
To Immortal Hunters familiar with the blood swamp, this place might not be so scary.
However, to strangers who accidentally entered the blood swamp, this place was hell.
A type of bright red leech was hidden in the blood swamp. This type of leech could perfectly merge with the blood swamp. It was difficult to detect it with Divine Perception.
Once it silently attached itself to a non-human or Immortal Hunter, it would enter the head and release poison to paralyze the target. Then, it would slowly devour the target's soul.
The scariest thing was that after the soul leech devoured the soul, it would temporarily occupy the host's body.
These soul leeches didn't have much ability to think. However, they would instinctively control the host and send them into the lizard's mouth.
The blood swamp also had a unique ecology.
The lizard was a kind of fat giant worm. It was also a top-notch killer lurking in the swamp.
Any living creature that passed by the lizard's nest would be dragged into the nest and devoured.
The lizard couldn't leave its nest. Its range of activity was limited. Apart from those "foreign" living creatures who didn't know about this, the native living creatures of the blood swamp would go around the lizard's nest. Therefore, the lizard needed the help of the soul leeches to hunt.
The Soul Leeches needed to suck the blood of the lizards to grow stronger. In order to obtain the lizards' rewards, they would constantly help the lizards hunt in exchange for the lizards' blood.
The blood in the blood swamp splashed everywhere. Naturally, a lot of it splashed onto Luo Zheng's body.
The Soul Leeches also silently attached themselves to his body and crawled toward his head.
Although these Soul Leeches were very small, Luo Zheng noticed them immediately.
"Senior Heavenly Primogenitor, there are many leeches crawling up here!" Luo Zheng said.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven was silent for a moment and then said, "It's the Soul Leech. These leeches can directly devour your soul!"
"With these little things?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
"Yes, their ability is very magical. They are often undetectable …" The Primeval Lord of Heaven cautioned.
"Just clean it up!"
Before the Honoured Lord of the Origin finished his words, Luo Zheng's soul had already drilled out of his mind to clean up the Soul Leeches on his body.
He had already stepped into the Sacred Soul Realm. The strength of his soul was much stronger than most Chaotic Origin Realm experts. Hence, it was not a problem for him to kill these tiny insects.
"Weng!"
Luo Zheng's soul released a powerful soul suppression. This soul suppression turned into many small shadowless swords that shot towards the Soul Leeches.
"Da da da …"
These small shadowless swords were not weak. The Soul Leeches on Luo Zheng's body were immediately smashed into pieces.
After cleaning up the Soul Leeches on his head, Luo Zheng proceeded to clean his back, arms, and chest.
But during the process of Luo Zheng cleaning up, the blood in the swamp was stepped on and splattered in all directions. A portion of the blood splashed onto the surface of Luo Zheng's soul. The leeches hidden within the blood actually started to crazily gnaw on Luo Zheng's soul.
Even with nine Sacred Soul Engravings, Luo Zheng's soul could not withstand the gnawing of the Soul Leeches!
Luo Zheng felt the pain. He could only shrink back into his head.
"These leeches are so powerful!" Luo Zheng said while enduring the pain.
Just a moment ago, Luo Zheng's soul had suffered a lot of damage. He felt that half of his soul had been paralyzed.
"I haven't finished speaking yet, and you've already started," the Honoured Lord of Heaven said helplessly. "Let Zhurong deal with these things …"
As soon as he finished speaking, the God of Fire, Zhurong, appeared above Luo Zheng's head.
Zhurong's soul was wrapped in a thick layer of flames. The flames spread from his head all the way down, enveloping Luo Zheng's entire body. From afar, Luo Zheng looked like a running man on fire.
The Soul Leeches on Luo Zheng's body were naturally burned to ashes by the flames. The blood that splashed onto him was also evaporated by the flames.
While cleaning up the Soul Leeches, Shift was still controlling Luo Zheng's body to run forward without stopping.
In fact, the Honoured Lord of Heaven and Luo Zheng had already discovered that Shift did not have its own consciousness.
The Tianzhi Civilization had deciphered the words that Shift had said when it was possessed. It was instinctively expressing regret and anger, wanting to break free from the shackles of the Wailing Path.
Now, Shift was running wildly, still driven by its instincts.
As for its destination, Luo Zheng had no time to sit down and figure it out with the Honoured Lord of Heaven. The most important thing now was to stay alive!
Zhurong's soul was just a wisp of soul. Soon, he was a little tired.
Therefore, when Zhurong returned to his internal world, it was Emperor Jun's turn to attack. When Emperor Jun's body was tired, it would be replaced …
The human heroes became Luo Zheng's bodyguards, protecting Luo Zheng's soul from being eroded by the Soul Leeches.
Just as the human heroes took turns to attack, Luo Zheng's body stepped into a large pool.
He tiptoed lightly on the surface of the pool and walked on the water. With Luo Zheng's running speed, it was a piece of cake for him to walk on the water.
However, as soon as he took a few steps, a huge head suddenly emerged from the water. It was a lizard. This pool was a lizard's nest!
"Swoosh!"
The gigantic lizard opened its mouth and swallowed Luo Zheng in one gulp!
This lizard was a Nirvana Realm creature in the Shang Qing Heaven. Its strength was naturally much stronger than the Nirvana Realm creatures in the Tai Qing Heaven.
The lizard had already sensed Luo Zheng's existence through the Soul Leeches. However, Luo Zheng could easily eliminate the Soul Leeches, which made the lizard very disappointed.
However, the lizard did not expect Luo Zheng to come straight to its nest. This was a delicious meal delivered to its mouth.
The lizard closed its mouth happily and was about to sink into the pool to slowly chew Luo Zheng into pieces.
All of a sudden, cracks as thick as an arm began to appear on the surface of its sturdy head.
"Crack crack crack …"
As the cracks expanded, the head made a loud noise.
The lizard seemed to be a little confused and also a little uneasy.
When it realized that the problem was with the "human" in its mouth, its huge head suddenly exploded!
Luo Zheng's body seemed to be fine as he ran forward with his legs wide open.

Chapter 3585
When the lizard died, the creatures hiding around the blood swamp were all stunned.
As the overlord of the blood swamp, the lizard was torn apart from the inside. How could they have imagined such an outcome?
But these creatures were only surprised for a moment before they swarmed into the lizard's lair.
The lizard was huge, and its flesh was extremely valuable.
After devouring it, it could strengthen the power of these creatures.
How could they miss such an opportunity?
As the blood in the deep pool surged crazily, the dead lizard's bones and flesh had already been completely devoured …
"Splash splash splash …"
Luo Zheng's body was still running.
"Ah, this Shift is very strong. I don't have to worry about its safety," Luo Zheng sighed.
Shift seemed to have some kind of magical power.
This power had been displayed on the Wailing Path. No matter if it was Fuxi or Nuwa's attacks, they were all easily resolved by it.
Now that Shift had attached itself to Luo Zheng's body, it also displayed this strange power. It had used this power to kill the lizard just now.
"When it leaves the blood swamp, we will be able to determine its direction," the Primeval Lord of Heaven responded.
Luo Zheng nodded. Now, they could only wait and see.
"One more thing," the Primeval Lord of Heaven continued, "why didn't the bloodline in your body launch a counterattack against Shift?"
Although the origin divine blood was basically not used by Luo Zheng, it was often useful in times of danger.
"Maybe the bloodline can't do anything to Shift …" Luo Zheng said hesitantly.
Without a doubt, the origin divine blood had its own stance. At least, it was unwilling to fall into the hands of the Wu Kong Clan and Yuan Ling Civilization.
But this time, when Shift invaded its body, the origin divine blood did not react at all.
"Impossible," the Primeval Lord of Heaven shook his head.
The origin divine blood flowed in every part of Luo Zheng's body and overlapped with the position of Shift's possession.
It was a miracle that the two did not clash.
"Then why?" Luo Zheng asked with a puzzled expression.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven pondered for a long time. He considered all possibilities and his eyes suddenly lit up. "Your bloodline doesn't reject Shift. Perhaps it originally belonged to Shift?"
"They belong to Shift …" Luo Zheng's eyelids twitched slightly. "Does Heavenly Primogenitor Yuanshi mean that Shift is not a creature in the Chaos, but also a creature in the Xuanliang World? Or perhaps they originally came from the Four Spirit Gates?"
"Exactly."
"But the Four Spirit Gates only have Snake Spirit, Bird Spirit, Human Spirit, and Monkey Spirit. There are no monsters like Shift," Luo Zheng questioned.
"The Phantoms themselves are not normal things. They come in different forms and wander aimlessly in the Three Pure Heavens all year round. They are a special kind of spiritual body, or rather, a special kind of … soul," said the Heavenly Lord of the Origin.
Heavenly Lord of Primordial Beginning had once done some research on Eerie. Like most factions, this research ended up with nothing.
Shift was too dangerous. Sometimes it was very docile, but other times it was extremely violent.
It was difficult for many factions in the Three Pure Heavens to come up with the means to restrain it, so they could only choose to stay away.
On the other hand, Shift came and went without a trace. No one knew how it came into being and how it disappeared. Even if they wanted to study it, it was impossible to talk about it.
Even so, many people had made some guesses about the origin of Shift.
As the place closest to the Xuanliang World, the Three Pure Heavens liked to speculate about things that they could not understand in the Xuanliang World.
Many people thought that Shift was the soul of Cha Na civilization. As for why these souls could not be communicated with and why they were unpredictable, no one could explain it.
"It is indeed possible. I just don't know where this Shift will go …" Luo Zheng sighed softly.
After the expansion of the Blood Swamp, it occupied a very large area. It was almost boundless.
Luo Zheng ran wildly, burning countless Soul Leeches along the way. He also accidentally entered the lizard's nest six or seven times and slaughtered the huge lizards.
After a day and a night …
North of the Blood Swamp.
There was obviously less blood here, and the temperature had become cold.
Soul Leeches liked warm places. Naturally, there was no such thing in the Blood Swamp. The lizards that needed the help of Soul Leeches to hunt naturally could not survive in this area.
However, they were replaced by an even more ferocious creature, the Great Swamp Vicious Prawn.
The Great Swamp Vicious Prawn had an astonishingly hard shell, and there were many of them. They often appeared in groups and ate their prey in an instant.
The most important thing was that the Great Swamp Vicious Prawn was not afraid of death. No matter how hard the iron plate was, they would bite down, even if they suffered heavy casualties.
However, this time, they had encountered an iron plate that they could not eat.
These bright red Great Swamp Vicious Prawns pounced on Luo Zheng in groups. While controlling Luo Zheng's physical body to run, Shift's two hands kept drawing circles like a wheel.
The movements looked strange, but the power of the circles was extremely terrifying.
Any Great Swamp Vicious Prawn that came close would be crushed into pieces. There were no exceptions.
This group of Great Swamp Vicious Prawns was the largest group in the Blood Swamp. There were forty to fifty thousand of them, and even the lizards were their prey.
However, after more than half a day of strangulation, the entire group was slaughtered to death!
Just when the Great Swamp Vicious Prawns were almost slaughtered, a group of black wolves that emitted a rotten smell surged up.
While Luo Zheng's physical body was fighting with these rotten black wolves, the voice of the Wing King came from the Green Jade Civilization's Weapon of Civilization. The Wing King and his group had left the Wailing Path and entered Shang Qing Heaven. Therefore, Luo Zheng decisively told the Wing King his position. After the Wing King confirmed Luo Zheng's position, he flew over the Blood Swamp and chased after Luo Zheng.
After Luo Zheng's physical body slaughtered the rotten black wolves, he finally walked out of the Blood Swamp and came to a bare hill.
After Shift controlled his physical body to stand on the hill, he seemed to be a little lost. He faced the north, west, and east in circles, as if he wanted to determine his direction.
"The west is the Soul-cleansing Mountain, the north is the Hundred Million Pagoda Mountain, and the east is the Cold Plain. I don't know which direction Shift wants to go," said Heavenly Lord Yuanshi.
"Where are you trapped?" asked Luo Zheng.
"The north," replied Heavenly Lord Yuanshi. "Behind the Hundred Million Pagoda Mountain …"
If not for Shift's control, Luo Zheng and his group would have successfully entered Shang Qing Heaven. Naturally, they would have gone to the back to rescue Heavenly Lord Yuanshi and the others.
But now, the first thing to do was to get out of Shift's control.
"Rumble!"
At this moment, the hill under Luo Zheng's feet suddenly shook. From the front of the hill, a fierce tortoise head suddenly emerged. The tortoise head turned and bit at Luo Zheng.
This "hill" turned out to be a big tortoise!
Before the big tortoise could bite Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng suddenly stomped his foot. The tortoise shell instantly cracked, and the tortoise head that turned to bite him exploded into a cloud of blood mist before it could get close to him.
Then, Shift controlled his physical body to float away. He finally chose a direction, which was the Hundred Million Pagoda Mountain in the north.

Chapter 3586
"Hundreds of millions of pagodas in the north. What a coincidence, it's on the way …" Luo Zheng smiled. The Heavenly Lord of the Beginning had just said that the heroes of the human race were trapped behind hundreds of millions of pagodas.
The Heavenly Lord of the Beginning was also surprised. "It's not that it's on the way. After all, Shift's goal can't be to save us, but it's not a coincidence that it's in this direction … He might want to do something!"
"What's that?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Enter the Clear Jade Heaven," the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning replied.
The Clear Jade Heaven was the core area of the Clear Jade Heaven, and also the most mysterious area.
Starting from the Elemental Spirit civilization, almost all civilizations wanted to enter the Clear Jade Heaven to explore, and the human race was no exception.
"The Clear Jade Heaven is in that direction?" Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
The Heavenly Lord of the Beginning smiled. "Why else do you think we're trapped there?"
The 36 heroes of the human race were trapped on the way to the Clear Jade Heaven.
How to get to the Clear Jade Heaven was an unsolved mystery for all civilizations.
After the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning led the human race into the Clear Jade Heaven, this was his primary goal.
The entrance to the Clear Jade Heaven was located in the center of the Clear Jade Heaven, and the entrance to the Clear Jade Heaven should also be located in the center of the Clear Jade Heaven.
Based on this simple speculation, the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning really figured out some key points, and finally opened a cave behind hundreds of millions of pagodas.
"In fact, until now, I'm not sure if the cave can really lead to the Clear Jade Heaven," the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning said with some resentment. "But ever since we found this cave, the Elemental Spirit civilization began to act recklessly."
At that time, it was already the 78th chaos era, and the 77th chaos era of the human race had already ended.
Under the leadership of the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning, the human race began to rise rapidly in the Nirvana Realm, and for a period of time, they were evenly matched with the Elemental Spirit civilization.
At that time, the Wu Kong Clan had not yet appeared, and the human race was the only existence that could compete with the Elemental Spirit civilization.
Although the Elemental Spirit civilization saw the human race as a thorn in their flesh, and had always been restricting the development of the human race, they had never fought to the death with the human race. After all, the human race was not the only enemy of the Elemental Spirit civilization..... 77 chaos era, there were many powerful dominator civilizations, and these dominator civilizations.
But when the Primeval Lord of Heaven led the human race to that underground cavern, the Elemental Spirit civilization finally could not tolerate it any longer. They wanted to eradicate the human race at all costs.
"The Eye Spirits gathered the strength of their entire race, but they only managed to trap us," the Primeval Lord of Heaven said with a hint of arrogance on his face.
"Since the Yuan Ling civilization values it so much, that underground cave must lead to Clear Jade Heaven," Luo Zheng speculated.
"Maybe, maybe not." Heavenly Lord Yuanshi shook his head. "There are many secrets in Sanqing Heaven. The Elemental Spirit civilization came first and arrived first. What could be destroyed has been destroyed, and what could be hidden has been hidden. No matter what, there must be something that the Elemental Spirit civilization is eager to hide."
While talking with the Heavenly Lord of the Origin, Luo Zheng was also looking around.
The Hundred Million Pagoda Mountain was an endless mountain range.
The mountains here were completely different from the usual mountains and rivers.
Every mountain looked like a sharp tower at first glance.
There was not much difference in height, and there was not much difference in shape.
When walking through it, it was like entering a forest formed by high mountains. It could be considered a veritable "mountain forest."
"By the way, although Shift seems to have no intelligence, it knows how to speak Sanskrit after all. Why don't we try to communicate with it?" Luo Zheng suggested.
The Heavenly Lord of the Origin had already formed a team in the Inner World. This team was well-trained, and they combined different pronunciations to form "Sanskrit."
After running all the way out of the Wailing Path, everyone was also tired of dealing with the trouble in the Blood Swamp.
The Hundred Million Pagoda Mountain looked much calmer, so it was worth a try.
The Heavenly Lord of the Origin originally had this intention, and now it was an opportunity.
So in the Inner World, the group of people who had already been trained was quickly gathered together by the Heavenly Lord of the Origin. They had been carefully prepared and cooperated with each other very skillfully.
They began to chant according to the instructions of the Tianzhi civilization, and the voice began to linger around Luo Zheng's body.
"Can you understand me?"
"Who are you?"
"Can you communicate with me?"
When the Sanskrit was sung, it became a very rhythmic song.
Shift had completely taken over Luo Zheng's body. Naturally, his eyes, ears, mouth, and nose were also the same, so he could definitely hear this song.
Luo Zheng and the Heavenly Lord of the Origin looked on expectantly, hoping that Shift would respond.
But Shift still controlled his body to run wildly, as if he did not have ears.
Although this result was not unexpected, the two still showed some disappointment on their faces.
"Let's try a few more times," Luo Zheng said.
So those people in the Inner World chanted again, repeatedly saying the same few words.
In the time it took to burn a stick of incense, the few words were repeated dozens of times, but Shift still did not respond at all.
"Forget it," Luo Zheng curled his lips.
It was one thing to have his body taken over by a monster, but this monster did not even have intelligence, which was really depressing.
When Luo Zheng chose to give up, Shift suddenly controlled Luo Zheng's body to turn left.
After Shift entered the Hundred Million Pagoda Mountain, he was already some distance away. At this time, he was actually going to the west of the Hundred Million Pagoda Mountain. This sudden change of direction puzzled Luo Zheng, but the Heavenly Lord of the Origin made a sound of surprise with a hint of expectation in his voice.
"Senior Heavenly Lord of the Origin, what's wrong?" Luo Zheng asked, puzzled.
"The underground cave behind the Hundred Million Pagoda Mountain is very hidden. We spent a third of a chaos era just to find a way to open it. The first step to open the underground cave is to go three thousand miles to the north and five thousand miles to the west, and then open a magic formation behind the largest pagoda mountain!" The Heavenly Lord of the Origin explained.
Because the Heavenly Lord of the Origin knew this secret, he had already paid attention when Luo Zheng's body entered the Hundred Million Pagoda Mountain.
But he did not expect that Shift would decisively go west after just three thousand miles!
This did not seem like a coincidence!
"Maybe Shift's goal is really the underground cave, and the underground cave is really the way to the Sanqing Heaven," Luo Zheng also showed a look of excitement.
If so, he might be able to kill two birds with one stone.
If he entered the underground cave and met the original bodies of the heroes of the Human Clan, they would be able to find a way to change Luo Zheng's current state!
"If that's really the case, it is indeed excellent," the Heavenly Lord of the Origin nodded.
The Hundred Million Pagoda Mountain was indeed a peaceful place, and they did not encounter any obstacles all the way to the west.
Not long after, a round pagoda mountain appeared in front of Luo Zheng's eyes.
This pagoda mountain was several times thicker than ordinary pagoda mountains, and the mountain was also much taller.
After Shift arrived at the bottom of the pagoda mountain, he stopped for a while and then climbed up the mountain's cliff.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng and the Heavenly Lord of the Origin became even more excited.

Chapter 3587
Shift controlled Luo Zheng's body and quickly climbed to the top of the Pagoda Mountain, then slid down from the back of the Pagoda Mountain.
"Whoosh …"
When Shift slid to the mountainside of the Pagoda Mountain, he suddenly stopped.
Yuanshi Tianzun's eyes were full of excitement, and his voice also became excited. "It's here, yes, it's here!"
"But there's nothing here …" Luo Zheng said doubtfully.
Before Luo Zheng could finish his sentence, he saw Shift control his body and slammed it toward the Pagoda Mountain.
"Bang!"
Although these Pagoda Mountains were made of stone, their surface had accumulated dust and turned into soil over time.
The thin layer of green soil on the surface of Shift's palm was directly shattered, revealing a huge red copper disc. The surface of the red copper disc was engraved with complicated patterns.
"Buzz …"
When the light shone on the red copper disc, the surface of the purple-red disc glowed, and a thick layer of light film covered the surface of the disc.
"What should I do next?" Luo Zheng asked in his mind. "Do I really have to knock down this Pagoda Mountain?"
"Of course," Yuanshi Tianzun said with a smile. "There are a total of four Pagoda Mountains that have been knocked down. It took a lot of effort to find these four Pagoda Mountains."
As he said that, Shift controlled Luo Zheng's body and slammed it hard against the red copper disc.
The light film on the surface of the red copper disc was extremely strong. Shift's casual slam had a force of one million Shen Jun, but the light film did not break.
"Boom!"
The collision between the body and the red copper disc produced a huge shock wave. The shock wave spread out and also hit the surrounding Pagoda Mountains.
The surface of these Pagoda Mountains was green because it was covered with a layer of green soil.
After the shock wave shattered the green soil on the surface of the Pagoda Mountains, the Pagoda Mountains' original appearance was exposed. They were actually tall Pagoda Mountains made of red copper!
"These Pagoda Mountains are all made of metal?" Looking at these Pagoda Mountains made of copper, Luo Zheng was also surprised.
Although nature's workmanship was uncanny, it was impossible to cast so many complete and smooth Pagoda Mountains.
"All Pagoda Mountains are like this." Yuanshi Tianzun nodded and said, "These Pagoda Mountains are not naturally formed, but man-made. The purpose and creator are unknown …"
Of course, anything that could not be explained in Three Pure Heaven would be inferred to the Instant civilization …
Shift's attack just now had knocked the huge Pagoda Mountain a little.
It would not be an easy task to take down such a huge metal pagoda mountain.
"Boom!"
"Boom!"
Following that, the second, third, and fourth collisions were launched …
With each collision, the Pagoda Mountain tilted a little.
When the mountain was tilted to a certain degree, Luo Zheng was surprised to find that there were two huge gears at the bottom of the mountain. The mountain was locked to the ground with teeth, and there were three gears buried at the bottom of the mountain, one big and two small. Two of the small gears were firmly locked to the gears of the mountain.
"How could there be these things under the billions of pagodas?" Luo Zheng was more and more shocked.
"It's very delicate, isn't it?" Yuanshi Tianzun smiled and said, "There are large and small gears buried in the vast Pagoda Mountain. These things are ingenious, but they are only used to open the door. For the Instant civilization, there are simpler and more intuitive ways to open the door, but they have to spend so much time and effort to make such a thankless thing."
Luo Zheng looked into the distance. Countless Pagoda Mountains stood in the distance, and the naked eye could not see the end. "Such a large area is carefully carved. This is simply …"
"They have nothing to do." Yuanshi Tianzun continued Luo Zheng's words, "They are like prisoners in prison, purely to pass time."
"Senior, you mean …" Luo Zheng's eyes lit up slightly.
"The Instant civilization is trapped here," Yuanshi Tianzun said.
Although Luo Zheng knew some of the truth, he was not as thorough as Yuanshi Tianzun. Every detail that the Human Tribe found in Three Pure Heaven was inferred and restored, hoping to dig out more information. In this respect, Yuanshi Tianzun was much better than Luo Zheng.
"Boom, boom, boom, boom …"
The huge Pagoda Mountain finally fell to the ground after being bombarded again and again. But after the Pagoda Mountain fell, a "Ka Ka Ka Ka" sound came from the inside of the Pagoda Mountain. It was the sound of huge machinery knocking.
Before Luo Zheng could ask questions, Yuanshi Tianzun explained, "There are huge reed parts at the bottom of the Pagoda Mountain. These reed parts will pry the Pagoda Mountain back little by little when they spring back. The whole process takes 32 hours."
If they wanted to enter the underground cave, they had to put down four Pagoda Mountains at the same time in the next 12 hours. If they exceeded that time, the first Pagoda Mountain would spring back completely.
"It's so precise." Luo Zheng sighed.
At this time, Shift observed the place for a while and then went north again.
"The second Pagoda Mountain is 3,000 miles north and 1,500 miles east," Yuanshi Tianzun said.
Just as the Primeval Lord of Heaven had said, Shift began to travel east after three thousand miles on its back, and found another pagoda mountain after one thousand five hundred miles.
The first Pagoda Mountain was incomparably huge. One could tell that it was extraordinary at a glance.
However, the second Pagoda Mountain was nothing special. It was definitely not an easy task to find it among the countless Pagoda Mountains.
However, Shift seemed to be able to sense it and climbed up the Pagoda Mountain naturally.
Just like the first Pagoda Mountain, Shift bombarded the back of the Pagoda Mountain and put it on the ground bit by bit.
After the second Pagoda Mountain was put down, Shift controlled Luo Zheng's body and headed northeast without stopping to find the third Pagoda Mountain.
The first three Pagoda Mountains went exceptionally smoothly.
The billions of Pagoda Mountains were very desolate. There were no outsiders, nor were there any ferocious creatures.
When Shift put down these Pagoda Mountains, the Winged King finally flew over the Blood Swamp.
With the help of the Winged King, everyone was not afraid of the Soul Leeches and Lizards in the Blood Swamp.
However, it did not dare to fly too high …
The three Immortal Hunters were very familiar with the Blood Swamp. There was a kind of evil bird living in the Blood Swamp. When it discovered a target in the air, it would often swarm out of its nest.
Therefore, the Winged King maintained a relatively low altitude and flew along the Blood Swamp.
Along the way, it encountered a few trouble, but it avoided all of them without any danger.
After leaving the Blood Swamp, the Winged King communicated with the Green Jade Spirit again through the Weapon of Civilization. After confirming Luo Zheng's location, it went straight to the Pagoda Mountains.
"These Pagoda Mountains should be the safest area in the Shangqing Heaven. We have explored them before, but they are only huge copper mountains. There doesn't seem to be anything special," Fuxi said.
Nuwa did not say anything. The last time she entered the Pagoda Mountains, she felt that this area was very unique. Her intuition told her that there must be something hidden in it. She even spent a lot of effort with Fuxi to break through a Pagoda Mountain, but they did not find anything unusual.

Chapter 3588
It was the first time Wing King had come to Shang Qing Heaven. He was just curious about everything here, so he didn't express his opinion.
Blood Wolf, who was the most knowledgeable among the Immortal Hunters, smiled faintly. "What's so magical about the billions of towers? That's not right! "
Fuxi frowned slightly. "What's so magical about them? Please enlighten me."
Blood Wolf waved his hand and pointed at the towers. "These towers are keys."
"Keys?" Nuwa blinked.
Blood Wolf nodded. "The so-called billions of towers are actually countless keys arranged together. If you turn the keys in order within a specified time, you can open different channels."
"For example …"
Nuwa was about to say something when Blood Wolf continued, "For example, the channel to Jade Clear Heaven is also controlled by these towers."
As soon as Blood Wolf said this, the eyes of Donghuang, Elder Ying, Wing King, and the others all lit up.
They really didn't expect these seemingly ordinary towers to be so mysterious that they controlled the way to Jade Clear Heaven.
"But how do these towers control those channels? Is there a magic array inside the towers? "Nuwa asked.
Fuxi continued, "We once shattered the green soil on the surface of a tower and found a circular magic array. But that magic array only formed a formation shield."
"That formation shield is for you to push down the towers." Blood Wolf shrugged. "The way to open the channel is to push down the corresponding towers."
"But there are so many towers here. How do you know which ones to push down?" Elder Ying asked.
The Blood Lion answered from the side. "This place is called the Hundred Billion Pagoda Mountains. It doesn't mean that there are hundreds of millions of Pagoda Mountains, but it means that there are hundreds of millions of ways to arrange the Pagoda Mountains. To obtain a corresponding arrangement of the Pagoda Mountains is indeed harder than ascending to heaven. So far, the few ways to open the passage should be in the hands of the Yuan Ling civilization and the Wu Kong Clan."
Although the Immortal Hunters knew the secret of the billions of towers, they didn't know how to open the channel.
"Now that Shift is constantly changing directions, does it mean that it has some way to open the channel?" Wing King suddenly interjected.
Blood Wolf raised his eyebrows when he heard this. He had been answering Fuxi and Nuwa's questions, but he had ignored the situation on Luo Zheng's side.
Shift didn't swallow Luo Zheng, but flew in a completely unconscious state among the billions of towers. Was it really planning to open a channel?
If that was the case …
"There really is such a possibility!" Blood Lion's eyes lit up with joy.
"Every channel opened by the Tower Mountain contains great value. Let's quickly catch up," urged the Blood Wolf.
"Sou!"
The wings of the Winged King suddenly flapped, and he turned into a green stream of light and sped away from the Tower Mountain …
"The fourth Pagoda Mountain. Five thousand Pagoda Mountains to the north and three thousand and one Pagoda Mountains to the west." The Honoured Lord of the Origin reported the range of the last Pagoda Mountain.
Just as Heavenly Lord Yuanshi had said, Shift headed northwest.
When Shift arrived at the three thousand and first Pagoda Mountain in the west, both Yuanshi Tianzun and Luo Zheng thought that it would stop and push the Pagoda Mountain to the ground.
Unexpectedly, Shift directly passed by the pagoda mountain and headed further west.
"Missed it?" Luo Zheng asked suspiciously.
"That's not right." The Primeval Lord of Heaven narrowed his eyes and shook his head. "How could I have missed it …"
"Could it be that the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi was mistaken?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Impossible," the Honoured Lord of the Origin said firmly.
"I said long ago that the underground cavern we opened may not be the channel to Clear Jade Heaven, and there may not be only one channel opened by the Tower Mountain, and the underground cavern may not necessarily be the channel to Clear Jade Heaven." The voice of Nuwa's true body suddenly sounded from Luo Zheng's Inner World.
Thirty-six heroes of the Human Race were trapped in the underground cavern for the 78th chaos era.
Compared to now, the chaos was still in its early stages.
Many sovereign civilizations' understanding of Clear Jade Heaven was far inferior to now.
The Heavenly Lord of the Origin led the Human Tribe to explore the Shang Qing Sky and killed a huge ice snake in the Cold Plains. He took out an ancient map from the body of the ice snake and found the way to open the underground cave from the ancient map.
"Perhaps your guess is right." The Honoured Lord of the Origin nodded.
In fact, in the thousands of chaos eras that followed, the Tower Mountain had opened as many as six channels. The methods to open these six channels were in the hands of the Yuan Ling Civilization and the Wu Kong Race.
The situation after each pagoda mountain was pushed down was different. Since Shift didn't push down the pagoda mountain as the Honoured Lord of the Origin said, then Shift probably didn't want to open the underground cavern.
The situation changed again, and Luo Zheng could only wait and observe.
After an incense stick's worth of time, Shift finally chose the fourth pagoda mountain and pushed it down.
To Luo Zheng's surprise, when the fourth pagoda mountain was pushed down, Shift also pushed down the pagoda mountain next to it, which was the fifth pagoda mountain!
After pushing down the fifth pagoda mountain, Shift continued to move through the pagoda mountains, heading towards the easternmost part of the Tower Mountain. Clearly, he was looking for the sixth pagoda mountain!
The easternmost part of the Tower Mountain was connected to the Cold Plains, and the temperature gradually dropped. The tops of these pagoda mountains were covered in a thick layer of snow.
In these pagoda mountains, a group of black-robed people were walking in a line.
These black-robed people were no different from normal humans, and their bodies were covered in specially made thick robes, as if they were extremely afraid of the sun. No matter if it was their hands, feet, or faces, nothing was exposed.
"Father, we've been searching for half a month, can we really find it?" A slightly immature voice came from the robe.
"We can definitely find it." Another calm voice came from the back of the robe. "I'm not worried that we can't find it, but whether we can subdue those strange birds after we find it. Sigh …"
"Don't worry, leader, although those strange birds are powerful, we're well prepared this time, we can definitely do it!"
"Yes, we're determined to succeed this time!"
"We definitely won't fail!"
Several of the leader's subordinates cheered.
"I hope so." The leader also nodded.
This time, their clan's secret operation was related to the survival of their clan.
They could only succeed, and not fail.
At this moment, a loud bird's cry suddenly came from afar.
"Yo —"
An eagle with six heads flew across the sky.
When they heard the screech of the six eagles, these people all stuck to one side of the pagoda mountain.
The thick robes on their bodies could change color on their own, and all of them were completely integrated into the mountain walls. Even if one looked carefully, it would be difficult to find any trace of them.
"Follow!"
The leader gave the order.
They stuck close to the pagoda mountains, and their hands and feet became extremely agile. In the blink of an eye, they turned into fierce beasts hunting for food, quickly moving through the pagoda mountains, chasing after the six eagles.

Chapter 3589
The six-headed eagle spread its wings and glided through the sky at a moderate pace.
In the Upper Azure Heavens, the six-headed eagle had almost no real opponents. At least, no one dared to offend it.
It had almost no natural enemies, so naturally, it didn't need to be too vigilant.
The six-headed eagle's nest was built at the top of a pagoda mountain. There were three sparkling and translucent bird eggs in the nest.
These sparkling bird eggs were extremely precious to the beings of the Upper Azure Heavens. The liquid contained in the eggs had unimaginable corrosive power. In theory, it could corrode everything, including space itself.
This corrosive power was originally the six-headed eagle's innate ability. Some of the other races hoped to borrow the six-headed eagle's power. They tried to subdue, lure, or even force the six-headed eagle to submit, but all of these methods failed.
As the most violent creature in the Upper Azure Heavens, any creature that dared to offend it would be corroded into a puff of smoke.
However, in order to move about in the Upper Azure Heavens, they had to rely on the six-headed eagle's corrosive power to break through space …
Since the six-headed eagle's power couldn't be borrowed, they could only target the six-headed eagle's eggs. The corrosive power contained in these eggs was even stronger than the six-headed eagle's body.
The six-headed eagle's intelligence wasn't high, and it didn't live in groups. This gave the other races an opportunity to take advantage of.
Those non-human beings in thick robes had come for the eggs of the six eagles.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
As these people traveled, the color of their robes constantly changed.
Along the way, the six-headed eagle didn't notice them.
After following them for four hundred miles, they finally saw a huge nest.
At the top of a pagoda mountain, thick green tree branches were piled up. These seemingly random green branches formed a regular bird's nest. From afar, the pagoda mountain looked like it was wearing a green circlet.
"Screech screech!"
The six-headed eagle returned to the nest and let out a long cry.
At this moment, six eagle heads poked out from the nest. It was another six-headed eagle.
The one returning to the nest was a male eagle, responsible for hunting outside. The one in the nest was a female eagle, responsible for incubating the eggs.
The male eagle released its claws and threw its prey down from the sky.
It was a gigantic turtle known as the Stone Turtle.
The stone turtle was one of the toughest creatures in Shang Qing Sky. Its shell had an amazing defensive power. The seemingly ordinary stone turtle shell contained a unique space law. Even if some powerful creatures captured it, they couldn't do anything to it.
"Whoosh …"
After the stone turtle fell, the female eagle used its five heads to support the stone turtle.
The six heads of the six-headed eagle were not independent. Only the largest one was the leader. If the leader was chopped off, the next one would take over.
The five eagle heads were like a pair of hands biting the stone turtle. The largest eagle head opened its mouth and gently spat at the stone turtle.
"Hua!"
A green liquid fell on the Stone Turtle's shell.
"Zi zi zi …"
The green saliva was terrifyingly corrosive. The stone turtle's almost indestructible shell was like a piece of paper under the green saliva, and a crack quickly appeared.
The Stone Turtle in the tortoise shell started to struggle crazily, trying to escape from the six eagles.
However, it had already fallen into the eagles' nest. There was no way it could escape.
The eagle head reached into the tortoise shell and started to peck at the Stone Turtle's flesh. The scene was very bloody.
After a short while, the stone turtle was eaten clean. The remaining shell was thrown down from the top of the mountain by the six eagles. With a crashing sound, it smashed a big hole in the ground.
At the bottom of the mountain, there were all kinds of corpses. They were the remains of the six eagles after eating.
The group of foreign tribesmen who were wrapped in thick robes were hiding at the bottom of the mountain. The sudden fall of the turtle shell gave them a fright. When the leader saw the traces of corrosion on the turtle shell, he couldn't help but shake his head and sigh in his heart.
The six eagles used up a lot of saliva to corrode the stone turtle, but they only needed one cup …
However, the six eagles were very bad-tempered. They would attack anyone who trespassed on their territory. If they wanted to get the saliva, they had to use their wits to get the eggs.
The leader raised his hands and secretly made a gesture.
At the bottom of a mountain far away, a black-robed man took out an iron cage from his Sumeru Space. Then, the black-robed man ran away from the iron cage.
After the iron cage was placed on the ground, it kept shaking. It seemed like something was trying to struggle out of the cage.
"Ka, Cha Cha, Hua …"
Soon, the thing in the iron cage crazily bumped and finally broke. A black bird that was as long as a man drilled out. This was a Black-Toothed Swallow. It had an extremely fast flying speed and was the favorite food of the six eagles.
"Swish —"
The Black-Toothed Swallow didn't know that its natural enemy was in the sky. It only wanted to return to the sky quickly. It didn't know that its natural enemy was in the sky.
The male eagle was squatting at the side of the nest, observing the surroundings.
Suddenly, a black shadow flew up. Its six eagle heads let out excited cries at the same time. The Black-Toothed Swallow was always at the top of the six-headed eagles' list of food. However, the Black-Toothed Swallow was too small and couldn't fill their stomachs. It wasn't worth it to hunt this kind of swallow from so far away …
It was very unnatural for the Black-Toothed Swallow to appear in the hundreds of millions of towers. However, the six-headed eagles didn't think about it. After all, there were two of them guarding the nest.
"Swish!"
"Swish!"
When the Black-Toothed Swallow saw its natural enemy, it was scared out of its wits. It used almost all of its strength to escape.
This Black-Toothed Swallow had been fed "Phoenix Blood" by the black-robed men. It could greatly increase its speed for a few hours. It was almost impossible for the six-headed eagles to catch up to the Black-Toothed Swallow in a short period of time.
The two birds, one big and one small, quickly flew into the distance.
When the leader of the black-robed men saw this, he waved his hand again. Not far away, another black-robed man placed another iron cage. Soon, a second Black-Toothed Swallow came out of the iron cage!
Under normal circumstances, the female eagle would never leave the nest.
However, the appearance of the Black-Toothed Swallow was obviously not a normal situation …
The female eagle in the nest also noticed the Black-Toothed Swallow that was quickly rising into the sky. It first let out a series of urgent cries. It was calling for the male eagle to come and catch the Black-Toothed Swallow.
However, the male eagle was chasing after the first Black-Toothed Swallow. How could it call for it?
The female eagle considered its situation. It felt that it wouldn't take too long to catch a Black-Toothed Swallow. Unexpectedly, it left the nest and chased after the Black-Toothed Swallow.
The second Black-Toothed Swallow was also fed with Phoenix Blood. It quickly flew away, leading the female eagle into the distance.

The black-robed men suddenly jumped and climbed toward the nest at the top of the tower mountain.

Chapter 3590

The members of the Elemental Spirit clan inside Jie Yan's body are also divided into strong and weak. The weak ones are just ordinary Elemental Spirit clan members, but there are also very strong existences among them, and Claire is one of them.
"I hope so," Claire said in Jie Yan's body.
Although Thunder Demon's performance is very passive, they don't think that Thunder Demon will lose, let alone die.
"Bang, bang, bang …"
After Luo Zheng smashed out more than twenty punches, the square stone hanging beside Thunder Demon suddenly dimmed, and a thin crack appeared on the surface. At the same time, the transparent ball also collapsed with a "pop" sound.
"Crack!"
Thunder Demon in the transparent ball has been preparing for a long time. He suddenly stretched out a hand and grabbed Luo Zheng's neck. "After so many years, you are the first person to break the shield. Then I'll show you my power …"
"Thunder Matrix!"
"Boom boom boom boom …"
At this moment, countless thunderbolts fell from the sky.
The thunderbolts all hit the top of the Tower Mountain, forming a circle of circular lightning at the top of the Tower Mountain.
There was a circle of circular lightning at the top of each Tower Mountain, and there were probably millions of circular lightning in these hundreds of millions of Tower Mountains.
These circular lightning bolts were connected to each other by lightning, forming a huge Thunder Matrix between them.

Chapter 3594
Lei Sha's right hand was tightly gripping Luo Zheng's neck. Naturally, those thunderbolts also gathered towards Luo Zheng's body.

Anything thrown into this liquid thunderbolt would be melted without a trace.
Under the invasion of the liquid thunderbolt, Luo Zheng's body also began to change. Round blisters grew on the surface of his body again, releasing a green slurry. Immediately after, Luo Zheng's body was covered in a layer of metallic luster from head to toe!

Lei Sha snorted coldly.
The energy in the entire thunderbolt matrix flowed even more intensely.
Within the converging liquid thunderbolts were tiny triangles. These triangles polished and drilled into the surface of Luo Zheng's body. Luo Zheng's metallic skin actually began to crack little by little. Fine cracks gradually appeared, and they gradually broke open.
"Even the Origin Divine Blood can't block it?" In his mind, Luo Zheng was shocked.
After all, from the moment he obtained the Origin Divine Blood, Luo Zheng saw the countless possibilities of the Origin Divine Blood, and each possibility was so powerful.

He simply couldn't figure out what this metallic luster on Luo Zheng's body was. His thunderbolts, which had always been successful, had almost no effect on Luo Zheng.
Now, in order to break open Luo Zheng's body, he had no choice but to use the Supreme Thunder Crystal Fragment …
This Supreme Thunder Crystal Fragment was the toughest thing in the world. After breaking it like this, it finally broke open Luo Zheng's body. This made Lei Sha breathe a sigh of relief.
Relying on the Supreme Thunder Crystal Fragment, he could grind Luo Zheng alive!
Just as the Supreme Thunder Crystal Fragment tore open Luo Zheng's body, a white thing suddenly drilled out from Luo Zheng's wound. That thing had empty eye sockets and a strange big mouth. After the empty eye sockets appeared, it still stared at him and sized him up.

At this moment, he finally understood where that unpleasant feeling came from.
Treachery!
This kid was possessed by treachery!
As one of the highest echelons of the Elemental Spirit civilization, Lei Sha's understanding of Eerie naturally far surpassed that of ordinary people. Naturally, he also understood that this thing was extremely terrifying.
But as soon as Shift appeared, two white hands wrapped around Thunder Fiend's body.
"Zizi …"
After these two hands wrapped around Lei Sha's body, they formed a layer of white membrane, tightly wrapping around Lei Sha.

The Supreme Thunder Crystal Fragment could destroy almost anything he wanted to destroy, but when it hit Shift's body, it had no effect at all.
Those tiny triangular fragments couldn't even scratch a single crack on the white film.
"Coo, coo."
Following the two strange noises, a pair of empty eye sockets appeared on the surface of the white membrane that enveloped the Thunder Fiend. At the same time, an upside-down mouth opened, and this upside-down mouth revealed a strange smile.

On the other side, Ah Huo and Jie Yan cried out at the same time when they saw Shift who had appeared out of nowhere.

Although Ah Huo teased Lei Sha with the attitude of watching a show, she believed that Lei Sha would not die at that time. Now that the situation had changed, she was a little worried.
"It's good that he can't survive it." Inside Jie Yan's body, Ah Ci laughed. Ah Ci really hoped that Lei Sha would die so that he could replace him.
The Elemental Spirit civilization had stayed in Sanqing Heaven for a long time, so their understanding of Shift was naturally deeper than that of ordinary Nirvana Realm creatures.
These Shift were all special spirits, and according to their strength and harm, they were divided into three levels: Chou Level, Resentment Level, and Hate Level.
Chou Level was the lowest level of Shift. These Shift generally gathered in a fixed position. They would act according to their own purposes and were not very aggressive, unless there was something that provoked them or aroused their interest.
Judging from the behavior of "Luo Zheng" just now, when Lei Sha first took the initiative to attack it, it only wanted to run away.
But after "Luo Zheng" lay on the transparent light ball, it was obviously interested in Lei Sha …
Now that it had attacked Thunder Fiend, it was very likely that there were two reasons.
"Coo …"
The white film formed by the Shift continued to shrink, and the weird face on the white film was deformed because of the contraction, looking even weirder.
Originally, Lei Sha was fifty feet tall, but this white film quickly shrank from fifty feet to twenty feet, then ten feet, five feet …
Lei Sha itself was a thunder spirit, but its form had been maintained for countless years. Now it was forcibly collapsed by the white film.
Even if Lei Sha's form collapsed, the white film still had no intention of giving up and continued to shrink.
Three feet …
One foot …
Five inches …
Three inches …
After a while, the white film condensed into a very small form. Of course, the face that appeared on the white film also disappeared.
When the white film shrank to about an inch in size, it actually appeared square.
Luo Zheng and Yuanshi Tianzun stared at this square and felt very strange. Could it be that the condensed Lei Sha had become a square?
Soon they saw the answer to the riddle.
Under the slow compression of the white film, a small square stone was squeezed out. There were some cracks on the surface of this small square stone.
The energy of Lei Sha's transparent light ball came from this small square stone. After being hit by Luo Zheng again and again, the energy in the stone was exhausted, and the transparent light ball shield naturally collapsed.
The white film had almost compressed everything to the point that it could no longer be seen. The only thing that remained was the small square stone.
"I know," Yuanshi Tianzun suddenly said, "Previously, when Shift controlled your body to lie on the transparent light ball, it was not looking at Lei Sha, but the square stone on Lei Sha's body. It took a fancy to this thing …"

Something that could arouse Shift's interest was certainly not ordinary.
"Coo, coo, coo …"
The remaining white film flowed like liquid water, and then slowly condensed into a shape, revealing the weird face again. The inverted mouth laughed even harder, and at the same time, it made a sound …
A wailing sound …
Shift's wailing sound would cause great harm to all living things.
The Winged King's group and the black-clothed man's group did not have time to destroy their hearing, and all of them immediately suffered intense pain.
Fortunately, the wailing sound only lasted for a short while.
The part of Luo Zheng's body outside of Shift slowly retracted into Luo Zheng's body. Of course, it also brought the small square stone into Luo Zheng's body.
Now that the body was not under Luo Zheng's control, Luo Zheng did not feel any pain when the square stone drilled into his body.

Chapter 3595
After stuffing the square stone into Luo Zheng's body, the last bit of white membrane left outside Luo Zheng's body suddenly opened.

Some things looked exactly the same, but their actual functions were completely different. Luo Zheng was only speaking his own guess.
No matter what, something that could interest Shift was definitely not ordinary.

The Wings King's group and the black-robed men followed behind them for several hundred kilometers.

It would not be easy to enter the Clear Jade Sky.

After Shift opened the channel, its movements became much more agile. It was very sure of the direction it was heading in and stepped into the cold plains without any hesitation.

In the seemingly lifeless cold plains, there were many kinds of Nirvana Realm creatures hidden.

Then, thousands of short-snouted evil sparrows emerged from the snow.
The white short-snouted evil sparrows were like a snowstorm, sweeping toward the prey above.
With the terrifying attack power of the short-snouted evil sparrows, an ordinary Nirvana Realm creature would be bitten to the bone in one or two breaths.
But this time, the situation was beyond the expectations of the short-snouted evil sparrows. All the short-snouted evil sparrows were split in half by a strange force when they were three feet away from their "prey."
It was the same for the first sparrow, and so was the ten thousandth sparrow …
"Puff puff puff …"
The short-snouted evil sparrows were swept up like a snowstorm, but now they turned into a rain of blood and fell on the snow of the cold plains, leaving a shocking trail of blood.
The Wing King followed behind unhurriedly. Seeing this scene, the Blood Wolf was also amazed. "Shift's power is indeed terrifying. The cold plains are more dangerous than the blood swamp. Following Luo Zheng saves a lot of trouble …"

Although the black-robed people did not show any hostility, they were still following them. No one could be at ease.

"Night Man Tribe?" Nuwa had never heard of this tribe.
The Blood Wolf's eyes flashed. "It's a tribe that lives in the dark domain. They look very similar to you humans, but they have always stayed in the dark domain. Their bodies are pale and colorless. They are also a powerful tribe."
Actually, there was no need for the Blood Wolf to describe them. One could judge their body size from their thick robes.

"The eggs of the Six Eagles are very important to us. Previously, dozens of us had died. Thanks to our benefactor, we got them," the leader of the black-robed people replied.

Chapter 3596
