Hearing the black-robed leader's serious words, everyone was slightly relieved. They were waiting to see when "Luo Zheng" would stop.
The Frigid Plains was extremely vast.
Except for the pure white snow, there were almost no other colors between heaven and earth.
Occasionally, there would be blood-red blooming in front of them. That was because Shift had killed another alien race creature on the Frigid Plains.

They couldn't see the end of the wasteland.

Shift controlled Luo Zheng to flash gently, and then he had already rushed towards the snow line and stepped into the wasteland!

Chapter 3597

"God bless …"

After Fuxi returned to the snow line, he entered the Wilderness and traveled back and forth between the two regions.

Not long after …
In the vast snowfield, two transparent light balls floated over.

During his silent breaths, the Elemental Spirit clansmen in his body had a fierce debate. The debate quickly concluded. Most people believed that Luo Zheng and the others were hiding in the snowfield. Then the Elemental Spirit clansmen in various forms drilled out of the "Holy Wing Armor".

Two gray lights flashed on the helmet of the Holy Wing Armor. Now the Holy Wing Armor was controlled by Claire alone.

Chapter 3598
The road in the Wasteland of No Return was not a straight line, but curved all the way to the end as far as the eye could see.

The others did not stop and followed closely behind.
Unexpectedly, the cave was very shallow. It was only ten feet away from the end.
Above the end of the cave, there were densely packed fish hooks hanging down.
These fish hooks shone with a light blue luster and were very conspicuous in the darkness.
Treacherous was obviously familiar with this. He jumped up and bit one of the fish hooks with his mouth.
Luo Zheng's whole body was suspended in the air, twisting the fish hook, like a fish that had bitten the hook.
While Luo Zheng, Yuanshi Tianzun, and the others in the cave were puzzled in their minds, a huge force came from above and dragged Luo Zheng into the darkness above the cave.
It was as if a fisherman had caught a fish …

Chapter 3599

Looking at this situation, everyone seemed to be in some kind of special "water". But this "water" didn't exist after all. It was just the situation above that made them look like it.

These bronze statues were very similar to the ones in the Eternal Holy Court, but the bronze statues beside the pool looked different.
Some had long hair, some had bald heads, and some were very old. Some were sitting and some were standing, but they were all very lifelike. These bronze statues were definitely not nameless people.
Yuanshi Tianzun thought for a while and agreed with Luo Zheng's speculation.
The furthest the 77th civilization had gone in the Three Pure Heavens was the underground kiln that they were trapped in.

Chapter 3600

Chapter 3601

Chapter 3602

"Kacha, kacha, kacha …"
"Help me …"
The black-robed man named Le 'er still had a trace of consciousness and made a weak cry for help. In addition to the cry for help, the sound of bones rejecting him also stimulated everyone's eardrums in this quiet environment.
The other black-robed men could not help but step forward to attack the big-headed monsters to save Le 'er.
"Stop!"
The leader of the black-robed men scolded.
"But Le 'er …"
A black-robed man said anxiously. Le 'er was his best brother. How could he watch him be eaten bite by bite?
"I said, don't go over," the leader of the black-robed men said firmly.
As the leader of the Night Man Clan, he had affection for everyone in the clan. Now, his clansmen were in the mouth of the monsters dozens of feet away, but he did not save them. It was not that he did not want to save them, but that he could not.
The other black-robed men fell silent.
Even though their expressions could not be seen through the thick robes, they could still feel the helplessness in their hearts.
"What is this?" Blood Wolf asked in a low voice. His hands had turned into thin spider webs, ready to react at any time. The Immortal Hunters were at most in Shang Qing Sky. They did not have a basic understanding of Freaks and Jade Sky.
"That is also a Freak. It is an enemy level Freak. It belongs to the species of slaughter," the leader of the black-robed men replied.
"Species of slaughter … What does that mean?" Blood Wolf asked again.
"Most Freaks have their own purposes. For example, the Freak that possessed Luo Zheng will only attack when its purpose is blocked. However, species of slaughter are not. Their purpose is to kill," the leader of the black-robed men explained in a low voice and continued, "So we have to leave here!"
"Let's go!"
Blood Wolf said one word.
The three Immortal Hunters hurried toward the gate.
Nuwa glanced at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng still looked like he had regained his consciousness. He looked at everyone with a calm gaze.
This Freak warned them with a wail and asked them to leave. Obviously, it was out of kindness.
With this kindness as a guarantee, it should not … hurt Luo Zheng, right?
"Take care."
Nuwa didn't care whether Luo Zheng could hear her or not. She turned around and left with Fuxi and the other Immortal Realm experts.
The two big-headed monsters were about ten feet away from the group. They were still squatting on the ground and slowly chewing on the two black-robed men. They didn't seem to have any intention of pouncing on them. They just stared at the group with their huge and ugly eyes.
As they walked past them, everyone felt a chill run down their spines, and their hair stood on end.
In addition to the treacherous feeling that made people very unhappy, there was also a trace of cruelty, a murderous aura that looked down on all life.
Everyone's eyes didn't linger on the big-headed monster. Instead, they stared at the door. After entering the door, they should be safe.
However, when they were still three to four miles away from the gate, more than ten huge heads emerged from the edge of the circular tray!
These big-headed monsters seemed to have discussed it beforehand. After climbing up from the edge of the circular tray, they began to gather in the middle.
"So many! Squeak, squeak, squeak! "The Heaven Victorious Rat King's bean-sized eyes were also wide open.
"Charge in!" Donghuang said in a low voice.
They couldn't even deal with a normal treacherous monster, but there were more than ten of them here. Moreover, according to the black-robed leader, these slaughtering monsters were far more dangerous than normal treacherous monsters.
What they didn't know was that there was a huge black head hanging from the bottom of the circular tray.
This black head was about ten thousand feet tall. Its eyes were tightly shut, and it seemed to be in a deep sleep.
On the neck of this black head, there were many big-headed monsters stuck together. They looked like a bunch of grapes.
These big-headed monsters were constantly falling off the neck of the black head. After falling off, they climbed along the bottom of the circular tray, all the way to the top of the circular tray.
Everyone had a bad feeling, and they all increased their speed.
These big-headed monsters looked dull. Their heads were big, and their bodies were small. They moved very slowly.
However, when they really moved, their speed was shockingly fast!
The black-robed men were at the very front. With their speed, they could enter the door in the blink of an eye.
When these black-robed men were about ten feet away from the door, the big-headed monsters suddenly turned into black shadows and flew past them silently.
"Dodge!" The leader of the black-robed men shouted.
However, it was too late.
Although the people behind stopped, they didn't see how the big-headed monsters attacked at all.
The four black-robed men who were still running disappeared …
"Crunch, crunch, crunch …"
By the time everyone reacted, the four big-headed monsters had stopped not far away and were slowly chewing on the black-robed men in their mouths. Blood kept flowing out of their mouths, accompanied by the black-robed men's painful moans.
Further away, the big-headed monsters were still crawling slowly.
These big-headed monsters had very strange habits. They weren't in a hurry to attack. If you didn't run away, they would just stare at you with their dark eyes.
"Leader, what should we do …" A black-robed man whimpered.
It wasn't that the Night Man Clan hadn't dealt with treacherous monsters before.
Their clan's unique ability could be used in the Tenebrous Realm. They could even deal with the powerful treacherous monsters.
However, outside the Tenebrous Realm, their strength was even weaker than ordinary Immortal Realm experts.
"Retreat," Blood Wolf suddenly said.
"I'm afraid there won't be a chance if we retreat. We can only risk it. Everyone …" Donghuang was speaking when Blood Wolf pointed at a spot not far away. Dozens of big-headed monsters climbed up from the edge of the circular tray and slowly approached the door.
If they rushed over in this situation, they would only be annihilated.
"Retreat," Fuxi said.
No one expected to be involved in such a dangerous situation in such a short time.
This place was much more dangerous than the Wailing Path!
"These big-headed monsters are very strange. Everyone, don't move too fast. Retreat slowly!" Nuwa suddenly reminded them.
"What's the use of retreating? We'll still die! "A black-robed man shouted emotionally.
"Ji Shen! Shut up! "The leader of the black-robed men scolded again.
"It's your fault for coming in and bringing us into this desperate situation! If you want to retreat, you can retreat by yourself. Forgive me for not accompanying you! "
After the black-robed man called Ji Shen finished speaking, he took out a yellow stone from his body and crushed it with a 'crack'. A yellow light burst out from the yellow stone and quickly gathered on his legs.
This was the God's Running Stone. It could greatly increase one's speed in a short time.
"Swoosh —"
Then, Ji Shen turned into a stream of light and went straight for the door.
Even though his movement speed had increased by more than three times, on the way to the door, there were seven big-headed monsters that surrounded him at an even faster speed.
"Crunch, crunch, crunch …"
The seven big-headed monsters formed a circle and made chewing sounds. Ji Shen was quickly eaten.

Chapter 3603
Seeing Ji Shen's miserable end, the remaining black-robed men did not dare to act recklessly.
As for Nuwa, Fuxi, and the three Immortal Hunters, they remained calm.
Nuwa took the opportunity to say, "The Shift that possessed Luo Zheng should know what's going on. Perhaps he can protect us."
Let Shift protect them …
The possibility of that was not high.
However, the Shift in Luo Zheng's body had indeed issued a kind warning.
Seeking his protection might be the only choice.
Although they did not have much hope, they still retreated step by step according to Nuwa's instructions.
"Your Excellency Wing Monarch, can you inform Luo Zheng of our situation?" Nuwa asked.
The Wing Monarch nodded and sent the information of these big-headed monsters to the Green Jade Spirit through the Tool of Civilization.
In Luo Zheng's inner world, the Heavenly Lord of Primordial Beginning gathered dozens of people together and prepared to perform a "chorus".
Since Shift had regained consciousness, there was a possibility of communication.
Luo Zheng's inner incarnation floated to the side and watched these people do the preparatory work.
After a period of practice, these people became more and more familiar with each other. They just needed to wait for the Heaven's Administration core to transmit the disassembled sound.
Previously, Luo Zheng had tried to communicate with Shift, but at that time, Shift did not have much self-awareness, so the communication failed.
At that moment, the Green Jade Spirit used greater teleportation to appear beside Luo Zheng and said with a serious expression, "Luo Zheng, the Wing Monarch and the others haven't left."
"They haven't left? Why haven't they left? "Luo Zheng's expression changed slightly.
"Many big-headed Shifts have appeared and blocked their path," the Green Jade Spirit conveyed.
Many Shifts …
The situation had become even more troublesome …
According to what was said before, this Shift called "Tianchuan" would leave his body after completing his obsession.
Now, it seemed that things were not so simple.
"This circular tray has probably not been visited for a long time. So many big-headed Shifts have appeared as soon as we arrived …" Luo Zheng muttered.
"It's definitely not a coincidence, and this Shift called Tianchuan obviously knows. Otherwise, he wouldn't have warned Nuwa and the others about how powerful they are," the Heavenly Lord of Primordial Beginning analyzed.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly, then he said to the Tool of Civilization of the Heaven's Administration civilization, "Translate my words into Sanskrit."
Not long after …
From Luo Zheng's Inner World came a chorus of voices, "Your Excellency Tian Chuan! Can you hear my voice? My surname is Luo and my name is Zheng. I am the owner of this body! "
Still holding her shiny silver spear, Shift stood motionless in her original position.
From the edge of the circular tray, monsters with large heads kept climbing up. These monsters were all crawling slowly, and some of them were even playing on the spot, rolling their huge heads around like balls.
Although their movements were slow, they gradually formed an encirclement.
However, Shift, who was controlling Luo Zheng, was not in a hurry. It seemed to be waiting for these big-headed monsters to appear. As it waited, it saw Nuwa, Fuxi, and the others returning.
Under Shift's control, Luo Zheng's body let out a low howl. "These idiots, can't they understand my words?"
It let the humans leave out of kindness.
After all, these people were related to Shift, who was also a human spirit. It could be said that they were the continuation of the race.
While Shift was puzzled and thinking, an unpleasant voice came from her Dantian. The voice was neither male nor female, as if it was a combination of many voices. "Senior Tian Chuan, can you hear me? I am the owner of this body. My name is Luo Zheng. "
Shift, who was called "Tian Chuan," was obviously stunned. A trace of surprise appeared in its calm eyes. This was the genuine Brahman Domain!
How could this guy speak Sanskrit?
Although the voice was very unpleasant, Shift could distinguish it clearly!
"You? How can you speak our language? "Tian Chuan asked.
"Senior Tian Chuan, you can find out if you check the Inner World," Luo Zheng replied.
Out of curiosity, Tian Chuan looked into the inner world of Luo Zheng's body and saw a group of people divided into three rows. Under the command of an orderly order, they spoke in a complicated voice, "I have communicated with the creatures outside the Chaos, so I am prepared."
"Many people together to form a whole. Interesting …" Tian Chuan was interested in this behavior.
The creatures in the Chaotic World were born with a lack of cognition and were trapped in the limitations of the world. It was impossible to communicate with the creatures in the Main World, and they couldn't have a complete understanding of the Main World.
But the guy it possessed actually relied on the advantage of numbers to overcome this limitation.
Shift had never seen such a thing …
If Shift's companions knew, their jaws would probably drop.
Tian Chuan's interest in Luo Zheng grew. He asked, "What's wrong? Do you want your body back? "
The Instrument of Civilization of the Tianzhi civilization transmitted this sentence to the core of the Tianzhi civilization for deciphering, and then to Luo Zheng. Although the core of the Tianzhi civilization was quite fast, it still needed some time.
After Luo Zheng and Yuanshi Tianzun heard Tian Chuan's tone, they looked at each other and smiled bitterly.
It seemed that it wouldn't be easy to get back the body.
But now, getting back the body was secondary. The key was to ensure the safety of Nuwa and the others.
"Senior Tian Chuan, we'll talk about it later. Seeing that we're all humans, can you protect my friends?" Luo Zheng asked.
When Luo Zheng said this, Nuwa and the others had slowly retreated to Luo Zheng's side.
Of course, those big-headed monsters also slowly followed them.
"I'm not a human," Tian Chuan said with a trace of arrogance on his face.
Human spirits were one of the noblest creatures in the Main World. They naturally had their own pride.
But Tian Chuan continued, "I can't protect them. Whether I can survive is a question mark!"
Luo Zheng and Yuanshi Tianzun were shocked. "Senior Tian Chuan isn't a match for those big-headed monsters?"
Hearing this question, Tian Chuan's face darkened. He continued to wail in a low voice, "Most likely not. At least in the past countless years, we weren't a match for them. Otherwise, we wouldn't have been trapped in that damned place …"
That damned place …
Although Tian Chuan didn't explain, Luo Zheng and Yuanshi Tianzun could tell that he was referring to the Wailing Path.
"We've tried many times. Even I've tried at least six times." Tian Chuan raised his right arm.
There were six bars engraved on his right arm armor.
Then Tian Chuan raised his spear and added a vertical line on his right arm armor with the tip.
This meant that he had come here for the seventh time.
Nuwa, Fuxi, and the others were quite puzzled when they saw this scene. They didn't know what this strange action meant.

Chapter 3604
After Tian Chuan carved that vertical line, he continued, "I have already failed six times. It is very likely that I will fail again this time."
Tian Chuan appeared to be very indifferent, but Luo Zheng and Yuanshi Tianzun suddenly became anxious.
Treacherous was immortal …
If he failed once, he would just return to the Wailing Path and be resurrected.
The others, including Luo Zheng himself, would probably be in trouble.
"If I can live, your friends should be able to live. If I fail, you can't blame me …" Tian Chuan finally said as he stretched out his silver spear and pointed it at Nuwa's feet.
"Da!"
"Hua …"
Silver energy flowed down like mercury. He dragged the spear and began to walk around Nuwa and the others.
When he finished a circle, a bright silver halo as thick as an arm surrounded them.
"Tell your friends not to walk out of the halo," Tian Chuan said to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng couldn't see the situation outside. After the Tianzhi civilization's Instrument of Civilization deciphered this sentence, Luo Zheng immediately asked Yi Wang if there was a silver halo outside.
After receiving a "yes" answer from the Winged King, Luo Zheng told the Winged King what he had heard from the sky. The Winged King also conveyed Luo Zheng's words to everyone, "Treacherous said that this silver halo can protect us!"
When everyone saw the large number of big-headed monsters, their faces turned green. After hearing Yi Wang's words, they were still a little surprised and doubtful.
Would Treacherous, who was so powerful that it was almost invincible, be afraid of a small silver halo?
But even if they had doubts in their hearts, they couldn't do anything about it. They could only stay in this circle.
When Treacherous finished drawing this circle, the slowly moving big-headed monsters were getting closer and closer. The nearest big-headed monster was only about ten feet away.
Luo Zheng let out a mournful wail. Then, he shook his long spear slightly and leaped into the air like a goshawk in flight.
His vertical speed was astonishing. He was like a silver waterfall floating in the sky in an instant.
Just as Luo Zheng's body rushed into the air, the slow-moving big-headed monsters jumped up one after another like beasts smelling blood. They turned into black shadows that were difficult to catch with the naked eye and pounced on Luo Zheng.
"Buzz —"
The long spear in Luo Zheng's hand rotated at a high speed like a silver millstone.
The big-headed monster that was the first to reach him opened its bloody mouth and tried to bite him.
"Heh …"
Luo Zheng opened his mouth to reveal a sarcastic smile. The silver spear tip drew a complete silver wheel in the air, streaking across the center of the big-headed monster.
The reason why Shift was so difficult to deal with was because it was difficult for ordinary methods to work on it.
Even if their bodies were twisted, squeezed, or even stretched into the shape of a noodle, they could still recover.
But the silver energy on Shift's armor had a miraculous effect on Shift!
"Boom!"
The silver light streaked across the large, ugly head like a sharp weapon streaking across a rubber ball.
The oval-shaped head of the big-headed monster was directly pierced by the tip of the spear, leaving a deep wound as it fell down vertically.
"Shua!"
Luo Zheng gently retracted his spear. The silver spear stabbed backwards from under his armpit.
Puff! Puff! Puff!
Puff! Puff! The silver spear instantly shot out four straight spear lights, like four silver dragons, piercing through the four big-headed monsters without deviation.
The spear lights that pierced through the big-headed monsters extended straight into the distance, shooting all the way out of the circular tray. Then, the spear lights strangely disappeared.
Inside the silver circle of light, everyone was originally on tenterhooks.
But when Shift controlled Luo Zheng and began to fight fiercely with the big-headed monsters, the big-headed monsters seemed to have locked onto Luo Zheng as their target.
There were five or six big-headed monsters slowly wandering around the silver circle of light, but these big-headed monsters were extremely afraid of the silver circle of light and refused to step into it. They kept looking up at Luo Zheng in the sky, ready to pounce on him at any time.
"This silver circle of light is so magical? The big-headed monsters don't dare to come in, squeak! "Victorious Sky Rat King said in surprise.
"It's not that they don't dare, they just hate this energy," Nuwa said.
"Hate also comes from fear," the black-robed leader said. "This silver energy obviously has a miraculous effect on Shift. It's very similar to the energy released by my Black River Gecko!"
The gecko raised by the black-robed leader had a similar effect, but the Black River Gecko was too small to fight against Shift.
One by one, the big-headed monsters turned into black shadows and pounced on Luo Zheng in the sky.
However, no matter how fast they were or how wide their mouths were, the silver spear lights would pierce through them or slash them. After being injured by the silver energy, these big-headed monsters could not recover. After struggling on the ground for a while, they died, motionless.
Occasionally, a few heavily injured big-headed monsters fell into the circle of light, but they were quickly cleared out of the circle of light by the Immortal Hunters.
"So strong!" Blood Lion exclaimed.
Blood Wolf asked with a puzzled expression, "They're both Shift Enemies, but why is there such a huge difference in strength?"
"Shift, which has possessed Luo Zheng, is not powerful. It relies on the silver energy on its spear and armor," Nuwa said solemnly. "If we can use that energy, killing Shift will be very easy!"
The others nodded slightly. If they could control the silver energy, they should also be able to deal with Shift.
In a short time, Luo Zheng had killed almost 30 to 40 big-headed monsters. The immortal big-headed monsters were like chickens and sheep in Luo Zheng's hands. Everyone's pessimistic mood was swept away!
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
Not far away, eight big-headed monsters crawled over and leaped into the air again.
Luo Zheng turned his spear around. His calm eyes suddenly focused, and the silver spear stabbed out like raindrops.
From below, it looked like a silver rain was falling …
The big-headed monsters were riddled with holes by the silver rain and fell from the sky.
As soon as he finished off these eight big-headed monsters, three more big-headed monsters pounced on him from behind.
The silver spear in Luo Zheng's hand seemed to have a soul. With a light flick, it stabbed three of the monsters behind him. If there were no accidents, he would be able to kill them easily.
However, at this moment, the silver light on Luo Zheng's armor and spear suddenly dimmed.
Pop! Pop!
The first two spears pierced through two of the big-headed monsters, but when he pierced through the third one, the silver light on his armor and spear completely disappeared.
The third spear was far less powerful than the previous ones, and the big-headed monster easily blocked it.
Luo Zheng's expression changed slightly when his spear failed to pierce through the big-headed monster. He changed his spear from a stab to a sweep.
Smack!
The big-headed monster was smashed to the ground by Luo Zheng.
However, without the support of the silver energy, Luo Zheng's spear was unable to cause any substantial damage to Shift. The big-headed monster had just landed on the ground when it bounced back up and pounced on Luo Zheng once more.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng's body suddenly sank, landing next to Nuwa and the others. His spear was already in the middle of the silver circle.
Whoosh! Whoosh! Whoosh!
The spear was like a long snake, absorbing all the silver energy in the circle!
The tip of the spear flashed with silver light once more. At the same time, the big-headed monster was only a few feet away from Luo Zheng. It opened its mouth, its sharp teeth crisscrossing, and was about to swallow Luo Zheng.
Boom!
An Eight Trigrams seal exploded on the big-headed monster's face, blasting it several feet away!
It was Fuxi who had attacked.
Under Shift's control, Luo Zheng turned to look at Fuxi. He nodded slightly, and his spear easily strangled the big-headed monster.

Chapter 3605
Most of the big-headed monsters on the circular tray had been cleaned up, but there were still three or four wandering aimlessly in the distance.
Luo Zheng rushed over with his spear …
After a short while, several silver spear lights flashed in the distance, and the remaining big-headed monsters were killed.
Luo Zheng strode over with his spear in hand. He glanced at the crowd indifferently.
He tilted his head and opened his mouth.
"Gululu …"
Shift's body flowed out of Luo Zheng's eyes, ears, mouth, and nose, converging into the shape of a person.
Previously, this Shift was just an abstract human figure, especially its face, which was extremely strange. Now, it was very different. Every detail of its body was outlined, and its appearance was quite handsome. It was exactly the same as the bronze statue beside the pool.
Luo Zheng closed his eyes for a while, and when he opened them again, they seemed to be blooming with starlight. The corners of his mouth curled up, and he smiled at everyone.
"Luo Zheng!"
"Are you okay?"
"That's great …"
After dealing with these big-headed monsters, they were still thinking about how to communicate with Shift and have him leave Luo Zheng's body.
They didn't expect Shift to leave Luo Zheng's body directly.
"Don't worry, there's no problem. This senior Tianchuan is from our clan. How could he hurt me?" Luo Zheng smiled.
"His name is Tianchuan?" Nuwa asked.
"Where did he come from?" Fuxi also asked.
"Why did he possess you … squeak!" Victorious Sky Rat King said.
There were countless questions hidden in their hearts.
Hearing their questions, Luo Zheng smiled. Facing Slyness, a chorus of voices came from his body. "Senior Tianchuan, there are many things that I don't understand. Can I ask you for advice?"
Tianchuan glanced indifferently at Luo Zheng. "I've already returned your body to you. You can leave."
After saying this, he pointed lightly at Luo Zheng's body. The armor attached to Luo Zheng's body peeled off, and the spear was pulled out of Luo Zheng's hand.
Tianchuan had completely awakened. This was the first time he had succeeded in countless years.
Two hundred people were trapped on the Wailing Path. They kept going back and forth, and anyone who passed by would be possessed by them. No matter how difficult it was, they had to come here …
Today, he finally succeeded. Tian Chuan was actually very excited in his heart, but he didn't reveal any of his emotions.
"Senior Tianchuan is from the Human Spirit Sect, right? Our Human Race is also a kind of continuation of the Human Race. If there's anything we can help Senior Tianchuan with, please don't hesitate to tell us. There's no need to keep us at a distance, "Luo Zheng said patiently.
With Luo Zheng's personality, he didn't want to force answers from others, but he also knew that it wasn't easy to meet a Human Spirit. This was a once in a lifetime opportunity, so how could he be willing to leave so casually?
Tianchuan glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently. His facial expression changed slightly, and there was a hint of sarcasm in his eyes. "Your physical body is very good, but your biggest role is to help me come into contact with the Light of Annihilation, kill those treacherous things, and awaken me. As for other roles …" He shook his head as he spoke. "You can leave. These things have nothing to do with you."
The Light of Annihilation mentioned by Tianchuan was the silver energy attached to the armor and the long spear.
The King of Annihilation was very good at restraining treacherous things, but Tianchuan was also a treacherous thing now. Naturally, he couldn't come into contact with it directly, so he needed to attach himself to a physical body.
When Tianchuan chose a physical body, he would subconsciously choose a more powerful existence.
Now that Tianchuan had awakened, Luo Zheng's physical body naturally lost its function.
In his eyes, Luo Zheng, Nuwa, and Fuxi, who were in the Immortal Realm, were from the Secondary World. Whether it was strength or natural limitations, they weren't qualified to participate.
"In any case, I helped Senior Tianchuan awaken. I don't ask for a reward. I just want to know something," Luo Zheng continued to say shamelessly.
Although Tianchuan put himself on a high pedestal, he wasn't difficult to talk to. He seemed to spread his hands helplessly. "Ask away."
"Senior, is this Jade Clear Sky?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Jade Clear Sky …" Tianchuan nodded. "Yes, but this is just a small Clear Sky Platform. There are many Clear Sky Platforms in Jade Clear Sky. The real main Clear Sky Platform is in that direction. It's a hundred thousand times bigger than the Clear Sky Platform under our feet. Strictly speaking, that's the real Jade Clear Sky."
Tianchuan stretched out his hand and pointed into the distance.
Luo Zheng looked in the direction of Tianchuan's hand. There was a dense starlight in the distance. He couldn't see clearly at all.
Senior Tianchuan's words were similar to Nuwa's previous guess. This was a branch of Jade Clear Sky.
"Senior, are you from the Instant Civilization?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Instant Civilization?" Tianchuan obviously couldn't understand this term.
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng explained, "The first civilization to exist in the entire Chaos should be the civilization that built this Chaos."
With this question, Tianchuan understood. He smiled and said, "According to your understanding of the Instant Civilization, it should be referring to us, or rather, the two hundred of us. Because the Chaotic World that you speak of was built by us."
"You really built the Chaos World!"
Although Luo Zheng had such a guess in his heart, he didn't expect Tianchuan to directly admit it.
"Naturally," Tianchuan showed a hint of sadness. "The 113th Subworld BDING was also the last Subworld we built. But it ended here."
Perhaps because Luo Zheng asked the right question, Tianchuan explained his origin one by one.
These two hundred Strange Creatures were the builders of the Human Spirit Sect.
They had built one Subworld after another.
Without stopping, without rest, without interruption …
They had never thought that the struggle within the Four Spirit Sects would sweep over them.
After building the Chaos World, they planned to restart the 113th Subworld BDING. Next, the 113th Subworld BDING would operate on its own.
But the day before they planned to restart, traces of invisible poison drifted out of the Clear Sky Platform. Without knowing it, the two hundred people were all poisoned to death.
After the creatures in the Main World died in the Subworld, they would transform into Strange Creatures. They would never die, never be extinguished …
These Strange Creatures lived muddle-headedly, wandering according to their remaining consciousness.
But they all had their own obsessions in their hearts. Once their obsessions were completed, the Strange Creatures would awaken and become awakened species.
This Clear Sky Platform was the place where the two hundred builders of the Human Spirit Sect lived. Their obsessions that were trapped in the Wailing Path were to return to this place. They had a complete "awakening" and completely recovered their self-consciousness.
After regaining their self-consciousness, these builders wanted to investigate what happened in the past.
Why were they, the builders, erased in an instant?
Who was the one who erased them?
Unfortunately, these builders didn't have the chance to go out at all. They were chewed up and swallowed by those big-headed monsters.
Soon after, they were reborn in the Wailing Path.
But this time, they finally succeeded.

Chapter 3606
After listening to Tianchuan's words, Luo Zheng's eyes showed surprise.
From Tianchuan's tone, it seemed that he didn't know much about the Four Spirits Sects …
"Senior Tianchuan, what do you plan to do now?" Luo Zheng asked tentatively.
Tianchuan raised his head and glanced at the dense starry sky. "Since I've awakened, I naturally have to go back and find out the reason. Who poisoned us? Why did they stop us from awakening again and again? "
The builders had a high status in the Four Spirits Sects.
After all, the Four Spirits Sects relied on their hard work to expand their territory.
Poisoning the builders was a great crime in the Four Spirits Sects. Ordinary people didn't have the guts to do so.
Tianchuan looked at Luo Zheng's face after saying this.
He thought that Luo Zheng should have a look of sudden realization. He didn't expect Luo Zheng to look worried.
"What's wrong? Did I say something wrong? "Tianchuan asked.
"Senior Tianchuan, after you were poisoned, did you come into contact with people from other 'main worlds'?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Of course not. Not only did I not, but my companions also didn't." Tianchuan shook his head. Every time he came here with his obsession, he would be killed by those big-headed monsters. He couldn't even fully awaken.
"Senior Tianchuan, you probably don't know the current situation of the Four Spirits Sects …" Luo Zheng said with a strange expression.
Tianchuan saw Luo Zheng's expression and felt a little confused. "What situation?"
"If we want to enter Sanqing Heaven, we need to enter the Immortal Picture Scroll to reach the Immortal Realm," Luo Zheng said.
Tianchuan showed a matter-of-fact expression. "I built the passage. Of course I know."
"I entered the center of the Immortal Picture Scroll," Luo Zheng continued.
Tianchuan raised his eyebrows slightly. "Your talent is not bad. You can inherit the origin bloodline. No wonder your physical strength isn't weak."
The creatures in these secondary worlds who could obtain the origin bloodline were already very outstanding.
Of course, Tian Chuan was a member of the Four Spirit Gates, and he had been chosen as the creator. He did not think that a mere origin bloodline was unattainable. After all, the talented members of the Four Spirit Gates had all set their sights on the Origin Divine Blood.
"No, I joined the Eternal Divine Court," Luo Zheng denied.
Hearing this, Tianchuan's pupils shrank slightly.
It was rare for a creature from a subworld to successfully enter the Eternal Divine Court.
Once one obtained the acknowledgement of the Origin God's Blood, one would be qualified to become a member of the Four Spirit Gates. Moreover, this kind of person's talent was too outstanding. So, the speed of their growth in the Four Spirit Gates was also very fast. Their future was bound to be limitless.
Normally, the "Manor of Shifang" in the Spiritual Sect would immediately rush over to take him in. Why was this kid still in the Three Pure Heavens?
Could it be that he failed?
That's impossible …
The Four Spirit Gates were very strict in their assessment of this world. Only the most promising geniuses were allowed to enter the Eternal Church.
In other words, even though it was difficult for the Four Spirit Gates to enter the Eternal Divine Court, it was much more relaxed. Thus, many people were eliminated by the Origin Divine Blood.
However, it was extremely rare for a creature from a subworld to be allowed into the Eternal Divine Court. These people had a high chance of passing the Origin Divine Blood test.
"Were you invited?" Tian Chuan suddenly asked.
"Invited?" Luo Zheng shook his head. "I went in by myself …"
Tian Chuan jumped in fright. "You went in by yourself? How is that possible? Are you joking? "
The Eternal Divine Court was the center of the Four Spirit Gates …
How could they let Luo Zheng go in by himself …
"There's no one else there," Luo Zheng said. "The Eternal Divine Court has already been abandoned. The bronze statue inside told me so."
Tian Chuan stood in place and looked at Luo Zheng in astonishment. He didn't say anything for a long time.
"You're lying," Tian Chuan said in a very certain tone. "The Four Spirit Gates would never give up on the Eternal Divine Court!"
Luo Zheng nodded. "I believe that the Four Spirit Gates wouldn't be willing to give it up. However, what if something happened to the Four Spirit Gates and they were slaughtered?"
"Hahaha …" Tian Chuan laughed loudly, as if he had heard the funniest joke in the world.
The Four Spirit Gates were the only overlords in the Origin World. They had no opponents.
Although there were still some creatures and races in the Origin World, even the strongest existence among them was like an ant in front of the Four Spirit Gates.
"Who can slaughter the Four Spirit Gates?" Tian Chuan glanced at Luo Zheng in a rather aggressive manner.
However, Luo Zheng said with a calm expression, "The Four Spirit Gates are indeed the most powerful existence. However, the Human Spirits, Snake Spirits, Bird Spirits and Monkey Spirits shouldn't be as strong as an iron plate, right? Wouldn't there be internal strife and internal strife? "
"As you said, the Four Spirit Gates are so powerful. Who would dare to make a move against the world builders?"
"I've seen the administrator. He also told me that the Four Spirit Gates were indeed divided …"
"…"
Luo Zheng said a series of words to the Tianzhi Civilization in his Inner World. After the Tianzhi Civilization was broken, it was transmitted to the people in the chorus. Everyone also did their best to sing.
Luo Zheng's words convinced Tian Chuan.
After all, what he said was reasonable …
Who would dare to make a move against the world builders? Who would be able to poison them?
Other than the people in the Four Spirit Gates, it was impossible for the outside world to do it. It wasn't that they didn't want to, but it was impossible for them to poison 200 world builders without anyone knowing.
Because Luo Zheng's voice was transmitted directly to the Inner World, Nuwa, Fuxi, Donghuang, the Immortal Hunters and the black-robed people naturally couldn't understand. After all, some information wasn't suitable to be leaked.
They only heard waves of singing coming from Luo Zheng's Inner World. The treacherous person called "Tian Chuan" seemed to have lost his soul. Naturally, he was very curious.
Tian Chuan was dazed for a while. Then, his eyes suddenly widened. "No, I have to go there personally!"
Although what Luo Zheng said seemed to be true, there was a high possibility that it was true.
However, this matter was of great importance. He had to confirm it personally.
"Whoosh …"
Tian Xing's figure floated lightly. He stomped his feet on another part of the Clear Sky Platform.
A series of patterns flashed on the ground again …
One by one, strangely shaped houses floated up from the ground.
The house at the front was the shortest, only the size of a door, barely big enough for one person to pass through. The houses at the back increased in size, each of them twice as big as the one at the front. The fifth house was the last one, and it was already a colossus.
"Senior, where are we going?" Luo Zheng asked seriously.
"As the builders, we naturally have a path to the Main World. You don't have to follow me. As creatures of the Secondary World, you can't move a single step in the Main World before you undergo modification." As Tian Xing spoke, he pushed open the door. Just as he stepped in …
"Crack … Boom!"
A huge force blasted out from the door.
Tian Xing almost reflexively grabbed both sides of the door. After his body took the hit, he was directly blasted ten miles away. His hands and feet were also pulled ten miles away.
Fortunately, Tian Xing had a treacherous body. If he was alive, he probably wouldn't have been able to take the hit.

Chapter 3607
Because Tianchuan's body was stretched too long, it took a while for him to shrink back into his human form.
He returned to the door with a very ugly expression on his face.
The exit of this path had been blown to smithereens. Countless cracks extended from the front to the back. The entire passage had been destroyed.
"Was this a secret move arranged long ago?" Tianchuan asked coldly.
This secret path to the main world was only known by the Four Spirit Sects and even some people from the Human Spirit Sects.
To be able to tamper with this secret path, it meant that there must be a traitor in the Four Spirit Sects, and even the traitor came from the Human Spirit Sects.
This proved Luo Zheng's words once again.
"This place is so close to the Xuanliang World. Senior Tianchuan, can you leave directly from above?" Luo Zheng pointed to the sky and asked.
Looking up from Yu Qingtian, the Xuanliang World was so close that it seemed to be within reach.
Tianchuan glared at Luo Zheng. "Unless you want to die, don't take this path."
The force field hovering above Yu Qingtian was set up by Tianchuan and the others. He naturally knew its power.
"In that case, I'm afraid Senior Tianchuan can only go to the Path to Heaven," Luo Zheng suggested.
"I know that," Tianchuan replied.
The destroyed path on the Clear Sky Platform was a secret path deliberately left by the builders. The Path to Heaven was the right path from the Chaotic World to the Xuanliang World. Now that the secret path couldn't be taken, of course he had to take the right path.
"But as far as I know, the Path to Heaven is controlled by the Elemental Spirit Civilization. They probably won't allow Senior Tianchuan to pass through casually," Luo Zheng reminded him.
"Elemental Spirit Civilization? What's that? "Tianchuan asked.
No matter what the situation was, as long as the creatures in the Chaotic World were mentioned, Tianchuan's expression would become somewhat indifferent or condescending.
No matter how powerful the creatures in the Chaotic World were, it was difficult for them to enter Tianchuan's eyes.
But Luo Zheng in front of him was an exception. After all, Luo Zheng went to the Eternal Court and accepted the test of the Origin Divine Blood.
"It's the most powerful civilization in the Nirvana World. It's also the first civilization after Senior Tianchuan created the Chaotic World. We can't defeat them now, and they definitely won't allow Senior Tianchuan to pass through casually," Luo Zheng said.
"I can't win?" Tianchuan also asked.
Luo Zheng glanced at Senior Tian Chuan, and spoke without any confidence, "I am not sure, after all, Senior Tian Chuan killed Lei Sha without awakening."
"Lei Sha, he's a member of the Yuan Ling civilization?"
After Tian Chuan had awakened, he still retained his memories from before he had awakened. Naturally, he remembered how he had killed Lei Sha.
In Tianchuan's eyes, that Thunder Fiend was also considered to have some strength. It was just that he had died an unjust death. He had been forcibly shrunk to the point of collapse.
"Yes, it is said to be one of the three pillars of the Elemental Spirit civilization." Luo Zheng nodded.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Tianchuan felt relieved. "After all, it's a secondary world. It's not worth mentioning!"
Although his main body had already fallen, the Treacherous Body itself was a powerful weapon. Naturally, he would not take the creatures of the subworlds seriously.
But at this moment, a burst of strange laughter came from the Clear Sky Platform.
It was as if dozens of people were laughing. As the laughter echoed, a terrifying aura came from under everyone's feet.
This laughter was obviously a Sanskrit …
Tianchuan obviously understood this voice, and his expression became very ugly.
"Tianzhi civilization, help me decipher it!" Luo Zheng's expression suddenly changed, and he transmitted this laughter into the world inside his body.
Soon, the core of the Tianzhi civilization also translated the content of the laughter to Luo Zheng. "The subworlds are not worth mentioning … A mere world creator, a piece of trash that even Shi Fang Prefecture did not accept, dares to speak arrogantly now, hahaha …"
At the bottom of the Clear Sky Platform, the huge black head suddenly opened its eyes. Its mouth opened, and waves of wild laughter kept coming out.
While it was laughing, it also slowly moved along the bottom of the Clear Sky Platform and floated at the edge of the platform.
It was a black head that was thousands of feet tall. There was no body under this huge black head, but oval spheres. These spheres were the previous big-headed monsters.
After the big-headed monsters were killed by Tianchuan, they would gradually disappear and "resurrect" in another place. All Treacherous Bodies were like this.
But what they did not expect was that the place where the big-headed monsters resurrected was actually under the Clear Sky Platform!
These big-headed monsters gathered at the bottom of the black head like a bunch of grapes, making the black head even more terrifying.
When Nuwa, Fuxi, Donghuang, and Elder Ying saw this huge monster, the color on their faces instantly disappeared, and they looked as if they were facing a great enemy.
"Resentment level! Resentment level Treacherous Body! "the black-robed leader said.
Treacherous Bodies were divided into three levels.
However, no one had ever seen the most powerful Resentment level Treacherous Body.
Resentment level Treacherous Body was generally regarded as the most powerful Treacherous Body, and it was almost invincible.
The three Immortal Hunters' expressions were also extremely cold. Judging from the aura released by this black head, all the troubles they had experienced before were not worth mentioning.
"Who are you?" Tianchuan looked up and asked.
Treacherous Bodies were definitely formed after the death of living beings in the Main Realm.
The more powerful the living being was, the stronger the Treacherous Body formed after its death.
Resentment level Treacherous Bodies were almighty beings when they were alive …
The black head let out a series of rhythmic laughter again, and it said in Sanskrit, "You don't need to know who I am. You only need to know that the place you should be at is the Wailing Path!"
"Buzz!"
This black head's eyes began to flicker with red light. When the red light accumulated to a certain extent, it suddenly shot out from its eyes.
"Dodge!" Luo Zheng warned.
Everyone saw two red lights sweeping over from one side of the Clear Sky Platform, and they all dodged to the sides.
"Swish!"
When the red lights swept over, Nuwa, Fuxi, Luo Zheng, and the Immortal Hunters had already dodged to the sides.
Tianchuan's speed was not slow either.
Only one black-robed man was a step slower. He was swept by one of the red lights, and he stood rooted to the ground.
"Hua!"
That black-robed man's body suddenly collapsed into a pile of black ashes.
Seeing this scene, everyone's cheeks trembled uncontrollably. How could they not panic if they could not dodge the red lights?
As Tianchuan dodged the red lights, he reached out his hand and grabbed the air.
The armor scattered on the ground, and the long spear vibrated, and it went straight to Luo Zheng.
"Ka ka ka ka ka …"
In half a breath's time, all the armor was completely attached to Luo Zheng's body.
"Kill him!" Tianchuan said to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng held the long spear in his hand, and at the same time, he turned around and stabbed fiercely into the distance.
The white light hidden at the tip of the spear shot out, and the black head was directly penetrated by the "Light of Destruction" without any resistance.
After being penetrated by the white spear light, the black head only had a small hole, which was like a mosquito bite to the head that was more than ten thousand feet in size.
However, the Light of Destruction on the long spear in Luo Zheng's hand completely dimmed.

Chapter 3608
Luo Zheng held the dim spear and looked at Tian Chuan.
Tian Chuan had just killed so many Foe Level Shifters by relying on the Light of Destruction.
Now that the Light of Destruction was exhausted, this spear might be of extraordinary quality, but in front of the Shifters, it was just a piece of scrap metal.
After looking at the spear tip, everyone also looked at Tian Chuan.
"That stone," Tian Chuan said.
"What?" Luo Zheng looked surprised.
He didn't explain to Luo Zheng and pointed lightly at Luo Zheng's chest.
A small lump protruded from Luo Zheng's ribs. This lump moved along his body, quickly swam to his throat, and then into his mouth. Then Luo Zheng spat it out. It was a square stone with cracks on its surface.
When this square stone moved in Luo Zheng's body, Luo Zheng had already remembered that it was taken from Lei Sha.
At that time, Tian Chuan had not awakened. He just said that this stone was called the Holy Stone, and then forcibly stored it in Luo Zheng's body.
Luo Zheng and Yuanshi Tianzun couldn't figure it out. Lei Sha relied on this Holy Stone to open the circular shield, but after the shield was smashed, the energy contained in this stone should have been exhausted.
"Is this stone useful?" Luo Zheng asked.
"As creatures of the Secondary World, you naturally can't see through the mystery of this stone," Tian Chuan said as he reached out and pointed lightly at the Holy Stone.
"Hua!"
The Light of Destruction suddenly diffused from the Holy Stone like water.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng faintly raised his eyebrows. He had already clasped the Holy Stone in his hand and grasped the spear.
The body of the spear was covered with regular grooves. When the Light of Destruction from the Holy Stone surged out, it quickly covered the entire spear along the grooves.
Perhaps feeling the threat of the Light of Destruction, the red light in the black head's eyes brewed again. "Whoosh —" Two red lights crisscrossed and swept across the ground, heading straight for Luo Zheng's head.
Before anyone could open their mouths to warn him, Luo Zheng's silver spear suddenly protruded from the ground as he fell sideways. Like a dexterous monkey, he leaped high into the air, then fell down rapidly, and continued to roll.
The deadly red light followed Luo Zheng all the way, but was dodged by Luo Zheng's series of movements.
The others spread out during this process, afraid that they would be caught in the crossfire.
The red light chased after Luo Zheng for two rounds before it finally dimmed.
However, it was impossible for the black head to let Luo Zheng go. After all, the Light of Annihilation was an extremely deadly existence to it.
"Buzz …"
The red light in its eyes began to accumulate again.
Taking advantage of this gap, Luo Zheng also circled up. The long spear in his hand shook violently, and he pointed it at the huge black head.
"Shua!"
The silver Light of Annihilation turned into a sharp spear light that went straight for the black head.
Compared to those big-headed monsters, the black head's moving speed was not fast. For Luo Zheng, it was just a live target.
The spear light burst out and hit the black head between its eyebrows. With a "pop" sound, it pierced a big hole and disappeared into the world behind the Clear Sky Platform.
Everyone was cautiously observing the black head's reaction.
The previous Vengeful Level Freaks stopped directly after being hit by the Light of Annihilation. The effect was surprisingly good.
If this Vengeful Level Freak was the same, Luo Zheng's spear would be enough to kill it!
"Gululu …"
The black head shook in the air, and the round hole on its head quickly began to heal. At the same time, the two red lights in its eyes burst out toward Luo Zheng again!
Luo Zheng's pupils suddenly shrank. His whole body began to flee like a dexterous rabbit.
The others also looked as if they were facing a formidable enemy. The Clear Sky Platform was not big. Even if the black head did not deliberately target them, they would lose their lives if they were slightly affected.
As Luo Zheng ran along the Clear Sky Platform, the Light of Annihilation continued to gush out of the "Holy Stone" in his palm.
After taking advantage of an opening, Luo Zheng stabbed the black head again.
This spear pierced through the black head with great precision, but just like before, the wound quickly healed. This Vengeful Level Freak was not injured at all.
"It's not effective!"
"The silver energy can't kill a Vengeful Level Freak?"
"Let's just run. There's no need to fight with our lives on the line …"
Everyone thought that Luo Zheng could kill this Vengeful Level Freak with the silver energy. At the very least, he could severely injure it. Unexpectedly, the two spears hit and did not cause any damage to it.
"Senior Tianchuan, why is that?" Luo Zheng asked.
"I … don't know." Tianchuan shook his head.
As the world's creator, Tianchuan was not good at fighting.
Although he also knew that living creatures in the Main World would turn into Freaks after death and would fear the Light of Annihilation, he did not have a thorough understanding of the strength of Freaks and their strength.
Seeing Tianchuan's confused face, Luo Zheng also knew that it was useless to ask. Since they could not defeat the enemy, the best thing to do was to leave. Thus, he shouted loudly, "We can't hurt this guy. Everyone, move toward the gate and leave this place!"
The black-robed people moved the fastest. They had wanted to escape from this damned place earlier. By the time Luo Zheng shouted, several black-robed people had already reached the gate.
The black-robed person at the front grabbed the gate and suddenly grabbed it backward. However, the gate did not move at all. The black-robed person even shouted strangely, "The gate can't be opened!"
When the black-robed people at the back heard this, they immediately became anxious. They all rushed forward and grabbed the edge of the gate and pulled it outward. However, the gate still did not show any signs of loosening.
Soon, the leader of the black-robed people, the Immortal Hunters, Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others also joined in. However, this gate seemed to have been cast by a magical power and did not move at all!
"Hehehehe …" Seeing this scene, the black head let out a series of mocking laughter. "This is a dead end. How can the small fish and shrimps who enter this place leave alive?"
Last time, the black head spoke in Sanskrit. This time, it actually communicated in human language.
"Buzz!"
As soon as it finished speaking, the red light in its eyes accumulated again. It looked like it was about to shoot toward the gate.
"Whoosh, whoosh!"
Two spear rays whizzed out and accurately hit its pair of red eyes, creating two big holes in its face.
With its eyes gone, the red light naturally dissipated.
However, this kind of injury was still not a problem for it. The two holes quickly healed into a pair of eyes. It laughed, and the red light was brewing in them again.
"Whoosh, whoosh!"
Luo Zheng raised his hand and shot two more times, once again blinding it.
"You …"
The black head was finally a little depressed.
Its strongest magical power was the dazzling death light in its eyes.
If this kid kept blinding its eyes, it would not have a good way to deal with him.
Just as Luo Zheng was dragging the black head, Tian Chuan also floated to the gate.
As the creator of the world, Tian Chuan's strength might not be strong, but he was very familiar with the Chaotic World, the Nirvana Realm, and everything here. When everyone saw Tian Chuan coming over, they all made way.
Tian Chuan reached out and pressed on the gate, rubbing it twice. Then he said, "This gate is not right …"
Nuwa, Fuxi, and the others did not understand Tian Chuan's words.
Luo Zheng stabbed the black head with his spear and asked, "The gate is not right? Senior, what do you mean? "
"The gate has been replaced," Tian Chuan said.
"After so many years, maybe the original gate has rotted. Maybe someone replaced it," Luo Zheng replied.
Unexpectedly, Tian Chuan shook his head. "No, it was just replaced. There …"
Heaven Legend pointed at another Clear Sky Platform from afar.
A person stood on the Clear Sky Platform. That person's appearance was exactly the same as the black head.
When that person felt the gaze of Tian Chuan and Luo Zheng from afar, the corner of his mouth curled up, revealing a strange smile.
The black head's mouth also curled up, revealing a smile that was exactly the same as his.

Chapter 3609
When the others saw Luo Zheng and Tianchuan's gazes, they all looked toward the Clear Sky Platform.
When they saw the person on the Clear Sky Platform, they were instantly shocked.
"There's someone there!"
"That person looks the same as the black head!"
"Who is he?"
Those who could stay in the Clear Jade Sky were definitely not ordinary people.
"Senior Tianchuan, do you know him?" Luo Zheng asked.
Tianchuan stared across the Clear Sky Platform as if he was searching for memories of this person.
The person on the Clear Sky Platform opposite him had the same strange smile as the black head. He did not say anything as if he was giving Tianchuan time to think.
As a world builder, Tianchuan had always been focused on building one world after another and did not care about the Main Realm. If they encountered trouble before building a world, they only needed to call the Shifang Prefecture.
The experts dispatched by the Shifang Prefecture would clean up all the trouble.
The world builders had the most contact with people from the Shifang Prefecture …
Tianchuan searched through his memories for a long time before he suddenly remembered something. His eyes widened as he pointed at the person opposite him and said, "I remember now. You're from the Shifang Prefecture. You're a prefecture general, a prefecture general!"
The powerful experts in the Shifang Prefecture were called prefecture generals.
There were many big and small troubles in the Main Realm …
For example, when building a world, they invaded the territories of some races or demolished the settlements of some barbaric races. They would need the help of the Shifang Prefecture.
Under normal circumstances, the prefecture generals would not be needed. Ordinary experts from the Shifang Prefecture were enough.
However, there was one time when their group of 200 people was attacked by the Tailed Butterfly Tribe. All 200 builders were trapped. The people from the Shifang Prefecture had sent three batches of people, but they all returned in defeat. In the end, the Shifang Prefecture only sent six prefecture generals.
That was also the first time Tianchuan saw a prefecture general from the Shifang Prefecture. This Prefecture General Yue was the leader at that time.
The prefecture generals were terrifyingly powerful. The six prefecture generals quickly wiped out the Tailed Butterfly Race. Their efficiency was terrifyingly high.
Although this Prefecture General Yue left a clear impression on Tianchuan at that time, he had only met him once. That black head was clearly the face of Prefecture General Yue. However, Tianchuan only recalled it now.
"Prefecture General Yue, what happened? Why did someone dare to kill us? I heard that the Eternal God Court was abandoned. What happened in the Four Spirit Gates? "Tianchuan asked a series of questions.
Tianchuan had been attacked by the big-headed monster and was now being attacked by the black head suspected to be controlled by Prefecture General Yue.
However, Tianchuan still regarded the Human Spirit Sect as his home and trusted Shifang Prefecture General Yue.
Therefore, he only believed 30% of Luo Zheng's words at most. Now, he wanted to verify it with the General of the Si Mansion.
"Hahaha …" The general of the Si Mansion let out a series of laughter. This time, he began to speak in Sanskrit. "If I were you, I would kill all these people and then obediently commit suicide. The Wailing Path is a place to settle down and ensure that you can still live."
Luo Zheng and Tian Chuan's faces darkened when they heard the General's words. Tian Chuan asked again, "What do you mean by that?"
"You're just a Builder. Why do you have so many questions?" . "Prefecture General Yue scolded coldly," You just need to do as I say. I command you as Shifang Prefecture General! "
Some of the situations had already been arranged in the First Chaos Era. The appearance of the Prefect of Nether Manor was only to remove some of the variables.
The strength of the World Builders was not worth mentioning, but they were too familiar with the Chaos World and could be considered one of the variables, so they had set up a lot of enemies here.
Every time a world builder entered here, they would be beaten back to Wailing Path by the enemies...
. But this time, Tianchuan won, which alarmed Prefecture General Yue.
Shifang Prefecture General …
He was a high and mighty existence in the main realm. One word from him could determine the life and death of a large clan.
Given Tianchuan's status, he could only accept his orders unconditionally.
"Kill them and then commit suicide?" Tianchuan muttered.
Luo Zheng looked at Tianchuan calmly. He clenched the spear in his hand. The silver-white Light of Annihilation was slowly circulating in the veined patterns on the spear shaft and the spear tip. The Light of Annihilation could kill the enemies. Naturally, it could also kill Tianchuan, who had the body of an enemy.
"Senior Tianchuan, your obsession is to awaken. Could it be that you want to return to a muddle-headed state?" Luo Zheng asked.
Tianchuan glanced at Luo Zheng, then looked at Prefecture General Yue in the distance.
