Prefecture General Yue's eyes revealed contempt. The black head's eyes also had the same expression. "Ignorant thing. You think you're someone after sleeping for so many years?"
"Buzz, buzz …"
Prefecture General Yue's eyes began to flicker with blood-red light. The black head's eyes also accumulated red light. It was about to launch another wave of attack.
But the red light in its eyes didn't come out. "Pop, pop!" Two silver spear lights pierced its eyes again.
Luo Zheng rested the long spear on his shoulder and said, "More than a thousand chaos epochs have passed. Maybe the Human Soul Sect and Shifang Prefecture don't exist anymore. Instead of asking this guy, it's better to look for clues on our own!"
Luo Zheng's voice was transmitted from the Inner World. His voice wasn't loud, but Prefecture General Yue naturally heard it clearly through the black head.
In the eyes of Prefecture General Yue, except for the World Builder who couldn't be killed, the others were just some insects that couldn't be brought to the table.
Although Luo Zheng repeatedly pierced the black head's eyes, Prefecture General Yue still credited the Light of Destruction rather than Luo Zheng's strength.
"Hahaha, do you think you can leave?" Prefecture General Yue asked in a mocking tone.
"How would I know if I don't try?" The silver brilliance in Luo Zheng's right hand began to gather again.
"Arrogant insect, I'll let you try …"
Prefecture General Yue said as he walked two steps around the edge of the Clear Sky Platform. Round balls began to protrude from the skin around his neck, as if there were eggs swimming in them.
At the same time, the big-headed monsters below the black head began to tremble. They began to merge with each other and spread in all directions, gradually forming the body of a black giant!
The black giant was at least ten thousand feet tall. It could cross the Clear Sky Platform in just ten steps.
At this moment, Prefecture General Yue in the distance lifted the clothes draped over his body. His body trembled slightly, and cracks appeared on the surface of his body. Then, countless eyes opened all over his body.
The black giant's actions were exactly the same as his. Eyes also appeared on the surface of the black giant's body. When these eyes opened, the blood-red light began to gather again.

Chapter 3610
When everyone saw the red light in so many pairs of eyes, their hearts trembled.
This red light was extremely deadly. Anyone who was hit by it would immediately turn into a pile of ash.
How could they dodge the red light released by so many eyes?
"Retreat!" Luo Zheng's expression became grave when he saw those eyes.
The Wing Monarch, Fuxi, Nuwa, the Immortal Hunter, and the black-robed people quickly left the door and retreated towards the edge of the Clear Sky Platform.
Although they couldn't escape the range of the red light, they still had some room to dodge.
Even Tianchuan retreated like them. Only Luo Zheng didn't retreat.
Instead of retreating, he advanced.
For the sake of the others' safety, he had to lure the black giant to the other side of the Clear Sky Platform.
"Weng, weng, weng, weng …"
Because there were so many eyes, the process of the black giant accumulating the red light was much slower.
Luo Zheng took advantage of this time to attack!
"Break for me!"
Luo Zheng leapt up and spun his spear, stabbing towards the black giant's body.
"Xiu, xiu, xiu, xiu …"
Previously, Luo Zheng was afraid that the Holy Stone's Light of Annihilation would run out, so he attacked as accurately as possible, not daring to waste anything.
Now that the situation was dire, Luo Zheng couldn't care less.
Streaks of white spear light streaked through the air, heading straight for the black giant.
At first, the black giant's movements were very slow. It could only be used as a live target for Luo Zheng to attack. Now that it had a body, it was a complete body. Under the control of General Si, it was even more nimble than expected!
Luo Zheng's spear light was like a white glowing net, covering the black giant's vital points. In this round of attacks, Luo Zheng planned to pierce through the black giant's twenty pairs of eyes.
But the instant the spear light approached, the black giant gently moved a step to the side.
"Pop, pop, pop, pop, pop …"
The spear light pierced dozens of holes in the black giant's body, but only one of the eyes on its chest was pierced.
Luo Zheng wasn't surprised by this result.
General Si was definitely not a mediocre person in the Human Spirit Sect to be respected by Tianchuan. It would be stranger if such an opponent were easily dealt with.
"Buzz, buzz, buzz …"
It was true that Luo Zheng had pierced an eye, but more eyes were opening on the black giant's body. At the same time, the red light that had been brewing for a long time gushed out.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh —"
Nearly 40 red beams of light shot out from the black giant's body!
As the black giant continued to rotate, the red pillars of light swept in all directions without any pattern.
These light pillars extended all the way to the outside of the Clear Sky Platform before disappearing. Naturally, no one on the Clear Sky Platform could escape.
"Disperse!" The Wings King let out a low warning.
It flapped its wings and floated in the air. Its strange posture made it look like a pool of flowing water, floating against the light beams.
Fuxi, Nuwa, Donghuang, and the others used different techniques to dodge the light pillars.
"Creak creak …"
The Victorious Heavenly Rat King's small body gave it an advantage, but the direction and momentum of these light beams were irregular. Sometimes, a light beam that was clearly heading downwards would suddenly change direction, and the Victorious Heavenly Rat King's legs would be hit by the red light.
By the time it jumped up, its legs had already turned to ashes …
Seeing the Victorious Heavenly Rat King's body falling backwards, the Blood Wolf not far away suddenly stretched out his hand. A thin, almost imperceptible spider silk had already wrapped around the Victorious Heavenly Rat King's body, lifting it up.
The Blood Wolf admittedly saved the Victorious Heavenly Rat King's life, but another red pillar of light rose from his side, directly sweeping past his body. Only half of his originally complete body was left, and the other half was already flying ash.
Fortunately, the Blood Wolf's body was extremely special. His body was originally made of interwoven spider silk. After losing half of his body, his body made a hissing sound, and the disappeared body was actually rewoven.
However, compared to before, he was actually much shorter, and his face was also somewhat pale …
The black-robed people's situation was even worse!
The leader of the black-robed people still had the strength to protect himself, but the other black-robed people had almost no resistance in the face of the fluttering red light pillars. If they were careless, they would be swept by the red light pillars and turned into a pile of ash.
Although the leader of the black-robed people tried his best to save his clansmen while dodging, how could he save them in the face of so many red light pillars? In the blink of an eye, three black-robed people had fallen …
"Whoosh!"
Before a red light pillar shone over, Luo Zheng sprinted forward for a while, crouching down, and somersaulting up.
"Whoosh!"
The spear light transformed by the light of destruction accurately hit the leg of the black giant, destroying one of the red eyes on its leg.
After killing this red eye, Luo Zheng somersaulted up again. In a short time, he climbed up thousands of feet and was almost level with the black giant's head. The black giant and Luo Zheng in the air looked at each other. Seeing that the red light pillar was about to spurt out, Luo Zheng's long spear pierced through it again.
"Pop, pop, pop, pop, pop …"
Luo Zheng fell in a free fall, and at the same time, the silver spear light constantly pierced through the eyes of the black giant.
Although the black giant's eyes would still grow back, he tried his best to destroy these eyes to reduce the red light pillars, and also to relieve the pressure on the others.
On another Clear Sky Platform, the general of the Si Mansion looked at Luo Zheng jumping around like a flea, and a trace of boredom appeared on his face.
The speed of this human was pretty fast. But, speed was relative.
Luo Zheng could circle around the black giant and launch a series of attacks only because the general of the Si Mansion was not serious.
"Annoying bug," the general of the Si Mansion said indifferently.
Then, he suddenly raised his hand.
The black giant made the same movements as him. This giant did not slow down because of its huge size. In fact, its movements were almost synchronized with the general of the Si Mansion.
The general of the Si Mansion looked at the empty space in front of him and was silent for about two breaths. Then, like catching a fly, he suddenly grabbed at it.
The black giant's right hand also grabbed forward at an incredible speed!
While concentrating on piercing through the eyes, Luo Zheng was naturally wary of the black giant's hand. However, he never expected that the seemingly slow black giant could be so fast.
By the time he reacted, his vision turned black, and he was already caught in the black giant's hand.
"It's over. Die."
The voice of the general of the Si Mansion came from the black giant's mouth.
He spoke in human language. Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others, who were still dodging in the distance, naturally heard him. Seeing Luo Zheng caught in the black giant's hand, they immediately showed panic on their faces.
"Buzz!"
There was also an eye in the black giant's hand, and the red light in the eye burst out.

Chapter 3611
When Luo Zheng was caught in the palm, he wanted to pierce through it with his spear and break free.
However, the black giant's strength was amazing. After the huge force pinched Luo Zheng's body, Luo Zheng couldn't move at all.
"Buzz …"
As the red light lit up, Luo Zheng's pupils suddenly widened. Then, he was drowned by the red light.
"No!"
"Luo Zheng!"
"No way …"
The red light shot out from the gap in the black giant's palm.
Nuwa, Fuxi, Donghuang, and the others were horrified when they saw this.
The red light was unimaginably powerful. Anyone who touched it would turn into powder. Luo Zheng was probably no exception.
The three Immortal Hunters fell silent when they saw this.
There was no doubt that they thought highly of Luo Zheng. Otherwise, they wouldn't have followed him all the way here.
Who would have thought that this was Luo Zheng's final destination …
It might also be their final destination …
The leader of the black-robed men stood in place in a daze. Because the thick black robe covered his face, his expression couldn't be seen clearly.
The black giant slowly spread out his hand.
Everyone saw a pile of shiny silver armor and a spear, as well as a pile of powder mixed in them.
General Si, who was on the Clear Sky Platform opposite them, revealed a slightly bored smile and tilted his hand slightly.
The black giant also tilted his right hand, allowing the armor, spear, and powder to fall from his fingertips to the ground.
When Tian Chuan saw this scene in the distance, a sad expression appeared on his face. After many struggles, he finally awakened. However, he didn't even have the right to know the truth. Now, he was going to be obliterated.
"Dong, dong, dong …"
The black giant walked over step by step. Red light accumulated in his eyes again. At the same time, General Si said in Sanskrit through him, "I told you earlier that you saved me the trouble by doing it yourself. Since you asked me to do it, I won't let you die so easily. Although the Treacherous Body can't be killed, I can change some of your obsessions and reduce the possibility of your awakening infinitely. Moreover, you will be reincarnated in pain again and again …"
After the black giant stepped over the armor and spear on the ground, the pile of ashes scattered on the ground slowly moved.
This pile of scattered black dust was like thousands of ants. They divided their work and gathered together bit by bit, slowly forming a small pyramid about ten inches high.
On the pyramid formed by the black dust, a wisp of bright red blood grew from it.
When the bright red blood grew out, it emitted a strange and powerful aura. The moment this aura appeared, it attracted everyone's attention!
"What, what is that?" Tian Xing looked at the blood-red color behind the black giant, his eyes filled with confusion.
Nuwa, Fuxi, the Immortal Hunter, and all the other black-robed people stared at the blood-red thing in a daze. The blood grew out of the black ash like the roots of a plant. It extended upward bit by bit, like the rapid growth of a blood coral in the sea.
"What …"
"Da da!"
The general of the Ji Mansion took two steps forward, almost standing on the edge of the Clear Sky Platform. His eyes were fixed on the blood-red thing.
He sensed a very familiar aura …
That was the aura of origin …
For this brat to be able to come to the Three Clear Skies, he was definitely at the Immortal Realm. Immortal Realm cultivators could possibly obtain the acknowledgement of the Origin Divine Blood and obtain some sort of ability from it.
But what kind of ability was this?
Could it release the aura of the Origin Divine Blood? The general of the Ji Mansion was very confused.
What made him even more confused was, what was that blood-red coral that kept growing?
Sizzle sizzle sizzle …
As the blood continued to grow, it gradually formed a human-shaped coral.
The dust on the ground moved without any wind. It was attracted by the human-shaped coral and slowly gathered on its surface.
The human-shaped coral reached out and grabbed the silver armor and spear. All the armor flew backward and stuck to his body. The spear also flew out of his hand.
The last thing to return to his hand was the cracked square stone. When he held the square stone, the Light of Destruction began to flow in his hand again.
"Buzz!"
Guang Ze's armor and spear quickly recovered their silver color.
"Is, is he still human?"
"He's already turned into dust, but he can still stand up …"
"Luo Zheng's ability …"
During the battle at Li Mountain, everyone had seen Luo Zheng's ability. He could restrain almost everything and could transform into anything.
Even so, no one expected him to be able to stand up so quickly after turning into dust!
What was this brat's ability …
Everyone was stunned.
Tian Xing was also in disbelief.
Those who had been baptized by the Origin Divine Blood were usually very powerful.
The problem was, this brat was still alive even after turning into dust.
What kind of ability did he have?
General Si stared at the black giant for a long time but could not figure it out.
Even if his imagination was extremely rich, he could not figure out that the red blood was the Origin Divine Blood itself.
"Although I don't know what you experienced in the Eternal Divine Court, you have the right to enter the Shi Fang Residence with such a unique ability. It's a pity you were born at the wrong time …" General Si muttered as he suddenly turned around and slapped the ground with one hand.
The black giant turned around and slapped the human-shaped coral on the ground.
"Buzz!"
Red light shot out from the black giant's palm again, completely covering the human-shaped coral.
Nuwa, who was far away, took a deep breath. She stopped blinking and stared intently at the changes in the black giant's palm.
The red light gradually dissipated, and the human-shaped coral disappeared as well.
Then, everyone saw strands of red coral flickering along the black giant's arm. The red coral seemed to be the meridians in the black giant's arm and was rapidly moving upward.
Apart from the red meridians, there were also flashes of white lightning from time to time. That was the Light of Destruction!
The red and white lightning intertwined and spread along the black giant's arm. At the same time, the black giant's arm began to collapse from the hand!
At the same time, General Si clutched his right hand and let out a pained cry. His face became extremely distorted as he shouted, "How is this possible?!"
After the red and white lightning quickly drilled into the black giant's body, the Commander of the Imperial Palace clutched his chest. He felt as if a red-hot iron was stirring up his internal organs.
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh!"
A red and a white light stirred within the black giant's body, and the body collapsed and disappeared ….
The two lights did not stop and drilled into the black giant's head!
"No, no …"
General Si saw the commotion in the distance and realized that something was wrong. He immediately hugged his head and let out a shrill cry.
After he and the black giant became one, they shared their senses.
Unfortunately, the connection between him and the black giant was extremely complicated. It would take time to break it. General Si could only endure the intense pain.

Chapter 3612
General Si's wails lasted for more than ten breaths of time. At this point, the black giant had completely collapsed and dissipated.
Its huge body had completely disappeared, and a human-shaped coral slowly descended from the sky. Its human-shaped body trembled slightly, and the silver armor and spear slipped from its body and scattered all over the ground.
Everyone was still staring at the human-shaped coral in a daze.
They didn't dare to confirm that it was Luo Zheng. They couldn't even confirm what it was.
However, after a short while, countless granules grew from the human-shaped coral. The granules intertwined with each other to form a skeleton. Then, layers of flesh grew rapidly, and finally, Luo Zheng's original appearance was restored.
"Phew …"
Nuwa let out a deep sigh of relief.
She had been very nervous just now, and every muscle in her body had been tense. Now, she suddenly felt relieved.
The three Immortal Hunters opened their mouths wide, as if they couldn't understand at all.
The Immortal Hunters had seen many unique and miraculous creatures, but they had never heard of a creature with such powerful vitality and such a strange form!
Tianchuan was the first to step forward.
In the Wailing Path, Tianchuan had instinctively chosen Luo Zheng's body because he felt that Luo Zheng was indeed stronger than the people around him.
After Tianchuan awakened, he naturally understood that his choice was correct. After all, Luo Zheng was a genius who could be chosen to enter the Eternal God Court.
However, Tianchuan now realized that he had underestimated Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's bloodline power was far stronger than Tianchuan had imagined!
"Your ability comes from the Life Quadrant of the Origin Divine Blood, right? Such a powerful recovery ability is probably a combination of many abilities in the Life Quadrant. With your potential, it's even possible for you to become a general! "Tianchuan exclaimed.
In Tianchuan's eyes, a "general" was already an invincible existence.
However, this was indeed the case. No matter what trouble the world builders encountered, as long as Shifang Prefecture sent out a general, there was no problem that couldn't be solved.
This was also why Tianchuan was so respectful of "General Si."
"Life Quadrant?" Luo Zheng thought for a moment and said, "Probably …"
When the red light shone on him, Luo Zheng naturally felt the fluctuations of the Origin Divine Blood.
The Origin Divine Blood was not under Luo Zheng's control. In the beginning, the Origin Divine Blood seemed to be able to break down the information contained in the fluctuation process and inform Luo Zheng. Even when Luo Zheng came into contact with any energy, the Origin Divine Blood could analyze it and then give it the energy to restrain it.
But gradually, the Origin God Blood became cold to him. Now, as long as it wasn't a fatal threat, the Origin God Blood wouldn't take the initiative to help him …
Just now, when facing the black giant, Luo Zheng rushed forward without hesitation. He also had the purpose of stimulating the origin divine blood.
Tianchuan didn't continue to entangle himself with Luo Zheng's bloodline. He turned around and still looked at the other Clear Sky Platform. "But what on earth did General Si go through? Why did he become so violent and insist on throwing me back to the Wailing Road?"
"The situation must be more complicated than you think," Luo Zheng said as he looked over.
On the Clear Sky Platform in the distance, General Si was freed from extreme pain. His face was covered in dark clouds.
The awakening of the Creepy on the Wailing Road was originally an accident. This was also the reason why General Si hurried over. But after the accident, there was an even bigger accident. A bastard had actually killed a Resentment Level Creepy. This was too inconceivable and abnormal!
In the Human Spirit Sect, Shifang Prefecture had always welcomed all geniuses. This was also the foundation of the Human Spirit Sect's strength.
However, some Human Spirits in the Human Spirit Sect were like General Si. They looked down on humans from the Secondary World from the bottom of their hearts. In their eyes, although these humans continued to have the appearance of Human Spirits and were very similar in many aspects, they were still bastards.
But after this bastard killed a Resentment Level Creepy, he was still able to be reborn intact. How did this kid do that with his magical power that was like a magic trick?
"Gurgle gurgle …"
A mass of black substance floated up from the Clear Sky Platform where Luo Zheng and the others were. This black substance was a part of the black giant's remains. It could be considered a fish that had escaped the net.
General Si kept pinching it with both hands. The black substance in the pattern also changed shape as he pinched it. Soon, it turned into General Si's head.
This head was about the size of an ordinary person's head. The Creepy aura it contained was very weak.
The space of the Three Pure Heavens was very special. Whether it was energy or matter, it would disappear after leaving the Clear Sky Platform. The same was true for sound. General Si could only use this method to transmit his voice.
"How many light pillars did you raise in the Eternal Divine Court? Which light pillars were they?" General Si asked.
One had to know that there were 373 types of abilities in the Origin Divine Blood. These 373 types of abilities could be fused together to obtain countless abilities. The Four Spirit Sect had studied the Origin Divine Blood for so long, but they still could not completely figure out its order.
The ability Luo Zheng had displayed was very powerful. Of course, General Si wanted to know.
"No comment," Luo Zheng replied concisely.
General Si's expression changed. There was a hint of coldness in his eyes. "You're not afraid of death?"
"Maybe I am," Luo Zheng replied honestly.
General Si's expression softened when he heard this. "You are valuable to us. I can spare your life, but you have to kill everyone else."
Tian Xing's eyebrows rose when he heard this.
The others did not understand General Si's words, but they could tell from Tian Xing's expression that it was not something good.
Luo Zheng controlled his temper and smiled. "You? Who is it? Shifang Constabulary? Or the Human Spirit Sect? "
"You don't need to know too much. Just do as I say," General Si continued to tempt him.
"Sorry, I'm not interested." Luo Zheng reached out and grabbed at the air. The spear on the ground appeared in his hand. He pointed it at General Si's "head".
General Si did not expect Luo Zheng to suddenly change his expression. He hurriedly said, "Wait, wait …"
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng did not give General Si a chance to speak. The Light of Destruction pierced through General Si's head and dissipated.
The spear that pierced through General Si's head swayed slightly and pointed straight at the Clear Sky Platform. Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
He was interested in General Si's background. Since General Si was unwilling to speak, there was no need to continue the conversation.
General Si did not expect Luo Zheng to suddenly attack. He felt like he had been fooled. Especially since the one who had fooled him was a creature from another world! His eyes were about to spit fire.
"Luo! Contact the Yue House and have them attack! "General Si said in a low voice.
A brown shadow appeared next to the general. This was a type of strange object known as a "network". It was capable of transmitting information.
The surface of this "Luo" flickered and sent out a message. "Master Si, the Yue House said we can't go. The Aquarius Stage is under attack."
"They dare to attack the Aquarius Stage?" General Si was slightly shocked.
"The Yue House has requested for help. The Black House has already rushed over," Luo said. "Those fellows are very determined. They have turned out in full force. I'm afraid they want to take the energy that was extracted."

Chapter 3613
The Aquarius Platform was also a Clear Sky Platform. In the center of this Clear Sky Platform was an aquarius that was taller than a person, hence its name.
One of the Divine Daos extracted from the fusion energy was sealed in the aquarius. Its purpose was to prevent the complete fusion energy from being born in the Chaos World.
Although the possibility of relying on oneself to fuse with the fusion energy was extremely low, it was not impossible.
Once the complete fusion energy was grasped, it would cause a lot of trouble. Thus, the Aquarius Platform was heavily guarded and almost impossible to break through.
More importantly, the key to the Trillion Tower Mountain was extremely well protected and only in the hands of a few awakened ones. It had not been leaked for more than a thousand chaos epochs. How did the enemy know about it now?
The Aquarius General smelled something unusual in this matter.
In comparison, the world builder on the other side was just a small matter …
"Consider yourselves lucky, but you may not be able to escape!" The Aquarius General looked at Luo Zheng and said coldly. Then he turned and walked towards the center of the Clear Sky Platform. With a burst of orange light, the Aquarius General disappeared.
Luo Zheng and the others naturally saw the Aquarius General leave.
"The Aquarius General probably won't let this go," Tianchuan said worriedly.
Luo Zheng nodded. "Let's leave here first."
At this time, everyone's attention was focused on the door again.
According to the Heavenly Transmission, the door was swapped without anyone noticing. This door was to prevent those who entered the Clear Sky Platform from escaping, so it was not easy to break it open.
"Let's try again together. No matter what, this door is an inanimate object. I don't believe that we can't open it if we work together!" Fuxi suggested.
The situation just now was very urgent. They had not been able to open the door with brute force. Now that they had more time to deal with it, they were naturally more optimistic.
"Everyone, apply this." The Blood Lion suddenly took out a small greasy paper bag.
After opening the bag, a small grease cube was revealed. This grease cube emitted a faint fragrance.
Nuwa, Fuxi, and the others did not recognize it, but Donghuang's eyes lit up. "This is dragonbeast oil? It's so big! "
"Mm." The Blood Lion nodded.
The Dragon Beast was also a kind of living creature in the Divine River. After killing it, one could peel its skin and cut open its stomach to find this kind of oil. Smearing the oil on one's hands could greatly increase one's strength.
Dragonbeast oil was also a very rare item in the Blaze Maple Forest. After all, this item would not attract the attacks of the Blaze Maple Forest, but it could greatly increase one's strength.
This piece of dragonbeast oil was also a treasure to the Blood Lion, but in order to escape, it naturally couldn't hide it.
Everyone stretched out their hands, and the Blood Lion grabbed the grease cube and smeared it on his face.
Even the treacherous Tian Chuan was covered in it.
After Luo Zheng applied the grease on his hands, he felt a warm sensation. Then, he felt a sense of strength in his hands. This grease was really magical.
After applying it, the black-robed leader took out a small golden statue. It had a human face and a beast body, and there were four orbs on its back. Three of them had already been crushed, and the black-robed leader said, "This treasure can double everyone's strength, but it's already been used three times. This will be the last time."
As he spoke, he reached out and crushed the last orb. With a "pa" sound, two rays of golden light shot out from the eyes of the golden statue.
When everyone was shrouded in the golden light, they immediately felt their strength increase dramatically!
To be able to double the strength of living creatures within a certain range, this golden statue was naturally not an ordinary item. Unfortunately, it would be useless after this use. It was really a pity.
In addition to these two treasures, the others also tried their best to take out their own treasures.
The Nirvana Realm tokens in the Three Pure Heavens were naturally not ordinary items. For a moment, all kinds of energy auras flowed in front of the door.
After they were fully prepared, everyone began to pull together!
Under the terrifying force, the seemingly ordinary door began to tremble slightly.
"Everyone, come on. This door can't hold on." Fuxi's eyes shone.
As everyone's strength increased rapidly, the door trembled more and more violently, as if it was about to collapse.
But just as everyone's heart was filled with hope, a Sanskrit character suddenly appeared on the surface of the door. Around the Sanskrit character were complicated patterns that extended all the way to the edge of the door.
"This is the word 'seal'?" Nuwa said as she stared at it.
The Winged Monarch nodded. "It is indeed the word 'seal'."
Everyone present more or less dabbled in Sanskrit. They could even decipher part of the more complicated Sanskrit characters. Now that this Sanskrit character only expressed one word, they naturally understood it at a glance.
However, they only understood this word and did not understand the patterns on the door.
Tianchuan said to Luo Zheng, "We can't open it. This is a sealed door. Brute force alone can't destroy it."
"We can't open it no matter how strong we are?" Luo Zheng was somewhat doubtful.
Tianchuan nodded. "We can't open it."
Even though Tianchuan was speaking, everyone did not relax at all. Everyone seemed to be squeezing their strength to the limit. However, after the word 'seal' appeared, the door became more and more stable.
After Luo Zheng relayed Tianchuan's words to everyone, the Immortal Hunter and the black-robed man were instantly discouraged. Nuwa, Fuxi, and the other Immortal Realm experts also stopped.
"It's a pity that my dragon beast oil is gone," Blood Lion said regretfully.
The black-robed leader shrugged. Clearly, his precious golden statue was more precious.
"What should we do now? If we can't open the door, won't we be trapped here? Squeak." Rat King Sheng Tian asked.
"Senior Tianchuan, is there any other way to leave here?" Luo Zheng asked.
Tianchuan looked at the door and pondered for a long time. Suddenly, he said, "Then we can only dismantle this Clear Sky Platform …"
"Dismantle the Clear Sky Platform?" Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
Tianchuan looked rather confident. "Don't forget my profession."
In terms of combat ability, Tianchuan's strength might not be considered strong. However, he was a world builder. The entire Chaos was built by him. Of course, he was clear about the mysteries and principles behind it.
Previously, Tianchuan did not want to dismantle the Clear Sky Platform because he was concerned about the other world builders.
The obsession of the other 199 builders in the Wailing Path was similar to Tianchuan's. They had to return here to have a chance of "awakening."
However, in order to leave now, he naturally could not care so much …
Then, he turned around facing the door, walked ten steps away, pointed at his feet, and said, "Break this place."
Luo Zheng followed Tianchuan's instructions. He carried the long spear and stabbed in the direction Tianchuan pointed at.
The ground of the Clear Sky Platform should be very sturdy. The Clear Sky Platform did not break after the fight just now.
"Crack!"
Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng used the end of the long spear to smash the ground fiercely. The ground cracked, revealing a rune underneath.
Tianchuan did not even look at it. He took ten steps to the side again. "Break this place."

Chapter 3614
Luo Zheng followed Tian Chuan's instructions and smashed it down.
"Crack!"
With this smash, a unique talisman character was also revealed.
The second talisman character was somewhat similar to the second talisman character, but the direction was different. There must be some connection between them.
However, Tian Chuan didn't give any explanation. He still walked in the other direction and pointed at the ground, guiding Luo Zheng to smash it open.
"Pa, pa, pa, pa …"
More than twenty holes were smashed into the huge Clear Sky Platform, and there was a talisman character under each hole.
When Luo Zheng smashed the last hole, Tian Chuan stretched out his hand and lightly pressed on the talisman character in the hole. A faint yellow light was projected from the talisman character.
Then, the talisman characters in the holes lit up one after another. When all the talisman characters lit up, everyone felt the ground suddenly tremble.
"Bang!"
A muffled sound came from inside the Clear Sky Platform, as if something heavy had opened.
The huge Clear Sky Platform started to slowly rotate, and the stone pillars that previously held the armor rose up one by one. At the same time, a clear crack appeared in the center of the Clear Sky Platform, as if a tortoise shell split open from the middle was slowly unfolding, revealing the complicated structure inside.
"This Clear Sky Platform's structure is very ingenious," Fuxi praised.
"It's the same as those mechanisms at the bottom of the Myriad Towers Mountain," Nuwa nodded and said while looking at Tian Chuan, "These things were probably meticulously created by the builders of these worlds."
"I'm more interested in those armors and weapons! Squeak! "Victorious Heaven Rat King's soybean-sized eyes stared at the armors inside the stone pillars.
When they first saw those armors and weapons, they attracted everyone's interest.
However, Tian Chuan warned them that those armors and weapons were protected by powerful restrictions. Coupled with the fact that they had encountered Shift, they didn't have any ideas about them.
However, now that the Clear Sky Platform was "unfolding," Victorious Heaven Rat King had another idea.
"Luo Zheng, can we take away some armors and weapons? Squeak! "Victorious Heaven Rat King asked.
If these Immortal Realm experts used those weapons, they could kill Shift with the Light of Destruction.
Luo Zheng thought for a moment before asking, "Senior Tianchuan, these armors and weapons are useless here. Can you give a few sets to my friend? That way, he'll have a better chance of defeating Shift!"
As long as they could use the Light of Annihilation, everyone present would have the ability to deal with Shift.
Hearing Luo Zheng's proposal, Tianchuan turned around and glanced at him. He was a little hesitant.
Although both sides had a certain understanding of each other, Tianchuan was still not sure if Luo Zheng was on the same side as him. Or rather, Tianchuan was very confused about his own future. As the creator of the world, he was originally a member of the Human Soul Sect.
But what was going on with the Human Spirit Sect? Why did Shifang Prefecture attack him? The reasons were all shrouded in mystery. Could this young man named Luo Zheng be trusted?
I'm afraid it's a question mark.
"Senior Tianchuan, are you still questioning our position and origin?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes," Tianchuan replied.
Luo Zheng said sternly, "In fact, I don't know what position Senior Tianchuan holds. I believe Senior is also very confused now. But we can help Senior find out the truth. Before we determine Senior's position, we can choose to cooperate."
Seeing Luo Zheng's serious face, Tianchuan was touched. Then, he nodded and said, "In fact, our position may not be important anymore. If as you said, even the Four Spirits Sect has fallen, we, the world creators, would have been thrown away as useless abandoned pawns long ago …"
Tianchuan was very depressed when he was faced with such a situation after struggling for more than a thousand chaos epochs to awaken.
"Senior Tianchuan, you're undervaluing yourself." Luo Zheng shook his head. "If you were abandoned, Shifang Prefecture would not have specially sent people to guard here, and Shifang Prefecture would not have made a special trip here!"
Luo Zheng's words made Tianchuan feel a little better. He then said, "I can't give you those armors and weapons. The other world creators also need these things if they want to awaken."
The world creators came all the way to the Clear Sky Platform to fulfill the process of obsession. These weapons and armors were the necessary conditions for awakening, not to mention that these armors and weapons did not belong to Tianchuan.
"But the armors and weapons are not the source of the light of destruction." Tianchuan pointed to the bottom of the stone pillar. "The really important thing is there. Take it out."
Although Luo Zheng felt a little strange, he still approached the stone pillar and reached out to touch it.
As he touched it, he took out a palm-sized square stone from the bottom. In the center of this square stone was inlaid a white gravel. Luo Zheng took it to Tianchuan and asked, "Senior, this is …"
"This is also a holy stone, but it's very small." Tianchuan smiled slightly. "The light of destruction in the armors and weapons is obtained from this holy stone the size of a gravel."
Luo Zheng suddenly understood and was quite surprised at the same time.
A holy stone the size of a gravel could provide a lot of light of destruction. The holy stone snatched from Lei Sha was a hundred times bigger. No wonder the light of destruction in it was endless.
"The light of destruction in these holy stones will slowly be replenished after it is used up. It's a sharp weapon against Deception. Everyone present can take one," Tianchuan said.
After Luo Zheng relayed Tianchuan's words to everyone present, they all showed excitement in their eyes, especially the three Immortal Hunter brothers.
The light of destruction was extremely effective against Deception. It was indeed a sharp weapon.
After getting permission, the others took out a square stone from the bottom of the stone pillar.
After Tianchuan taught everyone the method of activating the light of destruction, they carefully put it away.
Then Tianchuan turned his gaze to the center of the Clear Sky Platform. There was a wall on one side of the unfolded part. "It's time to leave here. Just break that wall."
Luo Zheng flew forward after hearing the order and punched the wall.
"Boom!"
The wall collapsed with a bang, revealing a passageway half the height of a person.
Luo Zheng bent down and entered the passageway. After walking for more than 200 feet, another wall appeared in front of him. He punched it again.
"Boom!"
After the wall collapsed, a huge pool was revealed in front of everyone.
The others came out of the passageway in a line. When they saw the pool and the bronze statues again, they all felt like they had survived a disaster.
After this battle, Li Shan's group and the Immortal Hunters didn't suffer any casualties. They were very lucky.
On the other hand, more than half of the black-robed men had died.
These black-robed men had some complaints about their leader. After all, they had followed Luo Zheng all the way here. They didn't get any benefits and even lost so many of their brothers.
However, after the black-robed leader's persuasion, they calmed down a lot.
After all, they only got six eagle eggs with Luo Zheng's help. That saved the entire Night Man Clan. This was a great favor.
Moreover, each of them got a holy stone gravel. In terms of fighting against Deception, they had gained a lot.

Chapter 3615
At the edge of the Frigid Plain.
More than a hundred feet deep underground, two crystal-like ice frogs were sleeping.
This kind of Frigid Ice Frog was very rare. As long as they were not awakened, they could sleep for about a thousand years. It was one of the most popular Nirvana tokens in Shang Qing Sky.
"Crack, crack …"
A force dug down from above, and the large vibration woke up the two Frigid Ice Frogs.
"Hoo …"
Dense white mist was released from their bodies. This extremely low-temperature white mist not only froze them into a layer of protective ice, but also froze the surrounding enemies to death.
However, the diggers above were not afraid of this extremely cold white mist. They were the fire spirits of the Elemental Spirit Clan.
When the white mist rushed up, the fire spirit let out a sneer, and the hot flames immediately dispelled all the white mist.
"Hoo hoo …"
The flames above poured into the pit like poisonous snakes, and soon the Frigid Ice Frogs and the hard ice on their bodies turned into a pool of water.
"There's nothing here. I'll continue to dig to the west …" the fire spirit said.
On the east side of the Frigid Plain near the snowline, the snow that had accumulated for countless years had now disappeared completely, exposing the dark frozen soil layer. Even the frozen soil layer had been dug up …
The Elemental Spirit Clan really dug three feet into the ground.
Fire floated in the air and looked at the dark ground silently. "You can't even find such a large area?"
The holy garment armor floating next to Fire had been controlled by "Claire". Claire looked across the non-existent snowline and said, "Could it be that we have entered the Wilderness of No Return?"
A trace of caution flickered in Fire's coquettish eyes.
The snowline had been destroyed as soon as she arrived, which was a bit of a sign that she was trying to hide something. It was indeed possible.
But if they didn't return to the Wilderness of No Return, weren't they afraid of death?
"Maybe they can find a special way, and this way is in the Wilderness of No Return," Claire said. "I can give it a try …"
With that, Claire gently raised her hand, and an exit formed by blue light appeared above the palm of the holy winged armor. "Whoosh …"
Countless fist-sized fire spirits were released by Claire.
These fire spirits were all young …
They were also members of the Elemental Spirit Clan.
However, their intelligence was not high enough and they only knew how to follow orders.
Seeing Claire send these little fire spirits into the Wasteland of No Return, Fire said angrily, "Do we need to sacrifice so many fire spirits?"
"Anyway, this thing is easy to divide. Fire, you can go back to the Holy Wing Armor and make millions more." Claire shrugged, and the Holy Wing Armor suddenly made a sound of metal friction.
"You …" Fire's face darkened slightly. She regretted helping Claire get the Holy Wing Armor.
Ah Huo could indeed nurture millions of fire spirits, but in her eyes, no matter how weak a fire spirit was, it was still a member of the Meta Spirit Clan.
But Claire was different. In her eyes, these fire spirits were just a bunch of unthinking flames …
These little fire spirits huddled together and made chirping sounds, as if they were children of flames. Half of them were jumping around excitedly, forming a huge ball of flame as they huddled together.
When this huge ball of flame reached the edge of the snow line, it rushed into the Wasteland of No Return.
"Whoosh …"
"Ji ji …"
The first little fire spirit rushed into the Wasteland of No Return and rushed forward without hesitation.
Claire had already ordered these little fire spirits to turn back on their own if they could find the way. If they couldn't, they would die.
But this little fire spirit just kept running forward, obviously unable to turn back. It would exhaust its energy in the Wasteland of No Return and eventually turn into an ordinary little flame that would slowly extinguish.
Little fire spirits kept rushing out of the huge flame into the Wasteland of No Return one after another.
Looking from a distance, there was a flame line spreading diagonally forward on the Wasteland of No Return, which looked very shocking.
"Those human beings destroyed this snowline. If they entered from somewhere in the snowline, they must be able to find the way." Claire said confidently.
Of course, Fire knew that this method was useful, but looking at those little fire spirits who were "going to die", she felt very uncomfortable.
"Swoosh …"
As the little fire spirits rushed into the Wasteland of No Return one after another, they were getting closer and closer to the road in the Wasteland of No Return!
Ten miles …
Five miles …
Three miles …
When they were only a mile away from the road, the little fire spirits had all rushed into the Wasteland of No Return.
"There are not enough little fire spirits. Give me more!" Claire said and raised her hand again. From the blue light above her arm, groups of bright blue little thunder spirits drilled out. These little thunder spirits kept making crackling sounds and began to gather together.
Anyway, the other Elemental Spirits in the Holy Wing Armor had been released, and no one could control Claire. Everything inside the Holy Wing Armor was under Claire's control.
Just as Claire was about to issue an order, a voice suddenly sounded in her and Fire's minds.
"Fire, Jie, come back." It was Eye Spirit's call.
Hearing Eye Spirit's call, both Fire and Claire's faces became extremely serious.
If it weren't for special circumstances, Eye Spirit wouldn't make an emergency call.
"Lady Eye Spirit, what happened?" Fire asked.
"The Wukong Clan attacked the Aquarius Terrace." Eye Spirit said.
Fire's eyes were full of incredulity. "They … dare?"
On the surface, the Elemental Spirit civilization and the Wukong Clan were fighting in the Otherworld Battlefield, but in fact, there were two more powerful forces behind them.
The Aquarius Terrace had always been the focus of the Elemental Spirit civilization's protection. There was not only the Elemental Spirit civilization there, but also the powerful Treachery. How dare the Wukong Clan attack at this time? Were they crazy?
"Go back." Eye Spirit left only two brief words.
But for Fire and Claire, this was an order they couldn't disobey.
Claire gently raised her hand, and the released thunder spirits returned to the Holy Wing Armor.
Fire also informed the Elemental Spirits who were searching on the cold plain. After these Elemental Spirits drilled into the Holy Wing Armor, an emotionless voice came. "Claire, hand over the control of the Holy Wing Armor."
It was a special situation just now. The elders of the Elemental Spirits had all left the armor, so it was handed over to Claire for safekeeping.
Now all the Elemental Spirits had returned to their positions. Together, they were "Jie Yan" itself, so they naturally had to hand over Claire.
"The situation is urgent. I'm controlling the Holy Wing Armor now." Claire replied.
It wasn't easy for her to get control of the Holy Wing Armor, so how could she give it back casually?
"The more urgent the situation is, the more we have to abide by the rules. Fire, don't you think …" Jie Yan continued.
Claire just snorted coldly, and the voice of "Jie Yan" stopped abruptly.
The inner world of the Holy Wing Armor was controlled by Claire, so it was easy for Claire to block their voices.
"Let's go!"
"Hoo …"
Claire unfolded the wings of the Holy Wing Armor and drifted forward.
Fire froze in the air, looked at the back of the Holy Wing Armor, narrowed his eyes slightly, and then flew forward.

Chapter 3616
Luo Zheng and the rest returned to the pool and climbed along the long fishing rods.
When they reached the top of the fishing rods, they used two fingers to hold onto the hook and lowered it down.
Luo Zheng, Tian Chuan, Fuxi, and the rest were the first to go down. Not long after, they returned to the cave.

These green crystals completely sealed the cave.

Fuxi stepped forward and gently knocked on the green crystals. He said cautiously, "This thing is very strong. I'm afraid it won't be easy to break it …"

Although the Wing King didn't speak much, he deliberately covered up everything for everyone at the end.
He walked to the green jades and stretched out his wings. These extraordinarily strong green crystals melted like wax and began to flow back to the Wing King's body.
In a short while, the Wing King had absorbed all the wax and stood to the side.
The Green Jade Civilization had failed once and formed a deadly feud with the Wu Kong Clan. In the Hidden Domain of the Thirteenth Heaven, it had already taken a fancy to Luo Zheng and entrusted the hope of the entire Green Jade Civilization to Luo Zheng.
From the looks of it, its choice was very correct. Not only did Luo Zheng not disappoint it, but he also greatly exceeded its expectations. Now, no matter what choice Luo Zheng made, it would contribute unconditionally and silently.
After everyone left the cave, they returned along the path of the Plains of No Return.
The exit of the cave was very close to the Divine River in the Immortal Wasteland. It was only about ten meters away.
Previously, Donghuang was full of confidence that this path would meet the Divine River. If the river met all the way, Donghuang wanted to fish in the Divine River.
However, this cave that led to Clear Jade Heaven was the end of this path. Naturally, the idea of entering the Divine River was dispelled …
But just as everyone walked out of the cave, they saw a strange scene.
The water that flowed in the Divine River was originally emerald green in color. It was the Soul Avoiding Water that could prevent those at the Nirvana Realm from leaving the Nirvana Realm.
The water of the Divine River had always been the same color. Occasionally, when a group of Goldbiters passed by, those little golden fish would reflect the river water into a golden color. Other than that, there were no other changes.
But now, the water of the Divine River had turned dark red. At the same time, a thick smell of blood spread out from the Divine River.
"How did the Divine River become like this …" Donghuang, who was the most interested in the Divine River, said in a daze.
"It's the smell of blood." Nuwa's nose sniffed gently. "Although I don't know what kind of blood it is, it has been in the Divine River for some time. The blood has started to stink …"
Apart from the smell of blood, there was also a rotten smell coming from the divine river.
"Have you ever seen the Divine River like this?" Luo Zheng asked.
Both the black-robed leader and the Immortal Hunters quickly shook their heads.
The Immortal Hunters were quite knowledgeable. They had been in contact with the Divine River for a long time, but they had never seen it before.
"There's a battle upstream," Tian Chuan suddenly said.
Others could not understand Tian Chuan's words, but when Tian Chuan spoke, everyone still listened attentively. After all, this guy was the creator of the world. He knew the whole Chaos very well.
"Does the Upper Realm refer to the Clear Sky Platform?" Luo Zheng asked.
Before this, it was said that the Clear Sky Platform was the real Clear Jade Heaven. The upper reaches of the Divine River must be in Clear Jade Heaven.
However, Tian Chuan shook his head. "Not necessarily. When we first planned the Divine River, we led the Clear Sky Platform's Divine River to some other small Clear Sky Platforms. It took several turns …"
"Small Clear Sky Platforms? These small Clear Sky Platforms are not connected to each other. How did they become the flow of the Divine River? "Luo Zheng was a little confused.
"Of course, we used a special method." Tian Chuan's face showed a proud expression. When it came to his profession, he immediately became eloquent. "It's a method of permanent space integration. Only then can the complete Divine River be embedded …"
Just as Tian Chuan was still introducing the principle, the Blood Lion not far away suddenly said, "Look over there. Something is flowing down the Divine River!"
Everyone immediately looked in the direction the Blood Lion was pointing. Sure enough, there was a huge thing floating on the surface of the blood-colored Divine River. This huge thing was floating on the surface of the Divine River at a very fast speed.
After a certain distance, everyone could see it clearly.
It was the corpse of a giant lying on its side on the surface of the river.
The giant seemed to have been skinned. Half of it was underwater, and the other half was floating on the surface.
The half of its face that was exposed above the surface of the water was soaked and swollen. One of its eyes was glaring angrily. It seemed that the giant was very unwilling to die before it died.
And on this giant's body, there were black crows jumping around, pecking at the giant's rotten flesh.
These crows had only one eye. Although they were small, they revealed an indescribable evil aura. This evil aura was somewhat similar to the treacherous aura, but it was not exactly …
The one-eyed crows discovered Luo Zheng and the others.
The crows seemed unwilling to eat rotten flesh. They were more interested in fresh food.
"Caw!"
One of the crows took the lead and flew up. After flying out of the Divine River, it went straight to Luo Zheng's path.
But just as it flew a few feet away, a mysterious force wrapped around its body. It was dragged forward involuntarily. It could not turn back and went all the way to the depths of the Wasteland of No Return.
The other crows were in a similar situation. After they left the Divine River, they were affected by the Wasteland of No Return. They could only go in the opposite direction and let out a series of caws unwillingly.
Everyone was a little nervous at first. This strange one-eyed crow was probably a creature of the Nirvana Realm from the Jade Clear Sky. Who knew what kind of strange means it had. If it really came here, it would be difficult to deal with it.
Luo Zheng asked Senior Tianchuan if he knew these creatures of the Nirvana Realm. Tianchuan just shook his head.
Although he was a world builder, he usually left to find the next suitable place after building a world. He did not know much about the later development of these worlds. Moreover, he had never experienced the development of the Chaotic World. Naturally, he did not know.
Everyone stopped by the side of the Divine River for a while.
In a short time, six giants floated down with the water. From this, they could roughly infer that the Divine River was dyed red by the blood of these giants.
As for who killed the giants, it was naturally a mystery.
After observing for a while, they followed this path and embarked on the return journey. There was nothing in the Wasteland of No Return, so the return journey was smooth.
But when they reached the snow line and saw a bare brown layer of soil, they were still surprised.

Chapter 3617
"If I remember correctly, this should be a thick snowfield," Donghuang murmured.

The Wing King flapped his wings and flew over the vast snowfield.
Occasionally, a few foreign race creatures would appear from the snow layer below. They looked at him with a fierce look. Perhaps they still wanted to pounce on him.
However, the Wing King was too fast. Before they could take action, the Wing King had turned into a green stream of light and fled far away …
Tian Chuan looked at the snow-white scenery in the distance, and a sad expression appeared on his face. Before entering Shangqing Heaven, Luo Zheng hadn't realized this.
As he understood the Shift group, and saw General Xi of Shi Fang Fu, he suddenly realized that the Yuan Ling Civilization and Wu Kong Clan were not as simple as they appeared on the surface. The actual situation was much more complicated than he had imagined … …
"If that's the case, then you're all alone and without help. It's truly surprising that you were able to reach this stage." Heaven Legend turned around and glanced at the Sempiternal realm cultivators on the eagle's back.

The reason why the Chaos was destroyed was essentially for the Four Spirit Gates to extract energy.
Tianchuan lowered his head and was silent for a while before saying, "If it's really as you say, that the Four Spirit Gates have been destroyed, it's indeed meaningless to let the Chaos continue to be destroyed. I can help you before investigating the truth, but I still have to rescue my colleagues from the Wailing Path and awaken them."
"Leave the awakening to us. I believe it will be very smooth." Luo Zheng revealed a confident smile.
Through the communication with the black-robed leader on the Clear Sky Platform, Luo Zheng and the others already had a preliminary understanding of the Shift.
According to different obsessions, it would lead to different types of Shift. For example, the Shift in the Wailing Path belonged to the type of possession. It was to rely on a certain living creature to go to the Clear Sky Platform to retrieve everything that had happened during its lifetime. If it was successfully completed, it would naturally awaken.
So they only needed to send some living creatures into the Wailing Path and let those living creatures be possessed. Then, they would naturally awaken on their way to the Clear Sky Platform.
Luo Zheng then relayed to Nuwa, Fuxi, and the others, "Senior Tianchuan wants to help his colleagues awaken."
Nuwa and the others were of course clear that Tianchuan's colleagues were the other 199 Shift.
"Help them awaken … squeak." Heaven-Winning Rat King was still thinking, but Nuwa already had a plan in mind. "In that case, I'm afraid we'll have to capture 199 Nirvana creatures and throw them into the Wailing Path."
Fuxi said, "It shouldn't be difficult. Although the Nirvana creatures in the Sanqing Heaven are not to be trifled with, there are still some relatively weak clans after all. As long as we have enough time, isn't the most important thing now to rescue the original human?"
According to the plan formulated by Luo Zheng and Heavenly Lord Yuanshi, after entering the Shangqing Heaven, they should start rescuing the human heroes trapped in the underground cavern. It was just that they were possessed by the Shift by a freak combination of factors, so they took such a big detour.
If they were to rescue the Shift in the Wailing Path now, it would take a lot of time.
As soon as Fuxi finished speaking, aquamarine jade liquid flowed out from the back of the Wing King. This jade liquid slowly formed a human body, which then turned into a human-shaped green jade statue.

After getting everyone's approval, the Wing King sent Luo Zheng and the others to Billion Tower Mountain. Then, it changed its route and flew toward the Blood Swamp alone.

Chapter 3618
In the Clear Jade Sky, there was a huge bottle erected on the Clear Sky Platform that was hundreds of miles long and wide. The bottle was in the shape of a gourd.

Beside the bottle was a wide river that suddenly appeared in the void of the Clear Jade Sky. It crossed the entire Bottle Platform and flowed into the void of the Clear Jade Sky.
This was the famous Divine River.
On both sides of the Divine River, there were huge corpses of giants lying on the side.
Blood flowed out of their bodies and flowed into the drainage ditch beside the Divine River, dyeing the Divine River blood-red.
A fat monk with a smile on his face stood beside the corpses of the giants. The fat monk always had a crescent-shaped smile on his face, looking very kind and friendly. However, the blood on his body gave him a layer of murderous aura.

The fat monk looked like a monk, but in fact, he was a Daoist. He was Daoist Duobao.
The other woman was Fairy Bi Xiao of the Three Firmament Fairies. They were the eldest and youngest disciples of the Tongtian Sect Leader.

The skinned giants in the Clear Jade Sky were terrifyingly strong.
Not to mention facing so many skinned giants, Daoist Duobao could only run away if he faced one.
He was the eldest disciple of the Tongtian Sect Leader. Not only did he have all kinds of treasures, but the bloodline he had obtained from the Immortal Painting was also very magical. He was definitely a top existence among the many Immortal Realm experts.
Fairy Bi Xiao's eyes revealed a hint of worry. "Eldest Senior Brother, Master's strength has indeed increased, but don't you realize that Master seems to be a completely different person when he fights?"
"When he fights with others, he naturally has the determination to kill. Of course, Master will not be as amiable as usual," Daoist Duobao said matter-of-factly.
Unexpectedly, Fairy Bi Xiao still shook her head. "It's not like that. I always feel that Master was possessed by something strange during the battle. Do you still remember how Senior Sister died?"
The Eldest Senior Sister that Fairy Bi Xiao mentioned was the Goddess of Golden Spirit.
When Fairy Bi Xiao mentioned the Goddess of Golden Spirit, Daoist Duobao's expression changed. At the same time, he berated, "Don't mention this matter again!"
The Goddess of Golden Spirit was the second disciple of the Grandmaster of Heaven and the Senior Sister of the Three Xiao Immortals.
This senior sister took care of Yun Xiao like a mother. Qiong Xiao and Bi Xiao also treated the Goddess of Golden Spirit as their mother.
There was once when the Prime of Tongtian encountered a powerful enemy. During the battle, he suddenly lost control and slaughtered his enemy like a madman, forcing his opponent to retreat.
Although his master had won that battle, the Grandmaster of Heaven had accidentally killed the Goddess of Golden Spirit!
That had always been a shadow in Fairy Bi Xiao's heart that she could not get rid of. It was also something that the three sisters had always found difficult to face.
Originally, this matter had been sealed in the depths of Bi Xiao's heart. However, this time, her master suddenly became extremely crazy and killed all the Skinned Giants present with unbelievable methods.
At that time, Bi Xiao was also relatively close to one of the Skinned Giants. If Daoist Duobao had not dragged her away at the critical moment, the Grandmaster of Heaven would have killed her as well.
When Bi Xiao looked into the Grandmaster of Heaven's eyes, she could not help but shiver. At that time, her master did not look like a human at all, but a disgusting treacherous creature.
The battle on the Aquarius Platform had been won, but when she thought of her master's face, she thought of the death of the Goddess of Golden Spirit and felt even more uneasy.
She did not expect that the moment she mentioned it, her Eldest Senior Brother would scold her sternly. Seeing little Shimei's aggrieved appearance, Daoist Duobao, as the eldest Shixiong, felt his heart soften. He then advised, "Master also has Master's troubles. Many things are not as easy as they seem on the surface. How can our Wu Kong Clan compare to
The Essence Spirit Civilization Battle? Master alone, do you think it's possible? "
The outside world saw the Grandmaster of Heaven single-handedly carrying the Wu Kong Clan and fighting against the Elemental Spirit civilization, but very few knew the truth. Even the Three Xiao Fairies did not know the truth.
Bi Xiao pursed her lips and calmed her emotions. She said softly, "I know. No matter what Master becomes, he will always be my master …"
"Splash!"
Daoist Duobao pushed the corpse of a Skinned Giant into the Divine River. Layers of ripples appeared on the surface of the river. He then said with a smile, "Yes, we should not doubt Master."
The Wu Kong Clan had elites from all races. Among them, there were only nine humans, including the Grandmaster of Heaven. They had always been very close.
Under the huge aquarius, there were four red spheres floating.
Inside these red spheres floated powerhouses of all shapes and sizes. These were all powerhouses of other races who believed in the Path of Void. Some were directly the leaders of the Nirvana races.
They held the belief of the Path of Void and wanted to destroy everything within their power. Therefore, they chose to follow the Grandmaster of Heaven.
These four were You Gou of the Moxibustion Clan, Tian Sha of the Tiana Clan, Pan Shen of the Napan Clan, and Fish Spirit King of the Ghost Realm Clan.
Just now, the four powerhouses of other races in the red spheres each used their supernatural powers to try to open this aquarius. However, they spent a lot of effort. The huge aquarius remained motionless. Only a simple Sanskrit word appeared on the body of the aquarius: "Seal." Surrounding this "Seal" word, there were also dense patterns that extended to the entire body of the aquarius.
。
Of course, they knew that the aquarius was so sturdy because of this "Seal" word. However, no one present could break it.

"I didn't expect that after conquering the aquarius platform, this aquarius still can't be opened. How embarrassing …"

Chapter 3621

Daoist Duobao's mouth moved. He didn't intend to hide it and told Bi Xiao everything he knew.
In Supreme Clear Heaven, treachery was undoubtedly the most terrifying existence.
However, the Wu Kong Clan and Elemental Spirit Civilization both knew that the Light of Destruction was treachery's fatal weakness. The easiest way for treachery to avoid the threat of the Light of Destruction was to sneak into a living being's body!
Just like Tian Chuan, who possessed Luo Zheng's body, could use the Light of Destruction to deal with other treachery.
However, Luo Zheng was passively possessed …
Daoist Duobao took the initiative to possess a body. He used treachery as his weapon.
Although Zhao Gongming had asked for the Light of Destruction from his master a few years ago, he was still not satisfied.
The Light of Destruction could deal with ordinary treachery, but it might not be effective against truly powerful treachery.
Moreover, it would be even more difficult to deal with an expert possessed by a treachery.
"Won't one become a walking corpse if possessed by a treachery …" Bi Xiao shook her head.

Their obsession is to help some specific targets. If you encounter these good creatures, they can become your most powerful weapons!"
As Daoist Duobao spoke, he took out two glass lenses. The treachery that possessed his body came out of Daoist Duobao's eyes, ears, mouth, and nose, and returned to the glass lenses.
At this moment, Bi Xiao could distinguish the treachery's form through the glass lenses. She could distinguish the "sickle claw" and "chain" that eldest senior brother had just used.

Daoist Duobao shook his head and said, "It was also Zhao Gongming who killed a follower of the Elemental Spirit civilization and learned this' secret 'from that follower. Then, Zhao Gongming rushed to find Master to' denounce him '."

This is also a way for the good seeds to awaken. Therefore, Master would rather pass this method to God Pan, the wandering dog, and the others than to us."

Most of the Mutated Races and Immortal Realm cultivators paled at the mention of Eerie. Sui Meng.

com

"Hahahahaha, I'm really strong …" Claire let out a series of wild laughter from the Holy Winged Armor.

Chapter 3623

…
Amidst the billions of pagodas.

Chapter 3624

Chapter 3625

"Whoosh!"

Above his head was a passageway leading upwards.

Chapter 3626

As a creature in the Chaos, there were indeed too many limitations.
Luo Zheng's Heavenly Execution Core was already very powerful. It could help Luo Zheng read and understand Sanskrit. It could even help him understand the white light spots in the Mysterious World. It could restore the scene.

After Nuwa gave it a try, the others followed suit.
It was as if the Blood Wolf had released a living creature that looked like a black bat. This black bat was an alien from Shangqing Heaven, and was extremely poisonous. "Whoosh …"

However, its excitement only lasted for the blink of an eye. By the time it reacted, it felt its body being swallowed up and quickly disappeared from the sight of Luo Zheng and the others.
Energy can't pass through …
Even living creatures couldn't pass through …

This time, Luo Zheng and the Honoured Lord of the Origin in the Inner World were depressed, including Nuwa's original body, Fuxi's original body, and the original bodies of all the heroes who were listening. After the core of Tian Zhi translated Tian Chuan's words, their hearts suddenly sank.

"Repair the channel?" Luo Zheng's eyes lit up slightly.
