Luo Zheng asked, "Senior Tian Chuan, what are the things you are talking about?"
He naturally knew that the things that could repair the channel were extraordinary, but he was still somewhat unwilling.
Not only him, but the Honoured Lord of the Origin in the Inner World and the spirits of the original bodies were also looking forward to it. As long as there was a slight chance, they wouldn't give up.
"It's the Seed of Eternal Brightness. It looks like a copper pea," Tian Chuan said. "It was mined in the Main Realm."
"A seed mined in the Main Realm …" Luo Zheng murmured.
In the Inner World, the Honoured Lord of the Origin, Goddess Nuwa, and the others' faces became very ugly.
Anything that involved the Xuanliang World would become extremely complicated, and it could even be said that they were powerless.
"This Seed of Eternal Brightness is very unique. It's actually a kind of metal, but it's also a kind of plant. It's a living metal, and once it grows up, it's very stable." Tian Chuan patted the edge of the channel and said. "When its metal branches are completely unfolded, the hollow part will form a channel, and at the same time, it will produce a huge and flat corolla. You only need to do some simple trimming to quickly complete a Qingtian Platform."
Using the Seed of Eternal Brightness to build the top floor of a world was already a very mature plan at that time, and it was also an essential skill for every world builder.
When Tian Chuan mentioned the metal branches, Luo Zheng's eyebrows trembled slightly. At the same time, he asked, "Senior, how big are the branches of the Seed of Eternal Brightness?"
"When fully grown, it's this big." Tian Chuan pointed to the channel and said. The channel and even the Qingtian Platform were all grown by the Seed of Eternal Brightness.
"But the stem that just sprouted is about this big." Tian Chuan gestured again as he spoke.
"But the whole body is metallic? There are flower branches on it? "Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
His conversation with Tian Chuan was carried out by the group of people in the Inner World. Tian Chuan and the others naturally couldn't hear Luo Zheng's emotions.
But when Luo Zheng transmitted his voice to the Inner World for the core of Tian Zhi to translate, his voice was unusually excited.
Not only him, but the Honoured Lord of the Origin, Goddess Nuwa, and the others' minds were also greatly moved.
Tian Chuan nodded. "Those flower branches also need to be trimmed. You seem to have seen them before?"
A blue square frame appeared on Luo Zheng's right hand. He reached out and pinched the square frame lightly, taking a metal flower branch in front of Tian Chuan. "Senior Tian Chuan, are you talking about this metal flower branch?"
Not long after entering the Nirvana Realm, this metal flower branch was obtained in the Temple of Helianthus.
Luo Zheng once showed the metal flower branch to the Honoured Lord of the Origin and the others, but none of them recognized it.
Tian Chuan glanced at the metal flower branch and took it from Luo Zheng's hands. There wasn't much surprise on his face. "We have at least two thousand Qingtian Platforms yet to be completed. Two thousand Seeds of Eternal Brightness have already sprouted and grown into flower branches like this …"
These metal flower branches were extremely exquisite in appearance. In addition, they didn't have any energy reaction, so they couldn't be used as Nirvana tokens. This made people feel that this item had some kind of magical characteristics waiting to be discovered.
But without the world builders, they couldn't have grown into Qingtian Platforms.
At the same time, these metal flower branches couldn't be destroyed by ordinary means. For more than a thousand Divine Epochs, these metal flower branches had been circulating in the Nirvana Realm. Luo Zheng only came across one of them.
The Fearful Eye Demon Clan also obtained this metal flower branch by chance. Like countless Nirvana Realm clans, they studied it for a long time but couldn't find the main point. In the end, they hid it in the Temple of Helianthus as a useless item. Later, Luo Zheng obtained it.

Chapter 3627
The Heavenly Rat King stared at the metal flower branch for a while and finally couldn't help but ask, "Luo Zheng, what's the use of this thing?"
Donghuang, Nuwa, and the Immortal Hunters were also curious.
After all, they couldn't understand the conversation between Luo Zheng and Tianchuan.
"This metal flower branch can be used to build the Clear Sky Platform. It should also be able to repair this passage," Luo Zheng answered.
"Really?" Fuxi immediately became excited.
"There's always a way out!" Nuwa smiled faintly, as if she wasn't surprised by Luo Zheng's good luck.
"Squeak, I've traded two of this thing before," the Heavenly Rat King said disapprovingly. "I always thought it was useless. It wasn't easy to sell it."
Everyone was speechless at the Heavenly Rat King's words.
Luo Zheng was naturally a little surprised, but he quickly understood.
Although the metal flower branch was extraordinary, according to Tianchuan, 2,000 Seeds of Eternal Brightness had grown into metal flower branches, but they hadn't grown into the Clear Sky Platform.
These metal flower branches were difficult to destroy, so they naturally flowed around the Nirvana Realm.
Some Nirvana Realm creatures couldn't decipher the mystery of the metal flower branches after trading, so they had no choice but to trade them away. It wasn't strange that the Heavenly Rat King could get this thing.
The next task was to repair the Clear Sky Platform, but everyone had to leave the passage.
Tian Chuan then brought everyone along the passageway and sneaked back. When they reached the bottom, the entrance had disappeared.
"Boil the water," Tianchuan said.
This time, it wasn't Zhurong's turn to do it. Blood Wolf said, "Let me do it!"
After saying that, he spread out his hands, and blood-red flames bloomed around his body.
Gurgle …
In the blink of an eye, the exit that had disappeared reappeared.
After everyone went in, they returned to the underground kiln. The pool in the underground kiln was connected to the passage, so the water in the pool was naturally boiling.
"Luo Zheng, bring your companions out of the pool," Tianchuan ordered.
Luo Zheng nodded and asked everyone to leave the pool.
When the last person left, Tianchuan stood on the water with an unusually serious expression.
Building a world …
This was Tianchuan's old profession.
He hadn't done it for many years.
"Hua!"
He placed one hand in the pool and began to stir gently.
The seemingly careless paddling caused the entire pool to begin to churn.
"Wow! Hua! "
Very quickly, a huge whirlpool was formed inside the pool, and the whirlpool was concentrated at the entrance to the bottom of the pool!
At the same time, Tian Chuan raised the metal flower branch high into the air.
He stretched out his hand and grabbed at the side of the metal flower. Without touching the metal flower, the entire flower suddenly began to shake.
After that, Tian Chuan's expression became even more serious. He gently plucked the metal flower branch, and a constantly pulsating white light spot suddenly appeared at the bottom of the metal flower branch!
"This light spot …" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
Logically speaking, white dots of light should not appear in the Chaos World.
The only exception in the Chaos World was the Cold Prison. The Cold Prison was a phenomenon caused by extreme low temperatures.
But now, the metal flower branch had broken this rule, which naturally surprised Luo Zheng.
As Tianchuan's hand continued to "pluck", light spots appeared one after another. After these light spots fell into the whirlpool in the center of the pool, they were pulled into the entrance of the whirlpool and drilled into the passage of the Clear Jade Sky!
Others couldn't understand these light spots, but Luo Zheng could.
After the core of Tian Zhi continuously deciphered these light spots, the image was transmitted to Luo Zheng's eyes. Luo Zheng saw that one end of the metal flower branch had rapidly grown, spreading into the water, and finally drilled into the entrance at the bottom of the pool.
"Hua hua hua …"
The water in the pool was spinning faster and faster, and the metal flower branch was extending more and more branches into the pool.
What Sheng Tian and Rat King saw was naturally more and more white dots of light!
In the passage of the Clear Jade Sky, earth-shaking changes were taking place.
After the white dots drilled into the Clear Jade Sky, they quickly formed a huge metal branch, and this metal branch was still growing vigorously. It quickly drilled through the broken part of the passage and grew all the way to the Clear Sky Platform at the top!
Then the metal branch became thicker and thicker, gradually filling up the entire passage.
"Ka, ka …"
Under the expansion of the metal branch, cracks kept appearing on the surface of the old passage, and the fragments under the cracks were quickly peeling off and collapsing …
When the two disconnected passages collapsed and disappeared, a new and shiny metal branch appeared!
The metal branch in Tianchuan's hand didn't release any white dots of light, but the flower bud at the top of the metal branch was shining with a bright red light, as if it was going to bloom at any time.
Once this flower bud bloomed, it would unfold and form a huge Clear Sky Platform.
If the Clear Sky Platform unfolded here, it would squeeze everyone present to death.
Tianchuan carefully dealt with it and sealed the flower bud. The bright red light disappeared, and was replaced by a dead gray color.
"Luo Zheng, can you give me the rest of the metal branch?" Tianchuan requested.
Luo Zheng smiled slightly, "These metal branches originally belonged to Senior Tianchuan, so they should be returned to their original owner. Senior doesn't need to ask for my permission."
Tianchuan smiled and nodded. After putting away the metal branch, he said, "The repairs have been completed. You can go in now."
Everyone jumped into the water again and swam into the passage following the red path in the pool.
The size and width of the passage were exactly the same as before, but the inner wall of the passage looked very new. After all, it had just "grown".
"Squeak! Why is this new passage also filled with water? "Victorious Sky Rat King asked while cruising upwards.
Nuwa speculated, "Perhaps the end of this passage is connected to a water source. After the new passage was formed, the water in the water source naturally flowed down quickly and filled the entire passage."
Victorious Sky Rat King was somewhat skeptical. When it swam to the end of the passage with everyone, it was actually another brand new body of water.
Everyone floated up from the bottom of the body of water, and with a splash, they drilled out of the water. Everyone's eyes widened.
Just as Nuwa said, they were in the middle of a lake.
Night Vision Island was an extremely huge Clear Sky Platform. Although it wasn't as huge as the main Clear Sky Platform, it was thousands of times bigger than the Clear Sky Platform that Luo Zheng and the others had been to before. The lake in the middle of Night Vision Island occupied more than half of its area.
Surrounding the lake was a circle of grey-white mountain ranges. In the dim light of the night, these mountain ranges seemed like demons and ghosts lying on the ground, emitting a silent and strange aura.
Although this wasn't the main Clear Sky Platform, it was still within the scope of Jade Clear Sky.
Based on the information obtained so far, the treacherous things should have been passed down from Jade Clear Sky.
It wasn't surprising that this Night Vision Island had some powerful treacherous things.
After everyone left the water, they flew close to the mirror-like surface of the lake. Everyone, including Tianchuan, was very careful to avoid making any noise.

Chapter 3628
Luo Zheng flew at the front of the team. When he was close to the lakeside, he spread out his Divine Sense.
Divine Sense, as a means of exploration, could easily depict things in the distance. Even things hidden underground couldn't avoid it.
But when Luo Zheng's Divine Sense swept over the lakeside terrain, a strange pattern suddenly appeared in his mind.
It was a circle with ten animal teeth hanging around it!
"Ka!"
The circle rotated fiercely, and the animal teeth began to flash. Luo Zheng felt a sharp pain in his mind, which almost made him groan.
Nuwa, Fuxi, Donghuang, and the three Immortal Hunters also released their Divine Sense like Luo Zheng. They also saw the circle pattern and felt the sharp pain.
"Ugh …"

Under normal circumstances, Divine Sense could be unimpeded, but some special things could block Divine Sense. When Divine Sense bounced back, it could only observe a flat surface.
There were also some even more powerful things that could devour Divine Sense, causing the Divine Sense that was released to vanish without a trace.
This time, his Divine Sense didn't bounce back, nor did it disappear. However, the image his Divine Sense detected was the ring inlaid with teeth.
This was very strange, as if someone deliberately let them see this thing.
Everyone discussed in a low voice but couldn't come to a conclusion.
Luo Zheng asked Tianchuan. Tianchuan was also at a loss. He only said, "Night Vision Island is a relatively large Clear Sky Platform. We didn't set anything up on it. I've never heard of the ability to affect perception you mentioned …"
As a world builder, Tianchuan was very familiar with the Chaotic World and even other worlds, but his knowledge was limited to this.
"I'll go ashore first. You guys follow me," Luo Zheng suggested.
Everyone present had also witnessed Luo Zheng's nearly indestructible vitality. They all nodded in agreement.
Luo Zheng's figure flashed and he was already standing on a rock by the shore.
Boom!
The moment Luo Zheng's feet stepped on the rock, he felt a heavy impact in his ears. His head felt as if it was hit by a heavy wooden mallet. The moment his mind went blank, the ring with teeth appeared again!
"What's going on …" Luo Zheng frowned.
Although it was strange, Luo Zheng still transmitted his voice to Nuwa. "Goddess Nuwa, you can come over now. Be careful when you land. That seems to be a strange mental attack …"
Nuwa nodded and brought Fuxi and the others ashore.
Boom!
Nuwa had just landed on the ground when she was hit by a heavy hammer. Her face turned pale for a moment, but she soon recovered.
It was the same for Donghuang, Elder Ying, and the others. However, Rat King Sheng Tian and Fuxi's reactions were the most severe. They directly fell to the ground.
Only Tian Chuan felt nothing when he stepped on the ground.
"I see that ring again …"
"There is no mental attack nearby. What is it?"
"…"
Everyone's face became more worried, especially the three Immortal Hunters.
They had been in an environment where they had to be highly alert all year round. If they were careless, they would lose their lives. Generally, when they encountered such an inexplicable phenomenon, they would raise their vigilance greatly.
"Senior Yuanshi Tianzun, try to communicate with the Instrument of Civilization again," Luo Zheng said to Yuanshi Tianzun in his Inner World.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven was ready. As soon as Luo Zheng gave the order, he appeared above the pages of the book. As the pages of the book flipped open, the Weapon of Civilization tried to contact the true bodies of the human heroes.
The souls of Nuwa, Fuxi, Emperor Jun, Zhurong, and the other original bodies also gathered around the page. They all wanted to know the result as soon as possible.
Whoosh, whoosh …
The page stopped, and the page glowed with green light.
The souls of the original bodies immediately connected to the page, trying to receive the information from the page.
Boom!
The reactions of these souls were exactly the same as Luo Zheng's. They were also attacked and saw the ring with ten teeth …
Although the souls of the souls still had their supernatural powers, they were very weak in the soul level. A powerful soul attack was enough to seriously injure them.
However, the attack of the "ring" didn't cause any harm. It just made people feel pain.
Although the souls of the original bodies covered their heads and cried for a while, they didn't suffer any damage.
"Even the Instrument of Civilization was affected …" Luo Zheng was a little speechless.
"The Night Island should be covered by some kind of magical power. You can only explore it with your physical bodies," Yuanshi Tianzun suggested.
Luo Zheng nodded. Since they were already here, they couldn't give up just because their Divine Sense couldn't explore it.
Although the Night Island was not small, they could always find some clues along the mountain range.
After Luo Zheng came to the shore of the lake, there was a gray forest in front of him.
Previously, when he was on the lake, he had seen that everything on the Night Vision Island was grayish-white. Now, even the forest was the same color. Out of curiosity, he went forward and plucked a leaf. As soon as he plucked the leaf, countless grayish-white ashes rustled down, revealing a large patch of emerald green leaves!
It turned out that these trees were not gray and white, but just covered with a layer of ash …
"Are there volcanoes on the Night Island?" Nuwa's eyes narrowed.
If there were frequent volcanic eruptions in these ring-shaped mountains, the eruption of volcanic ash could cover the entire Night Island.

"Maybe for some reason, dust fell on the Night Island …" Blood Crow inferred, touching his nose.

Luo Zheng and others carefully observed, and it was really as Blood Wolf said.
Except for flowers and trees, there was no such layer of dust on the rocks and ground.
"This is very strange." Nuwa's brows were tightly knitted together. "Either the dust comes from the plants themselves, or it was sprinkled by someone …"
Just when everyone was puzzled, Luo Zheng suddenly saw a white claw sticking out of the tree. The claw held a handful of ash and carefully sprinkled it on the leaves.
Luo Zheng just brushed off the dust on these leaves.

Chapter 3629
"Rustle, rustle, rustle …"

As a native of this place, Treacherous Sloth was much more familiar with the environment than they were. Moreover, it was immortal and did not have much intelligence. There should be very few things that could make it afraid. Nuwa made her judgment based on this point.

Treacherously, the sloth noticed Luo Zheng's vigilance. It pointed at the sky again, and a trace of sincerity appeared in its hollow eye sockets. It grabbed the dust and walked towards Luo Zheng.
At this moment, an arrow suddenly flew out from not far away.
"Whoosh —"
The speed of the arrow was surprisingly fast, and it was firmly nailed in front of the sloth.

"Whoosh! Whoosh! "

Treacherously moved around in the forest, completely hiding itself.
Although these arrows were not aimed at Luo Zheng and the others, Luo Zheng and the others were also vigilant.

As soon as Fuxi finished speaking, a short alien walked out of the forest not far away. This alien looked very similar to a human child. Its face was wide and big, and it looked charmingly naive. The strange thing was that it had a long green tail. The end of the tail was flat and green, like a leaf hanging above its head.
"Don't be afraid. I've already chased Treacherously away," said the alien.
When everyone heard the standard human language, their tensed bodies relaxed a little.
Night Vision Island looked very strange. Although no one had died, in this strange environment, if they were not careful, they would have been completely wiped out.
If someone could be a guide to solve their problems, it would be best …
The alien in front of them not only helped them chase away Treacherously, but also had a kind face. Naturally, it brought hope to everyone.
"Thank you for your help, sir." Fuxi cupped his fists and thanked the child-like alien.
The smile on the alien's broad face was a little naive. "You're welcome," it said as it moved closer. After sniffing with its small nose, it said, "Your smell is very strange. It seems like you came from outside?"

"Ghost Medicine Powder? What's the use of it? "Nuwa asked again.
Cando's face showed a look of horror. "Every night, something will come down from the sky. They will eat the living things sprinkled with Ghost Medicine Powder!"
Hearing this, the faces of Rat King and the others changed slightly.
Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
The three Immortal Hunters exchanged glances with each other.
Nuwa, on the other hand, was not surprised. She continued to ask, "Those things you mentioned are also Treacherously?"
"Yes, they are very powerful Treacherously, Hatred Level Treacherously!" Cando said again.
"Hatred Level Treacherously!" Donghuang's face also showed a trace of horror.
"Squeak …" Rat King's soybean eyes were full of fear.
On the Clear Sky Platform before, they faced a Resentment Level Treacherously. If it weren't for Luo Zheng's last burst of power, they would have died long ago.
They didn't even have the strength to fight back when they encountered a Resentment Level Treacherously. If it were a Hatred Level Treacherously, they would have no room to resist at all.
Rat King, Elder Ying, Donghuang, and the others all took a few steps back.
They were relatively close to the woods, and some Ghost Medicine Powder was sprinkled on the ground. Since this thing can attract Hatred Level Treacherously, they were afraid that it would be too late to avoid it.
"Hatred Level Treacherously attack living things sprinkled with Ghost Medicine Powder. Then, as long as we don't have Ghost Medicine Powder on us, we should be fine, right?" Luo Zheng asked after thinking for a while.

Chapter 3630
"You are outsiders. Of course, you don't know the cruelty of Yatsumi Island. Generally, outsiders don't survive the night!" Cang Duo's face was slightly pale. Although the Clear Sky Platform on Yatsumi Island is many times larger than the previous one, it is still very small compared to Shangqing Sky.
In the Shangqing Heavens, the Vengeance Level Treacherous Beings and the Resentment Level Treacherous Beings could turn the heavens and the earth upside down. If this small island had so many Treacherous Beings, one could imagine how dangerous it was!
Blood Wolf has been thinking about how to persuade others to return to Shangqing Sky. Even if they can't retreat, they have to leave with Blood Lion and Blood Crow.
Not to mention anything else, as long as a Hatred Level Shifter appears, everyone will die. Staying here and continuing to explore is no different from courting death.

There are indeed some Aliens in Nirvana who are very kind and willing to help others. This Cang Duo seems to be a trustworthy person, but there are many loopholes in his words.
For example, it said that Konoha people often help outsiders, but the Night Island was originally an isolated island, and there was no way to enter it.
The only passageway was the one that they had just repaired. How could there be outsiders before this?
For example, if the Ghost Medicine Powder was applied to living things, it would attract things from the heavens. Were the plants on the island considered living things? If that was the case, why were all the plants on the island fine?
Moreover, Cang Duo seemed to be used to the treacherous Tianchuan and did not find it strange at all.

Not all Eerie Creatures were innately strong. Some Eerie Creatures were indeed weak.

Then the broken hand continued to shake the leaves, shaking off the Ghost Medicine Powder on the leaves and branches, and putting it into the Sumeru Space.

The Sloths kept sprinkling the Ghost Medicine Powder, but it was all absorbed by the Sumeru Space.
Although they felt strange, why couldn't the Ghost Medicine Powder be sprinkled on the hand, they didn't feel that there was anything wrong with it. Instead, they were very persistent and completely emptied their pockets of Ghost Medicine Powder before turning away.
It didn't take long for Blood Wolf to empty the Ghost Medicine Powder of the Sloths. After collecting almost all the Ghost Medicine Powder, the hand once again turned into a spider silk and quickly retracted back into Blood Wolf's body.
Everything was done secretly.
After a while, everyone arrived at the nearest mountain.

Chapter 3631
When Luo Zheng and his party approached, the hatred and greed on the faces of these Konoha people were swept away.

"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.

Although Cang Duo denied it immediately, Luo Zheng was keenly aware that when he mentioned the "human race", Cang Duo's eyes flashed with fear.
Clan Leader Gu Duo seized the opportunity and said, "Night is about to fall, and we still have to make many preparations. Everyone, please rest here first. If you have any questions, we can discuss them tomorrow …"
After saying that, Gu Duo took Cang Duo away, leaving only Luo Zheng's group in the cave.
After the two Konoha clansmen left the cave, Fuxi, Old Ying, and the Immortal Hunters' expressions became slightly cold.
"It seems that Cang Duo knows about the existence of the human race. He has seen a human, at least he has seen a human," Fuxi said.
"The human race makes him very afraid. What is the reason?" Donghuang was a little confused.
"Squeak! These guys' acting is clumsy, "Rat King sneered.
"Everything is clumsy," Blood Wolf sniffed his nose and sneered. "The fragrance in the cave can't cover up the strong smell of blood. This cave should be a slaughterhouse, right?"
With their keen sense of smell, they could smell the stench of blood as soon as they came in.
This cave was wide on the outside and narrow on the inside, and the exit was facing the sky. It was not suitable for a hiding place at all.
After carefully observing, Nuwa said, "This should not be a slaughterhouse."
"Then what is it?" Blood Lion glanced at Nuwa. Among this group, this woman was the smartest.
"These Konoha clansmen are not good at deceiving people, so they are full of loopholes. I guess' Cang Duo 'saw that we have a large number of people and temporarily decided to lure us here. He didn't have time to arrange, so he arranged us here," Nuwa deduced, "This cave should be a place to store prey."
"That's also a slaughterhouse," Blood Wolf shrugged his shoulders.
Nuwa pointed to the entrance of the cave and said, "Don't forget, whether it is the sloth or Cang Duo, it means that there are monsters falling from the sky. Can you think about the use of this cave again?"
Luo Zheng pondered and said, "Goddess Nuwa means that Konoha clansmen throw prey in this cave to sacrifice to the monsters in the sky?"
"Yes," Nuwa nodded.
"Why sacrifice to the monsters?" Blood Crow asked.
Nuwa smiled slightly, "It's very simple, to seek shelter."
The Konoha clansmen don't seem to be strong. On this island, the Konoha clansmen are basically at the bottom. There must be a special reason for their survival.
"If they are just seeking shelter, they can hunt weaker creatures and sacrifice them. The risk of deceiving us is not small," Luo Zheng questioned.
Luo Zheng and his party don't look weak.
In case this matter is exposed, they will definitely retaliate against the Konoha clan.
Nuwa thought for a moment and added, "Maybe by sacrificing more powerful prey, they can get some kind of valuable reward, so they are willing to take this risk."
Donghuang nodded, "Goddess Nuwa's inference is very reasonable, but what should we do next?"
"Since this place is a trap, we should leave as soon as possible," Old Ying suggested.
However, Nuwa said, "This is just my one-sided inference. Maybe the situation is completely opposite. The wild is more dangerous. My advice is to stay here and act according to the circumstances!"
…
In a circular cave on the far right of the Konoha clan.
Gu Duo's wrinkled face showed anger, "Why did you lead these people in! We don't lack prey to sacrifice. Have you forgotten the fate of the Thunder Clan and the Byakugu clan? Do you want to kill us? "
As the Patriarch of the Konoha clan, he has seen the terrible strength of that person.
"These humans have nothing to do with that person. They are not strong enough and don't know anything. They are the best prey!" Cang Duo's face showed a fierce expression.
"If that person knows, our Konoha clan will be wiped out directly," Gu Duo's face was a little stiff, he knew that this was an unforgivable mistake.
Cang Duo's face was full of confidence, "They don't know anything about this island. They won't survive tonight. As long as they die tonight, that person can't know."
"But …" Gu Duo was still a little hesitant.
"Don't forget the Evil Tiger clan from the North Mountain. They won't let us go. We are also doing this for the continuation of our clan," Cang Duo said.
Under Cang Duo's persuasion, Gu Duo's stiff face eased.
If these people are sacrificed, it will be a great harvest for the Konoha clan. Some risks are worth it. After all, the Konoha clan has no way out.

Chapter 3632
Night began to fall on the Yami Island.
After the light dimmed, the light spots in the Xuanliang World became more and more like stars.
The people of Konoha went back to their caves.
In the large cave above, Blood Wolf took out a bunch of grayish-white ghost medicine powder and gave everyone a share.
Although the ghost medicine powder had been distributed, they were not in a hurry to apply it.
The Rat King stood at the entrance of the cave, and a huge eyeball floated beside it. This was the Sky Eye of the Sky Eye Rock Vine. When necessary, the Sky Eye and its vine body could be separated.
Now, the Divine Sense could not detect the distant scene, but the Sky Eye could.
A vine of the Sky Eye was placed on the forehead of the Rat King. Standing on the side of the cave, it could observe the top of the mountain.
Half an hour ago, the Blood Wolf turned into a strand of spider silk and quietly left the cave. It climbed all the way to the top of the mountain and took out two living creatures from its spirit beast bag. They were two sturdy Kuai Bulls.
Although Kuai Bulls were also creatures of the Nirvana Realm, their spiritual intelligence was low and they had no magical powers. Instead, their meat was extremely delicious. Blood Wolf caught Kuai Bulls as food.
In order to prevent Kuai Bulls from running around, Blood Wolf used some means to knock them out and fix them on the top of the mountain. It sprinkled the ghost medicine powder evenly on each Kuai Bull until it became a "white bull". Then, it quietly returned to the cave.
Now, through the Sky Eye, the Rat King could see the two motionless Kuai Bulls on the top of the mountain. One was black and the other was white.
"Phew …"
When the light on the Night Vision Island dimmed to a certain degree, it was as if a door had been opened in a corner of the island. The unpleasant aura rushed out of the door and covered the entire Night Vision Island.
The Rat King's soybean eyes revealed a glimmer of light. "Squeak, the aura of Treacherous …"
Everyone in the cave was meditating, but the moment the aura came, they opened their eyes.
Fuxi turned over and walked to the entrance of the cave and asked, "Did you see anything?"
"Not yet, squeak!" The Rat King shook its head.
Although it did not see Shift itself, Heavenly Rat King still felt a little scared. A Resentment Shift did not have the ability to cover the entire island with its aura. This aura was most likely released by a Hatred Shift.
"When will you let me return to the Spirit Beast Pouch?" The Heaven's Eye Vine asked. This terrifying aura made it feel uneasy.
It had originally thought that these fellows had successfully entered the Shang Clear Sky. Who would have thought that they would suddenly run into the Jade Clear Sky? This terrifying world was clearly not one that the Heaven's Eye Vine was willing to stay in.
"Squeak, it's still early …"
Just as the Heaven Victorious Rat King was speaking, its soybean-sized eyes suddenly widened to the size of peas, as if it had seen an unbelievable scene.
His Heaven Eye's pupils were also wide open.
"What did you see?" Fuxi raised his eyebrows.
"Something is falling from the sky. It's white. It looks like … fur?" Heaven Winning Rat King said.
They were strands of white hair that were rustling down, emitting a faint white light at night. When these white hairs accumulated to a certain extent, they fused together to form a humanoid white-haired monster.
The white-haired monster had clearly discovered Kuai Niu and was crawling towards him.
When the white-haired monster passed by the "white bull", it hesitated for a moment, but quickly gave up on the "white bull" and quickly walked towards the black bull.
The Heaven Victorious Rat King's eyes stared fixedly at this scene. The moment the white-haired monster touched the black bull, the black bull's head disappeared, as if it had been directly swallowed by the humanoid white-haired monster.
Blood from the black bull's neck splashed onto the white-haired monster's body. When the blood touched the white-haired monster, it directly disappeared, not dyeing the white-haired monster red. Clearly, the blood had also been swallowed by the white-haired monster.
As the white-furred monster continued to move forward, a huge black bull was completely devoured.
"Hua!"
Following that, the white-haired monster suddenly dispersed, turning into strands of white hair that scattered on the ground.
These strands of white hair gradually disappeared, but the Heaven Victorious Rat King saw a few transparent crystals. These crystals did not disappear along with the white hair!
"What are these crystals …"
Just as the Heaven Victorious Rat King's interest was piqued, white hair rustled about ten feet away from it.
At close range, it looked like pure winter snow. Although it was very beautiful, it was fatal.
Only then did the Heaven Victorious Rat King remember its mission. It grabbed the spirit beast pouch and gently put it back into the spirit beast pouch. It hurriedly turned around and returned to the cave. "Squeak! Everyone, quickly … quickly apply the Ghost Medicine Powder! "
"Is the black bull dead?" Blood Wolf asked.
"Squeak! Yes! "The Heaven Victorious Rat King nodded as it applied the Ghost Medicine Powder on its body.
Luo Zheng, Nuwa, the Immortal Hunters, and even Tian Chuan applied a layer of Ghost Medicine Powder all over their bodies.
Luo Zheng wasn't sure if Shift could swallow it, but they didn't lack Ghost Medicine Powder. Naturally, it was safer.
Just as they finished applying the powder, four humanoid white-haired monsters walked into the cave.
These humanoid white-haired monsters entered the cave and looked around for something.
These white-haired monsters emitted a threatening aura. Everyone present resisted the urge to run away.
After searching for a while, one of the white-haired monsters suddenly turned around and looked at Luo Zheng. It strode toward Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng frowned. His body didn't move, but every muscle in his body was tense.
Although he didn't know how to deal with this thing, he definitely wouldn't sit still and wait for death.
After the white-haired monster approached Luo Zheng, it stretched out its hand, brushed past Luo Zheng's ear, and slapped the wall with one hand.
Luo Zheng turned his head slightly and realized that its target was a plant that had just sprouted on the wall!
No wonder Shift sprinkled the Ghost Medicine Powder on the tree. This white-haired monster's diet included plants!
The white-haired monsters searched every corner of the cave. After confirming that there was no "living thing" to be swallowed, they collapsed with a bang, turning into a pile of messy white hair and gradually disappearing.
"Phew," Donghuang exhaled. "We've dealt with it …"
"Squeak! Those white-haired monsters won't come back, "Sky Winning Rat King said.
Nuwa frowned and thought for a moment. "They rely on the power of life to find prey, right? After applying the Ghost Medicine Powder, you all look like dead people … "
How sensitive were the Immortal Realm experts present?
However, they were too nervous today because of Shift's matter. After Nuwa's reminder, they realized that the Ghost Medicine Powder's Ghost Medicine Powder completely blocked off all life.
The Ghost Medicine Powder had almost completely blocked off any signs of life. If a person who had been smeared with the Ghost Medicine Powder lay on the ground without moving, then he would be a lifeless corpse!
"That should be the case." Blood Wolf nodded.
Nuwa thought for a moment and said, "Let me try again. Please don't shake off the Ghost Medicine Powder on your body …"
"Don't …" Blood Wolf had just opened his mouth to stop her when Nuwa had already cleaned off the Ghost Medicine Powder on the back of her hand, revealing her white and delicate skin.
As soon as the Ghost Medicine Powder on the back of her hand was cleaned off, pieces of white hair slowly began to pile up outside the cave. The white-haired monsters appeared again and went straight for Nuwa.
Nuwa's expression was calm. She didn't even rush to apply the Ghost Medicine Powder. Instead, she raised her hand and slowly moved back, luring the white-haired monsters to move with her!
"She's crazy …" Blood Wolf muttered helplessly in his heart.

Chapter 3633
Through careful observation, Nuwa naturally has her own sense of propriety.
"This thing has no vision, it can only track the life force …" Nuwa said as she applied a layer of Ghost Medicine Powder on her hand.
The white-haired monster, which was originally following Nuwa, suddenly lost its target and sniffed around like a headless fly.
Nuwa's eyes flashed with a touch of contemplation, "The aliens on the island know how to use Ghost Medicine Powder, so the white-haired monster shouldn't be a threat to them. So, Konoha's people took the initiative to sacrifice their prey to the white-haired monster, there should be obvious benefits. What benefits?"
"It's hard to guess," Luo Zheng shook his head, "Maybe after sacrificing their prey, they can go somewhere to receive the so-called 'benefits'."
There are too many possibilities for this kind of thing, and it's difficult to infer with limited clues.
"Squeak! By the way, when I used my Heavenly Eye to observe Kuai Niu, the white-haired monster swallowed a Kuai Niu and left some transparent crystal behind, "Sky Winning Rat King suddenly reminded.
Just now, he was busy applying the Ghost Medicine Powder and neglected this point.
Nuwa immediately looked at Blood Wolf and asked, "Blood Wolf, do you have any more Kuai Niu in your Spirit Beast bag?"
"Yes," Blood Wolf nodded, he naturally knew what Nuwa wanted to do, so he took out a Kuai Niu from the Spirit Beast bag without asking much.
This Kuai Niu is also a living creature. After leaving the Spirit Beast bag, it felt the treacherous aura of the white-haired monster. It was immediately scared and wanted to run away.
Blood Wolf pointed at the Kuai Niu's head, and it immediately fainted.
The white-haired monster was about to leave, but after feeling the life force of the Kuai Niu, it immediately turned around and rushed over, swallowing the Kuai Niu in a few seconds, then it turned into a pile of white hair and disappeared.
"Ding ding ding …"
At the same time that the white-haired monster disappeared, several glass crystals fell to the ground with a crisp sound.
These glass crystals are thin and long, like long transparent needles.
Nuwa stepped forward and picked up the crystals one by one, then placed them in the palm of her hand and looked at them carefully. These crystals gave her an uncomfortable feeling, as if they were part of the treacherous aura.
"This should be the benefit that the Konoha clansmen want. If they devour us, the number of crystals they will get should be a lot more," Nuwa said and handed the crystal to Fuxi.
Fuxi put it in front of him and sniffed it. After observing it for a while, he couldn't find anything special about it. So, he passed it to Donghuang, and Donghuang passed it to Elder Ying.
After making a circle, the crystal fell into Luo Zheng's hand. Just as Luo Zheng was carefully sizing it up, Tian Chuan, who was next to him, said, "Can I have a look?"
Tian Chuan had a Treacherous Body, so his perception was completely different.
When Luo Zheng placed these crystals in Tianchuan's hand, Tianchuan clenched his hand into a fist, completely wrapping the crystals in it. Then, he closed his eyes and stood still.
Tian Chuan's originally neat and tidy eyes, ears, mouth, and nose began to melt like a candle. His humanoid body quickly turned into a white, translucent mud-like substance, and the armor he was wearing scattered all over the ground.
"Senior Tianchuan!" Luo Zheng hurriedly shouted.
"What happened to him?"
"Why is it like this …"
"Are these crystals also used to deal with the Treacherous Body?"
The others also showed anxious looks on their faces.
Although Tian Chuan was only an Enemy Level Treacherous Body, he was the World Creator!
His understanding of the entire Chaos far surpassed others. If he could stand with everyone, it would naturally be a great help.
If something happened to him because of a few unknown crystals, then it was not worth it.
"Look, those crystals …" Rat King Sheng pointed at Tian Chuan and said.
The previous few crystals had already drilled into Tian Chuan's body and were completely wrapped up.
"Let's wait and see. Maybe the situation is not as bad as we think," Nuwa said.
After all, Tian Chuan's Treacherous Body had only "melted" and was not corroded. With the Treacherous Body's immortal characteristics, as long as it could be restored, there would not be any major problems.
After about the time it took for three incense sticks to burn, Tian Chuan's body began to shake again. His head grew out of the transparent white mud bit by bit, and his body returned to human form under everyone's gaze.
"Senior Tianchuan, what happened to you just now?" Luo Zheng asked.
Tian Chuan stretched out his hand, and the few crystals were still in his hand. He said, "These things seem to be my home. I feel very comfortable and want to sleep in them …"
As a unique life form, the Treacherous Body was naturally completely different from ordinary creatures.
When Tian Chuan held these crystals, he felt a sense of sleepiness. These crystals seemed to be his bed.
Tian Chuan had just turned into a pool of mud when he fell asleep for a short while.
Tian Chuan handed the crystals to Luo Zheng, and Luo Zheng relayed Tian Chuan's words to the others.
"Sleep …" Donghuang frowned slightly.
Sleep was something that only weak existences needed.
A soul that had undergone a little tempering would never need it.
The Treacherous Body was undoubtedly a more powerful and special soul, but it actually needed to sleep. This made Donghuang even more puzzled.
"Maybe this thing can just hypnotize the Treacherous Body?" Blood Lion speculated.
Nuwa stared at the crystal in Luo Zheng's hand and frowned, "Senior Tianchuan is an awakened Treacherous Body, and he can't resist this crystal. What if it's not awakened?"
Whether it's a possessor or a slaughterer, these Treacherous Bodies rely on their obsession to act and don't have much judgment.
If these Treacherous Bodies encounter such a crystal, won't they always sleep?
"Maybe it can be used to trap the Treacherous Body," Blood Wolf said.
Nuwa nodded slightly, but her brows were still locked. She felt that these crystals had more than just this.
"We don't have to think too much. The mystery will naturally be revealed at dawn," Blood Crow suddenly said.
Hearing this, Nuwa's mouth curled slightly and sneered, "That's true!"
They were not cruel people, but since the Konoha clansmen dared to sacrifice their lives, naturally, they couldn't be blamed.
The night on the Yami Island lasted for ten hours.
After they applied the Ghost Medicine Powder, no other white-haired monsters broke into the cave that night.
The Rat King once again took out the Heavenly Eye from the Spirit Beast bag and observed the movement outside.
Occasionally, he heard the screams of some foreign creatures. The Rat King looked around and saw the white-haired monsters descending on other mountains, devouring the sacrificed creatures.
Ten hours later, the sky gradually brightened.
The unpleasant atmosphere that had been lingering around everyone quickly disappeared … …
In a small cave at the bottom of the mountain, Cang Duo drilled out of it, shaking like a sieve, shaking off the Ghost Medicine Powder on his body.
The other Konoha clansmen also drilled out of their "small homes", including Patriarch Gudo.
Cang Duo and Gudo looked at each other, and their eyes were full of excitement.
